      const id=rd(pos,true),sz=rd(pos+id.n,false);const ds=pos+id.n+sz.n;
      if(id.v===0x1F43B675){
        let end;
        if(!sz.unk)end=ds+sz.v;
        else{let p=ds;while(p<segEnd){const c=rd(p,true);if(L1.indexOf(c.v)>=0)break;const cs=rd(p+c.n,false);if(cs.unk)throw new Error('unk child');p+=c.n+cs.n+cs.v;}end=p;}
        if(end>segEnd)end=segEnd;
        clusters.push({hs:pos,ds:ds,end:end,unk:sz.unk,hdrLen:id.n+sz.n,szLen:sz.n});
        pos=end;continue;
      }
      if(sz.unk)throw new Error('unk l1');
      const end=ds+sz.v;if(end>segEnd)return null;
      if(id.v===0x1C53BB6B)return null; // already has Cues
      if(id.v===0x1549A966)info={ds:ds,end:end};
      else if(id.v===0x1654AE6B)tracks={ds:ds,end:end,hs:pos};
      else if(id.v!==0x114D9B74&&id.v!==0xEC)others.push({hs:pos,end:end});
      pos=end;
    }
    if(!info||!tracks||!clusters.length)return null;
    // Info
    let scale=1000000;const infoKids=[];
    {let p=info.ds;while(p<info.end){const c=rd(p,true),cs=rd(p+c.n,false);const cds=p+c.n+cs.n,cend=cds+cs.v;
      if(c.v===0x2AD7B1)scale=uint(cds,cend)||1000000;
      if(c.v!==0x4489&&c.v!==0xEC)infoKids.push(d.subarray(p,cend));
      p=cend;}}
    // Tracks -> video track number
    let vtrack=0;
    {let p=tracks.ds;while(p<tracks.end){const c=rd(p,true),cs=rd(p+c.n,false);const cds=p+c.n+cs.n,cend=cds+cs.v;
      if(c.v===0xAE){let num=0,type=0,q=cds;while(q<cend){const k=rd(q,true),ks=rd(q+k.n,false);const kds=q+k.n+ks.n,kend=kds+ks.v;if(k.v===0xD7)num=uint(kds,kend);else if(k.v===0x83)type=uint(kds,kend);q=kend;}
        if(type===1&&!vtrack)vtrack=num;}
      p=cend;}}
    if(!vtrack)return null;
    // Clusters: timecode + blocks
    const cues=[];let maxTs=0,vFirst=-1,vLast=-1,vCount=0;
    clusters.forEach((cl,ci)=>{
      let p=cl.ds,tc=0;
      while(p<cl.end){const c=rd(p,true),cs=rd(p+c.n,false);const cds=p+c.n+cs.n,cend=cds+cs.v;
        if(c.v===0xE7)tc=uint(cds,cend);
        else if(c.v===0xA3||c.v===0xA0){
          let bs=cds,isGroup=c.v===0xA0;
          if(isGroup){let q=cds;bs=-1;while(q<cend){const k=rd(q,true),ks=rd(q+k.n,false);const kds=q+k.n+ks.n;if(k.v===0xA1){bs=kds;break;}q=kds+ks.v;}}
          if(bs>=0){const tr=rd(bs,false);let rel=d[bs+tr.n]*256+d[bs+tr.n+1];if(rel>=32768)rel-=65536;const flags=d[bs+tr.n+2];const ts=tc+rel;
            if(ts>maxTs)maxTs=ts;
            if(tr.v===vtrack){vCount++;if(vFirst<0||ts<vFirst)vFirst=ts;if(ts>vLast)vLast=ts;
              if(!isGroup&&(flags&0x80))cues.push({t:ts,ci:ci});}}
        }
        p=cend;}
    });
    if(!cues.length)clusters.forEach((cl,ci)=>{let p=cl.ds;while(p<cl.end){const c=rd(p,true),cs=rd(p+c.n,false);if(c.v===0xE7){cues.push({t:uint(p+c.n+cs.n,p+c.n+cs.n+cs.v),ci:ci});break;}p+=c.n+cs.n+cs.v;}});
    cues.sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t);
    const frameDur=vCount>1?Math.round((vLast-vFirst)/(vCount-1)):Math.round(33*1000000/scale);
    const durUnits=Math.max(maxTs+Math.round(20*1000000/scale),vLast+frameDur);
    // Duration element (float64)
    const dur=new Uint8Array(8);new DataView(dur.buffer).setFloat64(0,durUnits,false);
    infoKids.push(el(0x4489,dur));
    const infoEl=el(0x1549A966,concat(infoKids));
    const tracksEl=d.subarray(tracks.hs,tracks.end);
    const otherEls=others.map(o=>d.subarray(o.hs,o.end));
    // Cues (fixed-width fields => size known before offsets)
    const cueLen=19,cuesContent=cues.length*cueLen;
    const cuesHdr=4+vsize(cuesContent);
    let pre=infoEl.length+tracksEl.length+cuesHdr+cuesContent;otherEls.forEach(o=>pre+=o.length);
    // Cluster layout
    const offs=[];let cur=pre;
    clusters.forEach(cl=>{offs.push(cur);cur+=cl.hdrLen+(cl.end-cl.ds);});
    const segSize=cur;
    const cueBytes=new Uint8Array(cuesContent);let q=0;
    cues.forEach(c=>{
      cueBytes[q++]=0xBB;cueBytes[q++]=0x80|17;
      cueBytes[q++]=0xB3;cueBytes[q++]=0x84;cueBytes[q++]=(c.t>>>24)&255;cueBytes[q++]=(c.t>>>16)&255;cueBytes[q++]=(c.t>>>8)&255;cueBytes[q++]=c.t&255;
      cueBytes[q++]=0xB7;cueBytes[q++]=0x80|9;
      cueBytes[q++]=0xF7;cueBytes[q++]=0x81;cueBytes[q++]=vtrack&255;
      const o=offs[c.ci];
      cueBytes[q++]=0xF1;cueBytes[q++]=0x84;cueBytes[q++]=(o>>>24)&255;cueBytes[q++]=(o>>>16)&255;cueBytes[q++]=(o>>>8)&255;cueBytes[q++]=o&255;
    });
    const cuesEl=concat([idBytes(0x1C53BB6B),wsize(cuesContent,vsize(cuesContent)),cueBytes]);
    // Output parts: small typed arrays + original byte ranges (copied by the Blob constructor, not by us)
    const parts=[d.subarray(0,ebmlEnd),concat([idBytes(0x18538067),wsize(segSize,8)]),infoEl,tracksEl].concat(otherEls).concat([cuesEl]);
    const ranges=[];
    clusters.forEach(cl=>{
      const hdr=concat([d.subarray(cl.hs,cl.hs+(cl.hdrLen-cl.szLen)),wsize(cl.end-cl.ds,8)]);
      // header keeps its 12-byte layout only when the original size field was 8 bytes; otherwise grow it and rebase offsets
      ranges.push({hdr:hdr,from:cl.ds,to:cl.end});
    });
    return {parts:parts,ranges:ranges,segSize:segSize,expectedHdr:clusters.map(cl=>cl.hdrLen)};
  }
  async function evltMakeRecordedMediaSeekable(blob){
    try{
      if(!blob||!blob.size||blob.size>1.5e9)return blob;
      const d=new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());
      const r=evltRemuxSeekable(d);
      if(!r)return blob;
      // Cluster headers were rewritten with an 8-byte size field; verify the layout used for the Cues is what we write.
      let okLayout=true;r.ranges.forEach((rg,i)=>{if(rg.hdr.length!==r.expectedHdr[i])okLayout=false;});
      if(!okLayout)return blob;
      const out=[];r.parts.forEach(p=>out.push(p));
      r.ranges.forEach(rg=>{out.push(rg.hdr);out.push(blob.slice(rg.from,rg.to));});
      const fixed=new Blob(out,{type:blob.type});
      if(!fixed.size||fixed.size<blob.size*0.9||fixed.size>blob.size+4096+r.ranges.length*16)return blob;
      return fixed;
    }catch(_e){return blob;}
  }
  window.__evltMakeRecordedMediaSeekable=evltMakeRecordedMediaSeekable;
  window.evEffectiveVideoSrc=ov=>String((ov&&ov.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc)||(ov&&ov.src)||'');
  window.evPrimeEnhancedVideo=function(el,url){
    const source=String(url||'');
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||!source||el.src!==source||el.dataset.evEnhPrimed===source)return;
    const clipId=String(el.dataset.evV72OverlayId||'');
    const model=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===clipId)||null;
    if(!model||model.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc!==source||el.__evEnhPrimeInProgress===source)return;
    let playing=false;try{playing=typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&!!isPlaying;}catch(_e){}
    if(playing||window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing){
      if(el.__evEnhPrimeRetryUrl===source||el.__evEnhPrimeRetryUsed===source)return;
      const retryOnScrub=!!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing;
      const retry=()=>{
        if(retryOnScrub)window.removeEventListener('ethvelleTimelineScrubReleased',retry);else el.removeEventListener('pause',retry);
        if(el.__evEnhPrimeRetry===retry){delete el.__evEnhPrimeRetry;delete el.__evEnhPrimeRetryUrl;}
        el.__evEnhPrimeRetryUsed=source;
        window.evPrimeEnhancedVideo(el,source);
      };
      el.__evEnhPrimeRetryUrl=source;el.__evEnhPrimeRetry=retry;
      if(retryOnScrub)window.addEventListener('ethvelleTimelineScrubReleased',retry,{once:true});else el.addEventListener('pause',retry,{once:true});
      return;
    }
    if(el.readyState<1){
      if(el.__evEnhPrimeMetadataWaitUrl!==source){
        el.__evEnhPrimeMetadataWaitUrl=source;
        el.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',function(){if(el.__evEnhPrimeMetadataWaitUrl===source)delete el.__evEnhPrimeMetadataWaitUrl;window.evPrimeEnhancedVideo(el,source);},{once:true});
      }
      return;
    }
    el.__evEnhPrimeInProgress=source;
    (async function(){
      const t0=Number.isFinite(Number(el.currentTime))?Number(el.currentTime):0;
      const priorVisibility=el.style.visibility,priorMuted=el.muted;
      const stillCurrent=()=>el.src===source&&model.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc===source;
      try{
        el.muted=true;el.style.visibility='hidden';
        if(!stillCurrent())return;
        await new Promise(resolve=>{
          let settled=false,guard=0;
          const finish=()=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;el.removeEventListener('durationchange',onDuration);el.removeEventListener('seeked',finish);clearTimeout(guard);resolve();};
          const onDuration=()=>{if(Number.isFinite(el.duration)&&el.duration>0)finish();};
          el.addEventListener('durationchange',onDuration);
          el.addEventListener('seeked',finish,{once:true});
          guard=setTimeout(finish,2500);
          try{el.currentTime=1e7;}catch(_e){finish();}
        });
        if(!stillCurrent())return;
        await new Promise(resolve=>{
          let settled=false,guard=0;
          const finish=()=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;el.removeEventListener('seeked',finish);clearTimeout(guard);resolve();};
          el.addEventListener('seeked',finish,{once:true});
          guard=setTimeout(finish,1500);
          try{el.currentTime=t0;}catch(_e){finish();}
        });
      }catch(_e){}
      finally{
        try{el.muted=priorMuted;}catch(_e){}
        try{el.style.visibility=priorVisibility;}catch(_e){}
        const primed=stillCurrent();
        if(primed)el.dataset.evEnhPrimed=source;
        if(el.__evEnhPrimeInProgress===source)delete el.__evEnhPrimeInProgress;
        if(primed){try{window.requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(stillCurrent()&&typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();});}catch(_e){}}
      }
    })();
  };

  }

  function setRuntimeOutput(target,blob,quality,assetInfo){

    if(target.kind==='overlay'){
      try{const el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls[model.id])||null;if(el){if(model.type==='video'){el.autoplay=false;el.removeAttribute('autoplay');try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}}if(el.src!==url){el.src=url;if(model.type==='video'){el.dataset.evWarmSrc=window.evEffectiveVideoSrc(model);el.load();window.evPrimeEnhancedVideo(el,url);try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}}}else if(model.type==='video'){el.dataset.evWarmSrc=window.evEffectiveVideoSrc(model);window.evPrimeEnhancedVideo(el,url);}el.style.filter='none';el.style.opacity='1';}}catch(e){}

  }
  async function restoreEnhancedAssets(){
    const tasks=[];
    const all=[...(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]),...(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[])];
    all.forEach(model=>{if(!model||!model.enhancedAssetKey||!model.enhancedBaseStamp||mediaStamp(model.deviceMediaKey?'ethvelle-device-media:'+model.deviceMediaKey:model.src)!==model.enhancedBaseStamp)return;tasks.push((async()=>{try{const rec=await loadEnhancedMedia(model.enhancedAssetKey);if(!rec||!rec.blob||mediaStamp(model.deviceMediaKey?'ethvelle-device-media:'+model.deviceMediaKey:model.src)!==rec.stamp)return;defineRuntimeUrl(model,URL.createObjectURL(rec.blob),rec.quality||model.enhancedAssetQuality||1080);}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle: saved enhanced media could not be restored',e);}})());});
    await Promise.all(tasks);if(tasks.length){try{if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();if(typeof refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead==='function')refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead();if(typeof applyBackground==='function')applyBackground();if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(e){}}
  }
  const oldOpenEditor=window.openEditor;
  if(typeof oldOpenEditor==='function'&&!oldOpenEditor.__evltV5Restore){const wrapped=function(){const r=oldOpenEditor.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(()=>restoreEnhancedAssets(),60);return r;};wrapped.__evltV5Restore=true;window.openEditor=wrapped;}
  function downloadBlob(blob,name){const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>{try{URL.revokeObjectURL(url);}catch(e){}},60000);}
  function outputSize(iw,ih,quality){const ratio=iw/ih;let w,h;if(ratio>=1){h=quality;w=Math.round(quality*ratio);}else{w=quality;h=Math.round(quality/ratio);}return {w:Math.max(2,w-(w%2)),h:Math.max(2,h-(h%2))};}
  // Per-frame enhancement renderer. WebGL performs bicubic sampling/upscale and the
  // denoise/contrast/sharpen/vibrance passes on the GPU; Canvas2D is the compatibility path.
  function createEnhancePipeline(sourceWidth,sourceHeight,outWidth,outHeight){
    const sw=Math.max(2,Math.round(sourceWidth||2)),sh=Math.max(2,Math.round(sourceHeight||2));
    const ow=Math.max(2,Math.round(outWidth||sw)),oh=Math.max(2,Math.round(outHeight||sh));
    const stage=document.createElement('canvas');stage.width=sw;stage.height=sh;
    const stageCtx=stage.getContext('2d',{alpha:false,desynchronized:true});
    if(!stageCtx)throw new Error('Frame staging is unavailable in this browser.');
    stageCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;stageCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
    const vertexSource='attribute vec2 a_position; varying vec2 v_uv; void main(){v_uv=(a_position+1.0)*0.5;gl_Position=vec4(a_position,0.0,1.0);}';
    const fragmentSource=`
      precision highp float;
      uniform sampler2D u_image;
      uniform vec2 u_sourceSize;
      uniform vec2 u_outputSize;
      uniform float u_amount;
      varying vec2 v_uv;
      float luma(vec3 c){return dot(c,vec3(0.2126,0.7152,0.0722));}
      float cubic(float v){v=abs(v);if(v<=1.0)return 1.5*v*v*v-2.5*v*v+1.0;if(v<2.0)return -0.5*v*v*v+2.5*v*v-4.0*v+2.0;return 0.0;}
      vec4 bicubicSample(vec2 uv){
        vec2 pixel=uv*u_sourceSize-0.5;vec2 base=floor(pixel);vec2 f=fract(pixel);
        vec4 sum=vec4(0.0);float total=0.0;
        for(int y=-1;y<=2;y++){for(int x=-1;x<=2;x++){
          float weight=cubic(float(x)-f.x)*cubic(float(y)-f.y);
          vec2 at=(base+vec2(float(x),float(y))+0.5)/u_sourceSize;
          sum+=texture2D(u_image,clamp(at,vec2(0.0),vec2(1.0)))*weight;total+=weight;
        }}
        return sum/max(total,0.0001);
      }
      void main(){
        vec4 raw=bicubicSample(v_uv);vec3 original=raw.rgb;
        float a=clamp(u_amount,0.0,1.0);
        if(a<0.0001){gl_FragColor=vec4(original,raw.a);return;}
        vec2 t=1.0/u_sourceSize;
        vec3 n1=texture2D(u_image,clamp(v_uv+vec2(t.x,0.0),vec2(0.0),vec2(1.0))).rgb;
        vec3 n2=texture2D(u_image,clamp(v_uv-vec2(t.x,0.0),vec2(0.0),vec2(1.0))).rgb;
        vec3 n3=texture2D(u_image,clamp(v_uv+vec2(0.0,t.y),vec2(0.0),vec2(1.0))).rgb;
        vec3 n4=texture2D(u_image,clamp(v_uv-vec2(0.0,t.y),vec2(0.0),vec2(1.0))).rgb;
        float y=luma(original);
        float w1=exp(-abs(y-luma(n1))*16.0)*0.25;
        float w2=exp(-abs(y-luma(n2))*16.0)*0.25;
        float w3=exp(-abs(y-luma(n3))*16.0)*0.25;
        float w4=exp(-abs(y-luma(n4))*16.0)*0.25;
        vec3 local=(n1*w1+n2*w2+n3*w3+n4*w4)/max(w1+w2+w3+w4,0.0001);
        vec3 denoised=mix(original,local,a*0.18);
        float localY=luma((n1+n2+n3+n4)*0.25);
        float adaptive=clamp(abs(y-localY)*3.0,0.0,1.0);
        vec3 contrasted=(denoised-0.5)*(1.0+a*(0.20+0.15*adaptive))+0.5;
        float shadow=pow(max(0.0,1.0-y),2.0);
        contrasted+=vec3(shadow*a*0.105);
        vec3 sharpened=contrasted+(denoised-local)*a*0.58;
        float gray=luma(sharpened);
        float saturation=max(max(sharpened.r,sharpened.g),sharpened.b)-min(min(sharpened.r,sharpened.g),sharpened.b);
        sharpened+=(sharpened-vec3(gray))*a*0.27*(1.0-clamp(saturation,0.0,1.0));
        // A second, lighter post-upscale sharpen keeps fine edges legible after interpolation.
        sharpened+=(original-local)*a*0.12;
        gl_FragColor=vec4(clamp(sharpened,0.0,1.0),raw.a);
      }`;
    function compile(gl,type,source){const shd=gl.createShader(type);gl.shaderSource(shd,source);gl.compileShader(shd);if(!gl.getShaderParameter(shd,gl.COMPILE_STATUS)){const msg=gl.getShaderInfoLog(shd)||'Shader compilation failed';gl.deleteShader(shd);throw new Error(msg);}return shd;}
    function createGLCanvas(){
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=ow;canvas.height=oh;
      let gl=null;try{gl=canvas.getContext('webgl',{alpha:false,antialias:false,preserveDrawingBuffer:true,premultipliedAlpha:false})||canvas.getContext('experimental-webgl',{alpha:false,antialias:false,preserveDrawingBuffer:true,premultipliedAlpha:false});}catch(_e){}
      if(!gl)return null;
      let program=null,texture=null,buffer=null;
      try{
        const vs=compile(gl,gl.VERTEX_SHADER,vertexSource),fs=compile(gl,gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER,fragmentSource);
        program=gl.createProgram();gl.attachShader(program,vs);gl.attachShader(program,fs);gl.linkProgram(program);
        if(!gl.getProgramParameter(program,gl.LINK_STATUS))throw new Error(gl.getProgramInfoLog(program)||'WebGL program link failed');
        gl.deleteShader(vs);gl.deleteShader(fs);
        buffer=gl.createBuffer();gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,buffer);gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,new Float32Array([-1,-1,1,-1,-1,1,1,1]),gl.STATIC_DRAW);
        texture=gl.createTexture();gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,texture);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,gl.LINEAR);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,gl.LINEAR);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        gl.useProgram(program);const pos=gl.getAttribLocation(program,'a_position');gl.enableVertexAttribArray(pos);gl.vertexAttribPointer(pos,2,gl.FLOAT,false,0,0);
        gl.uniform1i(gl.getUniformLocation(program,'u_image'),0);gl.uniform2f(gl.getUniformLocation(program,'u_sourceSize'),sw,sh);gl.uniform2f(gl.getUniformLocation(program,'u_outputSize'),ow,oh);
        gl.viewport(0,0,ow,oh);gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL,true);gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_PREMULTIPLY_ALPHA_WEBGL,false);
        return {canvas,gl,program,texture,buffer,draw(){gl.viewport(0,0,ow,oh);gl.useProgram(program);gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,texture);gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,gl.RGBA,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,stage);gl.uniform1f(gl.getUniformLocation(program,'u_amount'),amountValue);gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLE_STRIP,0,4);},dispose(){try{gl.deleteTexture(texture);gl.deleteBuffer(buffer);gl.deleteProgram(program);}catch(_e){}}};
      }catch(_e){console.warn('Ethvelle WebGL enhancement fallback:',_e);try{if(texture)gl.deleteTexture(texture);if(buffer)gl.deleteBuffer(buffer);if(program)gl.deleteProgram(program);}catch(__e){}return null;}
    }
    let amountValue=0,glRenderer=createGLCanvas(),canvas=glRenderer?glRenderer.canvas:document.createElement('canvas'),ctx=null;
    if(!glRenderer){canvas.width=ow;canvas.height=oh;ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false,willReadFrequently:true});if(!ctx)throw new Error('Neither WebGL nor Canvas2D frame processing is available.');ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';}
    function renderFallback(amount){
      ctx.clearRect(0,0,ow,oh);ctx.drawImage(stage,0,0,ow,oh);
      if(amount<=0)return;
      const image=ctx.getImageData(0,0,ow,oh),src=image.data,out=ctx.createImageData(ow,oh),dst=out.data;
      const lum=(r,g,b)=>r*.2126+g*.7152+b*.0722,scale=amount/100;
      for(let y=0;y<oh;y++){for(let x=0;x<ow;x++){
        const i=(y*ow+x)*4,r=src[i],g=src[i+1],b=src[i+2],yy=lum(r,g,b);
        let sr=r,sg=g,sb=b,weights=1;
        const offsets=[x>0?i-4:-1,x<ow-1?i+4:-1,y>0?i-ow*4:-1,y<oh-1?i+ow*4:-1];
        for(let k=0;k<4;k++){const n=offsets[k];if(n<0)continue;const ny=lum(src[n],src[n+1],src[n+2]),w=Math.exp(-Math.abs(yy-ny)/255*16)*.25;sr+=src[n]*w;sg+=src[n+1]*w;sb+=src[n+2]*w;weights+=w;}
        const lr=sr/weights,lg=sg/weights,lb=sb/weights,localY=lum((r+lr)/2,(g+lg)/2,(b+lb)/2);
        const denR=r+(lr-r)*scale*.18,denG=g+(lg-g)*scale*.18,denB=b+(lb-b)*scale*.18;
        const adapt=Math.min(1,Math.abs(yy-localY)/255*3),contrast=1+scale*(.20+.15*adapt),shadow=Math.pow(Math.max(0,1-yy/255),2)*scale*26.8;
        let nr=(denR-127.5)*contrast+127.5+shadow+(denR-lr)*scale*.58;
        let ng=(denG-127.5)*contrast+127.5+shadow+(denG-lg)*scale*.58;
        let nb=(denB-127.5)*contrast+127.5+shadow+(denB-lb)*scale*.58;
        const gray=lum(nr,ng,nb),sat=(Math.max(nr,ng,nb)-Math.min(nr,ng,nb))/255,vib=scale*.27*(1-Math.max(0,Math.min(1,sat)));
        nr+=(nr-gray)*vib+(r-lr)*scale*.12;ng+=(ng-gray)*vib+(g-lg)*scale*.12;nb+=(nb-gray)*vib+(b-lb)*scale*.12;
        dst[i]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,nr));dst[i+1]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,ng));dst[i+2]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,nb));dst[i+3]=src[i+3];
      }}
      ctx.putImageData(out,0,0);
      // Separate, restrained post-sharpen after the upscale/denoise pass.
      const post=ctx.getImageData(0,0,ow,oh),pd=post.data,sharpened=ctx.createImageData(ow,oh),sd=sharpened.data;
      for(let y=0;y<oh;y++){for(let x=0;x<ow;x++){
        const i=(y*ow+x)*4;for(let c=0;c<3;c++){
          let sum=0,n=0;if(x>0){sum+=pd[i-4+c];n++;}if(x<ow-1){sum+=pd[i+4+c];n++;}if(y>0){sum+=pd[i-ow*4+c];n++;}if(y<oh-1){sum+=pd[i+ow*4+c];n++;}
          sd[i+c]=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,pd[i+c]+(pd[i+c]-(n?sum/n:pd[i+c]))*scale*.12));
        }sd[i+3]=pd[i+3];
      }}ctx.putImageData(sharpened,0,0);
    }
    return {canvas,renderer:glRenderer?'WebGL':'Canvas2D',render(source,filter,amount){
      amountValue=clamp(amount,0,100)/100;stageCtx.save();stageCtx.filter=filter&&filter!=='none'?filter:'none';stageCtx.clearRect(0,0,sw,sh);stageCtx.drawImage(source,0,0,sw,sh);stageCtx.restore();
      if(glRenderer)glRenderer.draw();else renderFallback(amountValue*100);return canvas;
    },dispose(){if(glRenderer)glRenderer.dispose();stage.width=stage.height=1;canvas.width=canvas.height=1;}};
  }
  window.ethvelleCreateEnhancePipeline=createEnhancePipeline;
  window.ethvelleGetEnhanceOutputSize=outputSize;
  function status(text,pct){const el=$('evltEnhanceStatus');if(el)el.textContent=text||'';const bar=$('evltEnhanceProgress'),fill=$('evltEnhanceProgressFill');if(bar)bar.style.display=pct==null?'none':'block';if(fill&&pct!=null)fill.style.width=clamp(pct,0,100)+'%';}
  function setCompare(percent){
    const p=clamp(percent,0,100),after=$('evltAfterLayer'),wipe=$('evltCompareWipe'),range=$('evltCompare');
    // Base image/video is BEFORE on the left; enhanced media is AFTER on the right.
    if(after)after.style.clipPath=`inset(0 0 0 ${100-p}%)`;
    if(wipe)wipe.style.left=p+'%';if(range)range.value=String(p);
  }
  window.ethvelleSetEnhanceCompare=setCompare;
  function showRuntimeInCompare(){
    const t=liveTarget();if(!t||!t.model.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc)return;
    const afterLayer=$('evltAfterLayer');if(!afterLayer)return;
    const media=afterLayer.querySelector('video,img');if(!media)return;
    const url=t.model.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc;
    if(media.src!==url){media.src=url;if(media.tagName==='VIDEO'){media.muted=true;media.playsInline=true;media.loop=false;media.preload='metadata';try{media.pause();}catch(_e){}}}
    if(media.tagName==='VIDEO'){try{media.pause();}catch(_e){}}
    afterLayer.style.filter='none';setCompare($('evltCompare')?.value||50);
  }
  function syncComparePanel(){setCompare($('evltCompare')?.value||50);showRuntimeInCompare();}
  const oldOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpenPanel==='function'&&!oldOpenPanel.__evltV5Compare){
    const wrapped=function(key){const r=oldOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='evltEnhance')setTimeout(syncComparePanel,40);return r;};wrapped.__evltV5Compare=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  // Apply a processed runtime source after the editor's own renderer has updated its media elements.
  const oldRenderMedia=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldRenderMedia==='function'&&!oldRenderMedia.__evltV5Runtime){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRenderMedia.apply(this,arguments);try{(overlays||[]).forEach(ov=>{if(!ov||!ov.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc)return;const el=overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[ov.id];if(!el)return;const u=ov.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc;if(ov.type==='video'){el.autoplay=false;el.removeAttribute('autoplay');}if(el.src!==u){el.src=u;if(ov.type==='video'){el.dataset.evWarmSrc=window.evEffectiveVideoSrc(ov);el.load();window.evPrimeEnhancedVideo(el,u);try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}}}else if(ov.type==='video'){el.dataset.evWarmSrc=window.evEffectiveVideoSrc(ov);window.evPrimeEnhancedVideo(el,u);}el.style.filter='none';});}catch(e){}return r;};wrapped.__evltV5Runtime=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }
  const oldApplyBg=window.applyBackground;
  if(typeof oldApplyBg==='function'&&!oldApplyBg.__evltV5Runtime){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldApplyBg.apply(this,arguments);try{const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0)):null;const url=bg&&bg.__evltRuntimeEnhancedSrc,img=$('bgImgLayer');if(url&&img){if(img.src!==url)img.src=url;img.style.display='block';img.style.filter='none';}}catch(e){}return r;};wrapped.__evltV5Runtime=true;window.applyBackground=wrapped;
  }
  function setPendingOutput(target,blob,quality){
    if(pendingPreviewURL){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(pendingPreviewURL);}catch(e){}}
    pendingPreviewURL=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    pendingEnhancement={target,blob,quality:Number(quality)||1080};
    try{Object.defineProperty(target.model,'__evltPendingEnhancedSrc',{value:pendingPreviewURL,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});}catch(e){target.model.__evltPendingEnhancedSrc=pendingPreviewURL;}
    try{Object.defineProperty(target.model,'__evltPendingEnhancedQuality',{value:Number(quality)||1080,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});}catch(e){target.model.__evltPendingEnhancedQuality=Number(quality)||1080;}
    try{Object.defineProperty(target.model,'__evltEnhanceChosenQuality',{value:Number(quality)||1080,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});}catch(e){target.model.__evltEnhanceChosenQuality=Number(quality)||1080;}
    try{if(typeof window.__ethvelleV46RefreshQualityBadges==='function')window.__ethvelleV46RefreshQualityBadges();}catch(_e){}
    const apply=$('evltEnhanceApply');if(apply)apply.disabled=false;
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance=async function(){
    const pending=pendingEnhancement;if(!pending){status('Tap Enhance first, then Apply.',null);return;}
    const current=resolvePendingTarget(pending);if(!current){status('The enhanced clip is no longer available in this project.',null);return;}
    const apply=$('evltEnhanceApply');if(apply){apply.disabled=true;apply.textContent='Applying…';}
    try{
      const stored=await storeEnhancedMedia(current,pending.blob,pending.quality);
      if(current.kind==='overlay'&&current.type==='video'){
        const m=current.model;
        if(m.enhancedOriginalSourceOffset==null)m.enhancedOriginalSourceOffset=Number(m.sourceOffset)||0;
        if(m.enhancedOriginalSpeed==null)m.enhancedOriginalSpeed=Number(m.speed)||1;
        m.sourceOffset=0;m.speed=1;
      }
      setRuntimeOutput(current,pending.blob,pending.quality,stored);
      try{delete current.model.__evltPendingEnhancedSrc;delete current.model.__evltPendingEnhancedQuality;}catch(e){}
      if(pendingPreviewURL){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(pendingPreviewURL);}catch(e){}pendingPreviewURL=null;}
      pendingEnhancement=null;
      status(`${qualityLabel(pending.quality)} · Applied`,100);
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(stored?'Enhancement applied to the clip':'Enhancement applied for this session; device storage was unavailable.',{duration:stored?1800:2600});
      setTimeout(syncComparePanel,50);
      try{if(typeof window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow==='function')window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow();}catch(e){}
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle Apply:',err);status(err&&err.message?err.message:'Could not apply the enhancement.',null);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(err&&err.message?err.message:'Could not apply enhancement',{duration:2400});}
    finally{if(apply){apply.disabled=!pendingEnhancement;apply.textContent='Apply';}}
  };
  /* Videos restored after a refresh (and enhanced clips, which are MediaRecorder .webm blobs) often report
     duration = Infinity/0 or have no decoded frame size yet, which used to fail with "Video has no readable
     frames". Wait for the first frame, force the browser to compute the real duration, and as a last
     resort fall back to the clip's own trim length. */
  async function evPrepareReadableVideo(v,model){
    const waitEvt=(name,ms)=>new Promise(res=>{let d=false;const fin=()=>{if(d)return;d=true;v.removeEventListener(name,fin);clearTimeout(g);res();};const g=setTimeout(fin,ms);v.addEventListener(name,fin,{once:true});});
    if(!v.videoWidth||!v.videoHeight||v.readyState<2){if(v.readyState<2)await waitEvt('loadeddata',5000);}
    if(!Number.isFinite(v.duration)||v.duration<=0){
      await new Promise(res=>{let d=false;const fin=()=>{if(d)return;d=true;v.removeEventListener('durationchange',chk);v.removeEventListener('seeked',fin);clearTimeout(g);res();};const chk=()=>{if(Number.isFinite(v.duration)&&v.duration>0)fin();};const g=setTimeout(fin,3500);v.addEventListener('durationchange',chk);v.addEventListener('seeked',fin);try{v.currentTime=1e7;}catch(_e){fin();}});
      const seekedBack=waitEvt('seeked',1500);try{v.currentTime=0;}catch(_e){}await seekedBack;
    }
    if(!Number.isFinite(v.duration)||v.duration<=0){
      const span=Math.max(0,Number(model&&model.trimEnd)-Number(model&&model.trimStart))*Math.max(.05,Number(model&&model.speed)||1),fallback=(Number(model&&model.sourceOffset)||0)+span;
      if(fallback>0){try{Object.defineProperty(v,'duration',{configurable:true,get:()=>fallback});}catch(_e){}}
    }
    if(!v.videoWidth||!v.videoHeight){await waitEvt('loadeddata',2500);}
  }
  /* v286 ENCODER FIX: Chrome (especially on Android) says H.264 is "supported" but its hardware encoder rejects
     2K/4K frames with "The given encoder configuration is not supported by the encoder." Before recording we now
     really test each size/codec on a throw-away canvas stream and use the first combination that actually starts
     encoding (H.264 hardware -> VP8 -> VP9 -> WebM default), stepping the size down 4K -> 2K -> 1080p -> 720p only
     if nothing at the requested size can encode. */
  const EV_ENHANCE_LADDER=[2160,1440,1080,720];
  const evEncProbeCache=new Map();
  async function evProbeEnhanceEncoder(w,h,mime,bitrate){
    let c=null,s=null,t=null,r=null,timer=0,killer=0,failed=false,gotData=false;
    try{
      if(typeof MediaRecorder==='undefined')return false;
      c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;const x=c.getContext('2d');if(!x)return false;
      x.fillStyle='#202020';x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      s=c.captureStream(0);t=s.getVideoTracks&&s.getVideoTracks()[0];
      r=mime?new MediaRecorder(s,{mimeType:mime,videoBitsPerSecond:bitrate}):new MediaRecorder(s,{videoBitsPerSecond:bitrate});
      await new Promise(resolve=>{
        let done=false;const fin=()=>{if(done)return;done=true;clearInterval(timer);clearTimeout(killer);resolve();};
        r.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size>0){gotData=true;fin();}};
        r.onerror=()=>{failed=true;fin();};
        let n=0;
        const tick=()=>{try{x.fillStyle='hsl('+((n*37)%360)+',60%,40%)';x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);x.fillStyle='#fff';x.fillRect((n*17)%Math.max(1,w-64),(n*29)%Math.max(1,h-64),64,64);if(t&&t.requestFrame)t.requestFrame();}catch(_e){}n++;};
        try{r.start(100);}catch(_e){failed=true;fin();return;}
        tick();timer=setInterval(tick,80);killer=setTimeout(fin,1100);
      });
      if(failed)return false;
      if(r.state==='inactive'&&!gotData)return false;
      return true;
    }catch(_e){return false;}
    finally{
      clearInterval(timer);clearTimeout(killer);
      try{if(r&&r.state!=='inactive')r.stop();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(s)s.getTracks().forEach(tt=>tt.stop());}catch(_e){}
      if(c){try{c.width=c.height=1;}catch(_e){}}
    }
  }
  async function evChooseEnhanceEncoder(iw,ih,requested,maxQuality,notify){
    const cap=Math.min(Number(requested)||1080,Number(maxQuality)||99999);
    let ladder=EV_ENHANCE_LADDER.filter(q=>q<=cap);if(!ladder.length)ladder=[cap];
    const families=[
      ['video/webm;codecs=h264,opus','video/webm;codecs=avc1,opus'],
      ['video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8'],
      ['video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp9'],
      ['video/webm']
    ];
    const mimes=[];
    families.forEach(f=>{const m=f.find(v=>{try{return !MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported||MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(v);}catch(_e){return false;}});if(m)mimes.push(m);});
    if(!mimes.length)mimes.push('');
    for(const q of ladder){
      const size=outputSize(iw,ih,q);
      for(const mime of mimes){
        const isH264=/h264|avc1/i.test(mime);
        const bitrate=isH264?(q>=2160?14000000:q>=1440?9000000:6000000):(q>=2160?18000000:q>=1440?12000000:8000000);
        const key=size.w+'x'+size.h+'|'+mime;
        if(typeof notify==='function')notify(q,mime);
        let ok=evEncProbeCache.get(key);
        if(ok===undefined){ok=await evProbeEnhanceEncoder(size.w,size.h,mime,bitrate);evEncProbeCache.set(key,ok);}
        if(ok)return {quality:q,mime,bitrate,size};
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  function evNextLowerEnhanceQuality(q){const lower=EV_ENHANCE_LADDER.filter(v=>v<q);return lower.length?lower[0]:0;}
  function evQLabel(q){return q>=2160?'4K':q+'p';}
  window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance=async function evltStartEnhanceCore(opts){
    opts=(opts&&typeof opts==='object'&&typeof opts.maxQuality==='number')?opts:{};
    const target=liveTarget();if(!target){status('');return;}if(window.__ethvelleV5EnhanceBusy)return;window.__ethvelleV5EnhanceBusy=true;
    const btn=$('evltEnhanceStart'),amount=clamp($('evltEnhanceAmount')?.value??target.model.enhanceAmount??0,0,100);
    let quality=Number($('evltEnhanceQuality')?.value)||1080,label=qualityLabel(quality),chosenEnc=null,retryMax=0;
    const requestedQuality=quality;
    try{if(typeof window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime==='function')window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();else if(typeof window.pausePlayback==='function')window.pausePlayback();}catch(_e){}
    const mediaAudioSnapshot=[];
    try{document.querySelectorAll('audio,video').forEach(media=>{mediaAudioSnapshot.push({media,muted:!!media.muted,volume:Number(media.volume)});try{media.pause();media.muted=true;media.volume=0;}catch(_e){}});}catch(_e){}
    if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Enhancing…';}status('',0);
    let sourceVideo=null,outStream=null,sourceStream=null,recorder=null,raf=0,rvfc=0,watchdog=0,stopResolve=null,stopped=false,pipeline=null,audioContext=null,audioSource=null,audioDestination=null,silentGain=null,canvas=null,ctx=null,canvasTrack=null;
    let stopMinTimer=0,stopFlushTimer=0,stopScheduled=false,recordingStartedAt=0,endPoll=0;
    try{
      if(target.type==='image'){
        const img=new Image();img.decoding='async';if(/^https?:/i.test(target.src))img.crossOrigin='anonymous';
        await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{img.onload=resolve;img.onerror=()=>reject(new Error('Image cannot be processed in this browser.'));img.src=target.src;if(img.complete&&img.naturalWidth)resolve();});
        const size=outputSize(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height,quality);
        pipeline=createEnhancePipeline(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height,size.w,size.h);
        pipeline.render(img,filterFor(target.model,amount),amount);
        const finalCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');finalCanvas.width=size.w;finalCanvas.height=size.h;const finalCtx=finalCanvas.getContext('2d');
        if(!finalCtx)throw new Error('Image output canvas is unavailable.');finalCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;finalCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';finalCtx.drawImage(pipeline.canvas,0,0,size.w,size.h);
        const blob=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>finalCanvas.toBlob(b=>b?resolve(b):reject(new Error('Image export failed.')),'image/png'));
        pipeline.dispose();pipeline=null;if(!blob||!blob.size)throw new Error('Image export failed.');
        setPendingOutput(target,blob,quality);status(`${label} · ${size.w} × ${size.h} · Ready to apply`,100);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Enhancement ready — tap Apply to use it',{duration:1800});setTimeout(syncComparePanel,50);return;
      }
      if(target.type!=='video')throw new Error('Choose an image or video clip.');
      if(typeof MediaRecorder==='undefined'||!HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.captureStream)throw new Error('Video enhancement is not supported by this browser.');
      sourceVideo=document.createElement('video');sourceVideo.preload='auto';sourceVideo.playsInline=true;sourceVideo.crossOrigin=/^https?:/i.test(target.src)?'anonymous':'';sourceVideo.muted=false;sourceVideo.volume=1;sourceVideo.setAttribute('playsinline','');sourceVideo.src=target.src;
      await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let complete=false;const finish=e=>{if(complete)return;complete=true;sourceVideo.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata',loaded);sourceVideo.removeEventListener('error',failed);e?reject(e):resolve();};const loaded=()=>finish();const failed=()=>finish(new Error('Video could not be read. Local files or CORS-enabled media are required.'));sourceVideo.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',loaded,{once:true});sourceVideo.addEventListener('error',failed,{once:true});sourceVideo.load();if(sourceVideo.readyState>=1)finish();});
      await evPrepareReadableVideo(sourceVideo,target.model);
      if(!sourceVideo.videoWidth||!sourceVideo.videoHeight||!Number.isFinite(sourceVideo.duration)||sourceVideo.duration<=0)throw new Error('Video has no readable frames.');
      const sourceStart=clamp(Number(target.model.sourceOffset)||0,0,Math.max(0,sourceVideo.duration-.06)),clipSpeed=clamp(Number(target.model.speed)||1,.05,4);
      const timelineSpan=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(target.model.trimEnd)-Number(target.model.trimStart)||sourceVideo.duration),sourceSpan=Math.min(Math.max(.06,sourceVideo.duration-sourceStart),timelineSpan*clipSpeed),duration=Math.max(.06,sourceSpan/clipSpeed);
      if(sourceStart>.025){await new Promise(resolve=>{let settled=false,guard=0;const done=()=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;sourceVideo.removeEventListener('seeked',done);clearTimeout(guard);resolve();};sourceVideo.addEventListener('seeked',done,{once:true});guard=setTimeout(done,2600);try{sourceVideo.currentTime=sourceStart;}catch(_e){done();}});}
      sourceVideo.playbackRate=clipSpeed;
      try{const cap=sourceVideo.captureStream||sourceVideo.mozCaptureStream;if(typeof cap==='function')sourceStream=cap.call(sourceVideo);}catch(_e){}
      let sourceFps=30;try{const vt=sourceStream&&sourceStream.getVideoTracks&&sourceStream.getVideoTracks()[0],settings=vt&&vt.getSettings?vt.getSettings():null;const detected=Number(settings&&settings.frameRate);if(Number.isFinite(detected)&&detected>=1&&detected<=120)sourceFps=detected;}catch(_e){}
      status('Checking encoder…',0);
      chosenEnc=await evChooseEnhanceEncoder(sourceVideo.videoWidth,sourceVideo.videoHeight,quality,opts.maxQuality,(q)=>status('Checking '+evQLabel(q)+' encoder…',0));
      if(!chosenEnc)throw new Error('This phone\'s browser cannot encode video here. Close other apps/tabs and try again.');
      if(chosenEnc.quality<requestedQuality){
        quality=chosenEnc.quality;label=qualityLabel(quality);
        if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('This phone can\'t encode '+evQLabel(requestedQuality)+' — enhancing at '+evQLabel(quality)+' instead',{duration:3200});
      }
      const size=chosenEnc.size;pipeline=createEnhancePipeline(sourceVideo.videoWidth,sourceVideo.videoHeight,size.w,size.h);
      canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=size.w;canvas.height=size.h;ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false,desynchronized:true});if(!ctx)throw new Error('Video output canvas is unavailable.');ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
      let manualFrames=false;try{outStream=canvas.captureStream(0);canvasTrack=outStream.getVideoTracks&&outStream.getVideoTracks()[0];manualFrames=!!(canvasTrack&&typeof canvasTrack.requestFrame==='function');if(!manualFrames){outStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());outStream=canvas.captureStream(sourceFps);canvasTrack=outStream.getVideoTracks&&outStream.getVideoTracks()[0];}}catch(_e){outStream=canvas.captureStream(sourceFps);canvasTrack=outStream.getVideoTracks&&outStream.getVideoTracks()[0];}
      // Route the decoded source audio into the output stream without playing it through phone speakers.
      let audioAdded=false;
      try{
        const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;
        if(AC&&sourceVideo){audioContext=new AC();await audioContext.resume();audioSource=audioContext.createMediaElementSource(sourceVideo);audioDestination=audioContext.createMediaStreamDestination();audioSource.connect(audioDestination);silentGain=audioContext.createGain();silentGain.gain.value=0;audioSource.connect(silentGain);silentGain.connect(audioContext.destination);const tracks=audioDestination.stream.getAudioTracks();tracks.forEach(t=>outStream.addTrack(t));audioAdded=tracks.length>0;}
      }catch(audioErr){console.warn('Ethvelle: Web Audio routing unavailable; using the captured source audio track when present.',audioErr);}
      if(!audioAdded&&sourceStream){try{sourceStream.getAudioTracks().forEach(t=>outStream.addTrack(t));}catch(_e){}}
      const mimeCandidates=['video/webm;codecs=h264,opus','video/webm;codecs=avc1,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8','video/webm;codecs=vp9','video/webm'];
      const isH264Mime=candidate=>candidate==='video/webm;codecs=h264,opus'||candidate==='video/webm;codecs=avc1,opus';
      const supportedMimeCandidates=mimeCandidates.filter(candidate=>{try{return isH264Mime(candidate)?!!(MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported&&MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(candidate)):(!MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported||MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(candidate));}catch(_e){return false;}});
      const mime=(chosenEnc&&chosenEnc.mime)||supportedMimeCandidates[0]||'';
      let recorderError=null;for(const candidate of [mime,...supportedMimeCandidates.filter(x=>x!==mime),'']){if(recorder||candidate===null)break;try{recorder=candidate?new MediaRecorder(outStream,{mimeType:candidate,videoBitsPerSecond:(chosenEnc&&candidate===chosenEnc.mime)?chosenEnc.bitrate:isH264Mime(candidate)?(quality>=2160?14000000:quality>=1440?9000000:6000000):(quality>=2160?18000000:quality>=1440?12000000:8000000)}):new MediaRecorder(outStream);}catch(err){recorderError=err;recorder=null;}}
      if(!recorder)throw new Error((recorderError&&recorderError.message)||'This browser could not start a compatible video encoder. Try 1080p or reopen the source clip.');
      const chunks=[];const stopPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{stopResolve=resolve;recorder.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size)chunks.push(e.data);};recorder.onerror=e=>reject(e.error||new Error('Video encoding failed.'));recorder.onstop=resolve;});
      recordingStartedAt=Date.now();try{recorder.start(500);}catch(err){throw new Error('The browser video recorder could not start: '+(err&&err.message||'unknown error'));}
      const drawFrame=()=>{if(sourceVideo.readyState<2||!sourceVideo.videoWidth)return false;pipeline.render(sourceVideo,filterFor(target.model,amount),amount);ctx.clearRect(0,0,size.w,size.h);ctx.drawImage(pipeline.canvas,0,0,size.w,size.h);try{if(manualFrames&&canvasTrack&&canvasTrack.requestFrame)canvasTrack.requestFrame();}catch(_e){}return true;};
      const stopOnce=()=>{if(stopped||stopScheduled)return;const elapsed=Date.now()-recordingStartedAt;if(elapsed<750){stopScheduled=true;stopMinTimer=setTimeout(()=>{stopScheduled=false;stopOnce();},750-elapsed);return;}stopped=true;if(endPoll){clearInterval(endPoll);endPoll=0;}if(raf)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);if(rvfc&&sourceVideo&&sourceVideo.cancelVideoFrameCallback){try{sourceVideo.cancelVideoFrameCallback(rvfc);}catch(_e){}}if(watchdog)clearTimeout(watchdog);try{if(recorder&&recorder.state!=='inactive'){try{if(typeof recorder.requestData==='function')recorder.requestData();}catch(_e){}stopFlushTimer=setTimeout(()=>{try{if(recorder&&recorder.state!=='inactive')recorder.stop();else if(stopResolve)stopResolve();}catch(_e){if(stopResolve)stopResolve();}},160);}else if(stopResolve)stopResolve();}catch(_e){if(stopResolve)stopResolve();}};
      watchdog=setTimeout(()=>stopOnce(),Math.max(15000,Math.ceil(duration*1000)+10000));
      const reportAndContinue=()=>{const pct=clamp((sourceVideo.currentTime-sourceStart)/Math.max(.06,sourceSpan)*100,0,99);status(`${Math.floor(pct)}%`,pct);if(sourceVideo.ended||sourceVideo.currentTime>=sourceStart+sourceSpan-.025){stopOnce();return false;}return true;};
      const onVideoFrame=()=>{if(stopped)return;try{drawFrame();}catch(e){status('Video frame processing failed: '+(e&&e.message||''),null);stopOnce();return;}if(!reportAndContinue())return;if(typeof sourceVideo.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function')rvfc=sourceVideo.requestVideoFrameCallback(onVideoFrame);};
      try{drawFrame();}catch(_e){}
      await sourceVideo.play();
      endPoll=setInterval(()=>{if(stopped){clearInterval(endPoll);endPoll=0;return;}try{if(sourceVideo&&(sourceVideo.ended||sourceVideo.currentTime>=sourceStart+sourceSpan-.025))stopOnce();}catch(_e){}},120);
      if(typeof sourceVideo.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function')rvfc=sourceVideo.requestVideoFrameCallback(onVideoFrame);
      else{let lastDraw=0;const draw=now=>{if(stopped)return;if(sourceVideo.readyState>=2&&now-lastDraw>=1000/sourceFps){lastDraw=now;try{drawFrame();}catch(e){status('Video frame processing failed: '+(e&&e.message||''),null);stopOnce();return;}if(!reportAndContinue())return;}raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);};raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);}
      await stopPromise;
      const usableChunks=chunks.filter(c=>c&&c.size>0);if(!usableChunks.length)throw new Error('The browser recorder started but produced no video frames. Try 1080p, then reopen the clip and try again.');
      const blob=new Blob(usableChunks,{type:recorder.mimeType||mime||'video/webm'});if(!blob.size)throw new Error('The video output is empty.');
      let finalBlob=blob;try{if(typeof window.__evltMakeRecordedMediaSeekable==='function')finalBlob=(await window.__evltMakeRecordedMediaSeekable(blob))||blob;}catch(_e){finalBlob=blob;}
      setPendingOutput(target,finalBlob,quality);status(`${label} · ${size.w} × ${size.h} · Ready to apply`,100);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Enhancement ready — tap Apply to use it',{duration:1800});setTimeout(syncComparePanel,50);
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle V5 enhancement:',err);
      const msg=String(err&&(err.message||err.name)||'');
      const lowerQ=evNextLowerEnhanceQuality(quality);
      if(target.type==='video'&&lowerQ&&/encoder|encoding|configuration|not supported|no video frames|recorder/i.test(msg)){
        retryMax=lowerQ;
        try{evEncProbeCache.forEach((v,k)=>{if(k.startsWith(chosenEnc&&chosenEnc.size?chosenEnc.size.w+'x'+chosenEnc.size.h+'|':'\u0000'))evEncProbeCache.delete(k);});}catch(_e){}
        status(evQLabel(quality)+' encoder failed — retrying at '+evQLabel(lowerQ)+'…',0);
        if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(evQLabel(quality)+' encoder failed — retrying at '+evQLabel(lowerQ),{duration:2400});
      }else{
        status(err&&err.message?err.message:'Enhancement failed.',null);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(err&&err.message?err.message:'Enhancement failed',{duration:2200});
      }
    }
    finally{
      if(raf)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);if(rvfc&&sourceVideo&&sourceVideo.cancelVideoFrameCallback){try{sourceVideo.cancelVideoFrameCallback(rvfc);}catch(_e){}}if(watchdog)clearTimeout(watchdog);if(endPoll){clearInterval(endPoll);endPoll=0;}if(stopMinTimer)clearTimeout(stopMinTimer);if(stopFlushTimer)clearTimeout(stopFlushTimer);
      try{if(recorder&&recorder.state!=='inactive')recorder.stop();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(sourceVideo){sourceVideo.pause();sourceVideo.removeAttribute('src');sourceVideo.load();}}catch(_e){}
      try{if(sourceStream)sourceStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());}catch(_e){}try{if(outStream)outStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());}catch(_e){}
      try{if(audioContext&&audioContext.state!=='closed')await audioContext.close();}catch(_e){}try{if(pipeline)pipeline.dispose();}catch(_e){}
      mediaAudioSnapshot.forEach(state=>{try{if(state.media&&state.media.isConnected){state.media.muted=state.muted;state.media.volume=Number.isFinite(state.volume)?state.volume:1;if(state.media.tagName==='VIDEO'){state.media.autoplay=false;state.media.removeAttribute('autoplay');state.media.pause();}}}catch(_e){}});
      window.__ethvelleV5EnhanceBusy=false;if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='Enhance';}const apply=$('evltEnhanceApply');if(apply)apply.disabled=!pendingEnhancement;
    }
    if(retryMax)return evltStartEnhanceCore({maxQuality:retryMax});
  };
  // Toolbar visibility is deliberately not written from this V5 enhancement patch.
  // V80 is the single owner of bottomToolsDefault / bottomToolsClip / bottomToolsKeyframe
  // display state. Previously this patch wrote non-important inline display values and
  // observed those same writes while V80 re-applied important values, creating a feedback loop.
  // Opening Enhance renders the old base preview first; restore the proper right-side AFTER layer.
  const panel=$('toolPanel');if(panel)new MutationObserver(()=>{if($('evltCompare'))syncComparePanel();}).observe(panel,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<div id="ethvelleStoragePermission" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="evspTitle">
  <div class="evsp-sheet">
    <div class="evsp-mark" aria-hidden="true">▣</div>
    <h2 id="evspTitle">Allow device storage</h2>
    <p>Ethvelle needs local browser storage to keep your projects on this device, including timeline edits, uploaded clips, images, and project settings. When supported, the media picker can link directly to a selected device file instead of copying a long video.</p>
    <div class="evsp-info"><span aria-hidden="true">ⓘ</span><span>This approval enables Ethvelle's browser storage; it is not unrestricted Android storage permission. Browser storage can still be cleared, and direct file access depends on browser support and permission, so export important work as a backup.</span></div>
    <div class="evsp-actions"><button class="evsp-cancel" id="evspCancel" type="button">Not now</button><button class="evsp-allow" id="evspAllow" type="button">Allow &amp; continue</button></div>
  </div>
