<script id="ethvelle-device-storage-enhance-fix-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const CONSENT_KEY='ethvelle.deviceStorageConsent.v1';
  const DB_NAME='ethvelle-device-project-storage-v1';
  const SOURCE_DB_NAME='ethvelle-source-media-v1';
  let dbPromise=null,sourceDbPromise=null,saveTimer=0,lastStorageErrorAt=0,pendingCreate=false;
  let canvasComparePercent=50,canvasCompareDrag=null;
  function openSourceMediaDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.reject(new Error('This browser does not support device media storage.'));
    if(!sourceDbPromise){
      sourceDbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const req=indexedDB.open(SOURCE_DB_NAME,1);
        req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('assets'))db.createObjectStore('assets');};
        req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not open device media storage.'));
        req.onblocked=()=>reject(new Error('Device media storage is busy in another tab.'));
      }).catch(err=>{sourceDbPromise=null;throw err;});
    }
    return sourceDbPromise;
  }
  async function putSourceMedia(key,blob,meta={}){
    const db=await openSourceMediaDb();
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('assets','readwrite');tx.objectStore('assets').put({blob,name:meta.name||'Video',type:meta.type||blob.type||'video/mp4',size:blob.size||0,lastModified:meta.lastModified||0,savedAt:Date.now()},key);tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Could not save the selected media file.'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Media save was interrupted.'));});
    return key;
  }
  async function getSourceMedia(key){const db=await openSourceMediaDb();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('assets','readonly'),req=tx.objectStore('assets').get(key);req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result||null);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not read the saved media file.'));});}
  async function listSourceMediaRecords(){
    const db=await openSourceMediaDb();
    return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const tx=db.transaction('assets','readonly'),req=tx.objectStore('assets').openCursor(),rows=[];
      req.onsuccess=()=>{const cur=req.result;if(!cur){resolve(rows);return;}rows.push({key:String(cur.key),...(cur.value||{})});cur.continue();};
      req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not list device media.'));
    });
  }
  function mediaFamilyMatches(model,record){
    const family=String(model&&model.type||'').toLowerCase();
    const mime=String(record&&record.type||'').toLowerCase();
    const name=String(record&&record.name||'').toLowerCase();
    if(family==='video')return mime.startsWith('video/')||/\.(mp4|m4v|f4v|mov|webm|mkv|avi|3gp|3gpp|3g2|mpeg|mpg|mpe|m2v|ogv|ts|mts|m2ts|wmv|flv)$/i.test(name);
    if(family==='audio')return mime.startsWith('audio/')||/\.(mp3|m4a|aac|wav|ogg|opus|flac|aiff?)$/i.test(name);
    if(family==='image')return mime.startsWith('image/')||/\.(png|apng|jpe?g|jfif|webp|gif|avif|bmp|dib|heic|heif|tif|tiff|svg|ico|jxl)$/i.test(name);
    return true;
  }
  function repairLegacyOverlayMediaRefs(root,records){
    let changed=false;
    const list=Array.isArray(root)?root:[root];
    for(const project of list){
      const state=project&&project.savedState;if(!state)continue;
      const updatedAt=Number(project.updatedAt)||0;
      const mediaModels=[...(Array.isArray(state.overlays)?state.overlays:[]),...(Array.isArray(state.backgrounds)?state.backgrounds:[])];
      for(const model of mediaModels){
        if(!model||typeof model.src!=='string')continue;
        const wrapped=/^url\(\s*blob:/i.test(model.src);
        const isBlob=model.src.startsWith('blob:')||wrapped;
        if(!isBlob)continue;
        let key=model.deviceMediaKey?String(model.deviceMediaKey):'';
        let record=key?records.find(r=>String(r.key)===key):null;
        if(!record){
          const names=[model.deviceMediaName,model.name].filter(Boolean).map(v=>String(v));
          let candidates=records.filter(r=>names.includes(String(r.name||''))&&mediaFamilyMatches(model,r));
          // If duplicate filenames exist, prefer the saved file closest to this project's last
          // edit time. A small timestamp window prevents an old, unrelated same-name asset from
          // being attached to the wrong clip when a more precise metadata match is unavailable.
          if(updatedAt){
            const near=candidates.filter(r=>!Number(r.savedAt)||Math.abs(Number(r.savedAt)-updatedAt)<=14*24*60*60*1000);
            if(near.length)candidates=near;
            candidates.sort((a,b)=>Math.abs((Number(a.savedAt)||0)-updatedAt)-Math.abs((Number(b.savedAt)||0)-updatedAt));
          }else candidates.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.savedAt)||0)-(Number(a.savedAt)||0));
          record=candidates[0]||null;
          if(record){key=String(record.key);model.deviceMediaKey=key;model.deviceMediaName=record.name||model.name;model.deviceMediaSize=Number(record.size)||undefined;model.deviceMediaLastModified=Number(record.lastModified)||undefined;}
        }
        if(key){
          model.src=wrapped?'url(ethvelle-device-media://'+key+')':'ethvelle-device-media://'+key;
          changed=true;
        }
      }
    }
    return changed;
  }
  function projectSnapshotWithMediaRefs(){
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(projects,function(key,value){
      if(key==='src'&&typeof value==='string'&&this&&this.deviceMediaKey){
        if(value.startsWith('blob:'))return 'ethvelle-device-media://'+this.deviceMediaKey;
        const m=value.match(/^url\((blob:[^)]+)\)$/i);if(m)return 'url(ethvelle-device-media://'+this.deviceMediaKey+')';
      }
      return value;
    }));
  }
  async function rehydrateDeviceMediaRefs(root){
    const tasks=[],seen=new Set();
    const walk=(v)=>{if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||seen.has(v))return;seen.add(v);
      if(v.deviceMediaKey&&typeof v.src==='string'&&v.src.includes('ethvelle-device-media://')){
        const wrapped=/^url\(/i.test(v.src),key=String(v.deviceMediaKey);
        tasks.push((async()=>{try{
          const rec=await getSourceMedia(key);if(!rec)throw new Error('Saved media is missing');let mediaBlob=rec.blob||null;
          if(!mediaBlob&&rec.handle&&typeof rec.handle.getFile==='function'){
            if(typeof rec.handle.queryPermission==='function'){
              const permission=await rec.handle.queryPermission({mode:'read'});
              if(permission!=='granted')throw new Error('The browser needs renewed permission for this device file. Re-select it from the device picker.');
            }
            mediaBlob=await rec.handle.getFile();
            // Cache a durable Blob copy now so later refreshes do not depend on the original path.
            try{await putSourceMedia(key,mediaBlob,{name:rec.name,type:rec.type,lastModified:rec.lastModified});}
            catch(cacheErr){console.warn('Ethvelle: legacy media could not be upgraded to Blob storage',key,cacheErr);}
          }
          if(!mediaBlob)throw new Error('Saved media is missing');
          const url=URL.createObjectURL(mediaBlob);v.src=wrapped?'url('+url+')':url;delete v.__evDeviceMediaMissing;
        }catch(e){v.__evDeviceMediaMissing=true;console.warn('Ethvelle: could not restore device media',key,e);if(!window.__evDeviceMediaWarningShown){window.__evDeviceMediaWarningShown=true;setTimeout(()=>toast('A media file needs renewed device-file permission. Re-select that file if it appears missing.'),900);}}})());
      }
      if(Array.isArray(v))v.forEach(walk);else Object.keys(v).forEach(k=>walk(v[k]));
    };
    walk(root);await Promise.all(tasks);
  }
  function toast(msg){try{if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(msg,{duration:2600});}catch(_e){}}
  function openProjectDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.reject(new Error('This browser does not support IndexedDB.'));
    if(!dbPromise){
      dbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const req=indexedDB.open(DB_NAME,1);
        req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('snapshots'))db.createObjectStore('snapshots',{keyPath:'key'});};
        req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not open device storage.'));
        req.onblocked=()=>reject(new Error('Device storage is busy in another tab.'));
      }).catch(err=>{dbPromise=null;throw err;});
    }
    return dbPromise;
  }
  function approxJsonSize(root,limit){
    let total=0,stack=[root],seen=0;
    while(stack.length){const v=stack.pop();if(v==null)continue;const t=typeof v;
      if(t==='string'){total+=v.length+2;}
      else if(t==='number'||t==='boolean'){total+=16;}
      else if(t==='object'){
        if(++seen>150000)return limit+1;
        if(Array.isArray(v)){total+=2;for(let i=0;i<v.length;i++)stack.push(v[i]);}
        else{total+=2;for(const k of Object.keys(v)){total+=k.length+4;stack.push(v[k]);}}
      }
      if(total>limit)return total;
    }
    return total;
  }
  function makeProjectIndex(){
    try{return projects.map(p=>({id:p.id,name:p.name,ratio:p.ratio,created:p.created,duration:p.duration,updatedAt:p.updatedAt||0,thumbnail:(typeof p.thumbnail==='string'&&!/^data:/i.test(p.thumbnail))?p.thumbnail:undefined,savedState:null,__evIndexOnly:true}));}
    catch(_e){return [];}
  }
  let deviceSnapshotQueue=Promise.resolve();
  async function saveFullProjectsToDevice(snapshotOverride){
    const snapshot=snapshotOverride||projectSnapshotWithMediaRefs();
    const task=deviceSnapshotQueue.catch(()=>{}).then(async()=>{
      const db=await openProjectDb();
      await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const tx=db.transaction('snapshots','readwrite');
        tx.objectStore('snapshots').put({key:'all-projects',projects:snapshot,savedAt:Date.now()});
        tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Project save failed.'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Project save was interrupted.'));
      });
      return true;
    });
    deviceSnapshotQueue=task;
    return task;
  }
  window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow=()=>saveFullProjectsToDevice();
  function queueDeviceSave(delay){
    clearTimeout(saveTimer);
    saveTimer=setTimeout(()=>{saveTimer=0;saveFullProjectsToDevice().catch(err=>{
      console.error('Ethvelle project storage:',err);
      const now=Date.now();if(now-lastStorageErrorAt>10000){lastStorageErrorAt=now;toast('Could not save all project data to this device. Export a backup before leaving.');}
    });},Number.isFinite(delay)?delay:250);
  }
  // Keep small projects in localStorage for quick startup; keep big video/image projects in IndexedDB
  // so a few large Base64 media files cannot exhaust the usual localStorage quota.
  let localIndexGeneration=0;
  window.saveProjectsToStorage=function(){
    let size=0;try{size=approxJsonSize(projects,1400000);}catch(_e){size=1400001;}
    // Every save supersedes previous asynchronous index publications, including a transition
    // from a large/device-media project back to the small localStorage branch.
    const generation=++localIndexGeneration;
    let hasDeviceMedia=false;
    try{hasDeviceMedia=(projects||[]).some(p=>{const st=p&&p.savedState;if(!st)return false;return [...(Array.isArray(st.overlays)?st.overlays:[]),...(Array.isArray(st.backgrounds)?st.backgrounds:[])].some(m=>m&&m.deviceMediaKey);});}catch(_e){}
    if(size<=1400000&&!hasDeviceMedia){
      try{localStorage.setItem('riggy.projects.v1',JSON.stringify(projects));}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle local project save:',err);}
      queueDeviceSave(240);
      return true;
    }
    const snapshot=projectSnapshotWithMediaRefs();
    return saveFullProjectsToDevice(snapshot).then(()=>{
      if(generation===localIndexGeneration)localStorage.setItem('riggy.projects.v1',JSON.stringify(makeProjectIndex()));
      return true;
    }).catch(err=>{
      console.error('Ethvelle durable project save failed:',err);
      const now=Date.now();if(now-lastStorageErrorAt>10000){lastStorageErrorAt=now;toast('Could not finish saving this project to device storage. Keep the editor open and export a backup before leaving.');}
      return false;
    });
  };
  async function restoreProjectsFromDevice(){
    try{
      const db=await openProjectDb();
      const rec=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('snapshots','readonly'),req=tx.objectStore('snapshots').get('all-projects');req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result||null);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not read stored projects.'));});
      if(!rec||!Array.isArray(rec.projects))return;
      const device=rec.projects;
      let sourceMediaRecords=[];
      try{sourceMediaRecords=await listSourceMediaRecords();}catch(mediaErr){console.warn('Ethvelle media recovery lookup:',mediaErr);}
      const repairedDevice=repairLegacyOverlayMediaRefs(device,sourceMediaRecords);
      await rehydrateDeviceMediaRefs(device);
      const local=Array.isArray(projects)?projects:[],byId=new Map();
      device.forEach(p=>{if(p&&p.id!=null)byId.set(String(p.id),p);});
      local.forEach(p=>{
        if(!p||p.id==null)return;const id=String(p.id),saved=byId.get(id);
        if(!saved||(!p.__evIndexOnly&&Number(p.updatedAt||0)>Number(saved.updatedAt||0)))byId.set(id,p);
      });
      projects=Array.from(byId.values()).map(p=>{if(p&&p.__evIndexOnly){const q={...p};delete q.__evIndexOnly;return q;}return p;});
      const repairedMerged=repairLegacyOverlayMediaRefs(projects,sourceMediaRecords);
      await rehydrateDeviceMediaRefs(projects);
      try{await Promise.race([window.__evFramesGate,new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,5000))]);if(window.__evSourceFrames)await window.__evSourceFrames.restoreForProjects(projects);}catch(frameErr){console.warn('Ethvelle frame restore:',frameErr);}
      projects.sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.created||0)-new Date(a.created||0));
      // Make repaired references durable so the next refresh does not have to rediscover them.
      if(repairedDevice||repairedMerged){
        try{window.saveProjectsToStorage();await saveFullProjectsToDevice();}catch(saveErr){console.warn('Ethvelle repaired-media snapshot:',saveErr);}
      }
      try{if(typeof window.renderProjects==='function')window.renderProjects();if(typeof window.updateProfileUI==='function')window.updateProfileUI();}catch(_e){}
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: stored project recovery unavailable:',err);}
  }
  // Promote the storage request immediately from the user's click, then open the device DB.
  async function requestStorageAndContinue(){
    const allow=$('evspAllow'),backdrop=$('ethvelleStoragePermission');if(allow){allow.disabled=true;allow.textContent='Preparing storage…';}
    let persistenceGranted=null;
    try{if(navigator.storage&&typeof navigator.storage.persist==='function'){try{persistenceGranted=await navigator.storage.persist();}catch(_e){persistenceGranted=false;}}}catch(_e){persistenceGranted=false;}
    try{await openProjectDb();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: IndexedDB could not be opened:',err);}
    try{localStorage.setItem(CONSENT_KEY,'yes');}catch(_e){}
    backdrop?.classList.remove('active');if(allow){allow.disabled=false;allow.textContent='Allow & continue';}
    if(persistenceGranted===false)toast('Browser storage is available, but permanent retention was not guaranteed. Export important projects as backups.');
    if(pendingCreate){pendingCreate=false;try{if(originalCreateProject)originalCreateProject.call(window);if(projects&&projects[0])projects[0].updatedAt=Date.now();window.saveProjectsToStorage();}catch(err){console.error(err);toast('Project could not be created. Please try again.');}}
  }
  const modal=$('ethvelleStoragePermission');
  $('evspAllow')?.addEventListener('click',requestStorageAndContinue);
  $('evspCancel')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{pendingCreate=false;modal?.classList.remove('active');});
  modal?.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===modal){pendingCreate=false;modal.classList.remove('active');}});
  const originalCreateProject=window.createProject;
  if(typeof originalCreateProject==='function'&&!originalCreateProject.__evStorageGate){
    const gatedCreateProject=function(){
      let consent=false;try{consent=localStorage.getItem(CONSENT_KEY)==='yes';}catch(_e){}
      /* v219-fix: no permission panel any more. Storage is requested automatically in the background
         (persistent-storage request + opening the device DB) and the project is created straight away. */
      if(!consent){
        try{localStorage.setItem(CONSENT_KEY,'yes');}catch(_e){}
        try{if(navigator.storage&&typeof navigator.storage.persist==='function'){Promise.resolve(navigator.storage.persist()).catch(()=>{});}}catch(_e){}
        try{Promise.resolve(openProjectDb()).catch(err=>console.warn('Ethvelle: IndexedDB could not be opened:',err));}catch(_e){}
      }
      const result=originalCreateProject.apply(this,arguments);
      try{if(projects&&projects[0])projects[0].updatedAt=Date.now();window.saveProjectsToStorage();}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    gatedCreateProject.__evStorageGate=true;window.createProject=gatedCreateProject;
  }
  const originalPersist=window.persistProjectState;
  if(typeof originalPersist==='function'&&!originalPersist.__evDeviceSave){
    const wrappedPersist=function(){try{if(typeof currentProject!=='undefined'&&currentProject)currentProject.updatedAt=Date.now();}catch(_e){}return originalPersist.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedPersist.__evDeviceSave=true;window.persistProjectState=wrappedPersist;
  }
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=0;saveFullProjectsToDevice().catch(()=>{});});
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.visibilityState==='hidden'){clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=0;saveFullProjectsToDevice().catch(()=>{});}});
  // Opening a project before the IndexedDB restore finishes used to pass the early
  // localStorage index-only record (savedState:null) into openEditor. That made every Clips
  // row appear empty until the user re-uploaded media, even though the full snapshot existed.
  // Publish a restore promise and let the final openEditor guard wait for it.
  window.__evFramesGate = new Promise(r=>{window.__evFramesGateResolve=r;});
  window.__ethvelleProjectsRestoreReady = false;
  window.__ethvelleProjectsRestorePromise = Promise.resolve()
    .then(()=>restoreProjectsFromDevice())
    .catch(err=>{console.warn('Ethvelle project restore:',err);})
    .finally(()=>{window.__ethvelleProjectsRestoreReady = true;});

  // Video thumbnails are generated only by the time-windowed sequential decoder in V9.
  // The compatibility generateVideoThumbnails() helper above resolves metadata only.

  // Legacy per-tile live-video fallback removed; video rows use the shared lazy tile decoder.

  // Compare the selected media directly on the main canvas. Only the white wipe/handle accepts
  // pointer input; the comparison slider in the settings sheet is deliberately removed.
  function unwrappedSource(src){let s=String(src||'').trim(),m=s.match(/^url\((.*)\)$/i);if(m)s=m[1].trim().replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g,'');return s;}
  function getEnhanceTarget(){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
    try{const bg=(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'?backgrounds:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===id);if(bg&&unwrappedSource(bg.src))return {kind:'background',model:bg,type:'image',src:unwrappedSource(bg.src)};}catch(_e){}
    try{const ov=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===id);if(ov&&['video','image'].includes(ov.type)&&unwrappedSource(ov.src))return {kind:'overlay',model:ov,type:ov.type,src:unwrappedSource(ov.src)};}catch(_e){}
    if(!id){try{const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0):null;if(bg&&unwrappedSource(bg.src))return {kind:'background',model:bg,type:'image',src:unwrappedSource(bg.src)};}catch(_e){}}
    return null;
  }
  function transformFor(target){
    const t=target.kind==='background'?(target.model.transform||((typeof bgTransform!=='undefined')?bgTransform:null)):target.model.transform;
    if(!t)return 'none';return `translate(${Number(t.offsetX)||0}px,${Number(t.offsetY)||0}px) rotate(${Number(t.rotation)||0}deg) scale(${Number(t.scale)||1})`;
  }
  function referenceMediaForCompare(target){
    try{
      if(target.kind==='background')return $('bgImgLayer');
      const host=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[target.model.id]:null;
      if(!host)return null;
      if(host.matches&&host.matches('video,img,canvas'))return host;
      return host.querySelector?host.querySelector('video,img,canvas'):host;
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  let comparePipeline=null,comparePipelineKey='',compareLastPlaybackSyncAt=0;
  let compareSnap=null,compareSnapCtx=null,compareLastSource=null,compareLastKey='',compareLastTime=-1,compareLastCost=0,compareRvfcEl=null,compareRvfcId=0,compareLoopTimer=0;
  const COMPARE_PREVIEW_LONG_SIDE=720;
  function makeCompareMedia(target,after){
    const el=document.createElement('canvas');el.className='evcc-media';el.width=2;el.height=2;el.dataset.evSide=after?'after':'before';
    el.style.width='100%';el.style.height='100%';el.style.objectFit='contain';el.style.objectPosition='center';el.style.transform=transformFor(target);el.style.transformOrigin='center center';el.draggable=false;
    const reference=referenceMediaForCompare(target);if(reference){try{const cs=getComputedStyle(reference);if(cs.objectFit)el.style.objectFit=cs.objectFit;if(cs.objectPosition)el.style.objectPosition=cs.objectPosition;if(cs.transform&&cs.transform!=='none')el.style.transform=cs.transform;if(cs.transformOrigin)el.style.transformOrigin=cs.transformOrigin;}catch(_e){}}
    return el;
  }
  /* The tool panel's full-screen click-catcher (#panelBackdrop, z-index 100100) sits above the
     preview and swallowed every touch meant for the divider. Cut a hole in it exactly over the
     divider so the handle can be dragged while taps elsewhere still behave as before. */
  function updateBackdropHole(){
    const bd=document.getElementById('panelBackdrop');if(!bd)return;
    const wipe=document.querySelector('#ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot .evcc-wipe');
    if(!wipe){bd.style.removeProperty('clip-path');return;}
    const b=bd.getBoundingClientRect(),w0=wipe.getBoundingClientRect(),rr=wipe.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();if(!b.width||!w0.width)return;
    const l=Math.max(w0.left,rr.left),r=Math.min(w0.right,rr.right),w={left:l,top:w0.top,width:Math.max(0,r-l),height:w0.height};if(!w.width)return;
    const x=Math.round(w.left-b.left),y=Math.round(w.top-b.top),ww=Math.round(w.width),hh=Math.round(w.height),W=Math.round(b.width),H=Math.round(b.height);
    bd.style.setProperty('clip-path',`path(evenodd,'M0 0H${W}V${H}H0Z M${x} ${y}h${ww}v${hh}h-${ww}Z')`,'important');
  }
  window.__evUpdateBackdropHole=updateBackdropHole;
  function setCanvasComparePosition(p){
    canvasComparePercent=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(p)||0));const root=$('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot');if(!root)return;
    const after=root.querySelector('.evcc-after'),wipe=root.querySelector('.evcc-wipe');if(after)after.style.clipPath=`inset(0 0 0 ${canvasComparePercent}%)`;
    if(wipe){const width=root.clientWidth||root.getBoundingClientRect().width||0,x=width*canvasComparePercent/100;wipe.style.left=x+'px';wipe.style.setProperty('--evcc-line-offset','31px');updateBackdropHole();}
  }
  /* v287 BEFORE/AFTER SYNC FIX. The two halves used to be filled by two separate reads of the live video
     (the AFTER half through a full-resolution WebGL pass that was also rebuilt every time playback started or
     stopped), so AFTER could trail BEFORE by 0.1-0.5 s. Now:
       1. ONE snapshot of the current video frame is taken per update;
       2. BEFORE and AFTER are both drawn from that same snapshot in the same synchronous block, so they can never
          show different moments;
       3. updates are driven by the video's own frame callback (requestVideoFrameCallback) plus seeked/loadeddata,
          so both halves advance on exactly the frames the video presents;
       4. the preview pipeline works on the small snapshot and is NOT rebuilt on play/pause, so it is cheap and
          never stalls the start of playback. */
  function syncCanvasCompare(force){
    force=(force===true);
    const root=$('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot');if(!root)return;
    const target=getEnhanceTarget();if(!target)return;
    const before=root.querySelector('.evcc-before .evcc-media'),after=root.querySelector('.evcc-after .evcc-media');if(!before||!after)return;
    const source=referenceMediaForCompare(target);if(!source)return;
    const isVideo=source.tagName==='VIDEO';
    const sw=Number(source.videoWidth||source.naturalWidth||source.width)||0,sh=Number(source.videoHeight||source.naturalHeight||source.height)||0;
    if(!sw||!sh||(isVideo&&source.readyState<2))return;
    const live=!!(isPlaying||window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing);
    const now=performance.now();
    if(live&&!force){
      // While playing, the video frame callback drives the updates; only fall back to the render loop when it is unavailable.
      if(isVideo&&compareRvfcEl===source&&typeof source.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function')return;
      if(compareLastPlaybackSyncAt&&now-compareLastPlaybackSyncAt<33)return;
    }
    const factor=Math.min(1,COMPARE_PREVIEW_LONG_SIDE/Math.max(sw,sh)),pw=Math.max(2,Math.round(sw*factor)),ph=Math.max(2,Math.round(sh*factor));
    const amount=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number($('evltEnhanceAmount')?.value??target.model.enhanceAmount??0)||0));
    const filter=typeof window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter==='function'?window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(target.model.adjustments,0):'none';
    const key=`${source.currentSrc||source.src||''}|${pw}x${ph}|${amount}|${filter}`,t=isVideo?Number(source.currentTime)||0:0;
    if(!live&&!force&&source===compareLastSource&&key===compareLastKey&&t===compareLastTime)return;
    if(!isVideo&&!force&&source===compareLastSource&&key===compareLastKey)return;
    const started=performance.now();
    // 1) ONE snapshot of the current frame - both halves are built from this and nothing else.
    if(!compareSnap)compareSnap=document.createElement('canvas');
    if(compareSnap.width!==pw||compareSnap.height!==ph){compareSnap.width=pw;compareSnap.height=ph;compareSnapCtx=null;}
    if(!compareSnapCtx){compareSnapCtx=compareSnap.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});if(!compareSnapCtx)return;compareSnapCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;compareSnapCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';}
    try{compareSnapCtx.drawImage(source,0,0,pw,ph);}catch(_e){return;}
    // 2) Preview pipeline sized to the snapshot (rebuilt only if the preview size itself changes).
    const pkey=pw+'x'+ph;
    if(comparePipelineKey!==pkey||!comparePipeline){
      try{if(comparePipeline)comparePipeline.dispose();comparePipeline=window.ethvelleCreateEnhancePipeline(pw,ph,pw,ph);comparePipelineKey=pkey;}
      catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: comparison processor unavailable',err);comparePipeline=null;comparePipelineKey='';}
    }
    let processed=null;
    if(comparePipeline){try{processed=comparePipeline.render(compareSnap,filter,amount);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: comparison frame failed',err);}}
    // 3) Paint BEFORE and AFTER back-to-back in the same synchronous block, from the same snapshot.
    if(before.width!==pw||before.height!==ph){before.width=pw;before.height=ph;}
    if(after.width!==pw||after.height!==ph){after.width=pw;after.height=ph;}
    const beforeCtx=before.getContext('2d'),afterCtx=after.getContext('2d');
    if(beforeCtx){beforeCtx.save();beforeCtx.filter=filter;beforeCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;beforeCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';beforeCtx.clearRect(0,0,pw,ph);beforeCtx.drawImage(compareSnap,0,0,pw,ph);beforeCtx.restore();}
    if(afterCtx&&processed){afterCtx.clearRect(0,0,pw,ph);afterCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;afterCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';afterCtx.drawImage(processed,0,0,pw,ph);}
    if(!live)setCanvasComparePosition(canvasComparePercent);
    compareLastSource=source;compareLastKey=key;compareLastTime=t;compareLastPlaybackSyncAt=performance.now();compareLastCost=compareLastPlaybackSyncAt-started;
  }
  const compareFrameHandler=()=>{try{syncCanvasCompare(true);}catch(_e){}};
  function detachCompareFrameLoop(){
    if(compareLoopTimer){clearInterval(compareLoopTimer);compareLoopTimer=0;}
    if(compareRvfcEl){
      try{if(compareRvfcId&&typeof compareRvfcEl.cancelVideoFrameCallback==='function')compareRvfcEl.cancelVideoFrameCallback(compareRvfcId);}catch(_e){}
      try{compareRvfcEl.removeEventListener('seeked',compareFrameHandler);compareRvfcEl.removeEventListener('loadeddata',compareFrameHandler);compareRvfcEl.removeEventListener('pause',compareFrameHandler);}catch(_e){}
    }
    compareRvfcEl=null;compareRvfcId=0;
  }
  function attachCompareFrameLoop(){
    detachCompareFrameLoop();
    const check=()=>{
      if(!$('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot')){detachCompareFrameLoop();return;}
      const tg=getEnhanceTarget(),src=tg?referenceMediaForCompare(tg):null;
      if(src===compareRvfcEl)return;
      if(compareRvfcEl){
        try{if(compareRvfcId&&typeof compareRvfcEl.cancelVideoFrameCallback==='function')compareRvfcEl.cancelVideoFrameCallback(compareRvfcId);}catch(_e){}
        try{compareRvfcEl.removeEventListener('seeked',compareFrameHandler);compareRvfcEl.removeEventListener('loadeddata',compareFrameHandler);compareRvfcEl.removeEventListener('pause',compareFrameHandler);}catch(_e){}
        compareRvfcEl=null;compareRvfcId=0;
      }
      if(src&&src.tagName==='VIDEO'){
        compareRvfcEl=src;
        src.addEventListener('seeked',compareFrameHandler);src.addEventListener('loadeddata',compareFrameHandler);src.addEventListener('pause',compareFrameHandler);
        if(typeof src.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function'){
          const onFrame=()=>{
            if(compareRvfcEl!==src||!$('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot'))return;
            try{syncCanvasCompare(true);}catch(_e){}
            try{compareRvfcId=src.requestVideoFrameCallback(onFrame);}catch(_e){}
          };
          try{compareRvfcId=src.requestVideoFrameCallback(onFrame);}catch(_e){}
        }
      }
    };
    check();compareLoopTimer=setInterval(check,250);
  }
  window.ethvelleSetCompareSplit=function(p){setCanvasComparePosition(p);};
  function removeCanvasCompare(){detachCompareFrameLoop();if(compareSnap){try{compareSnap.width=compareSnap.height=1;}catch(_e){}compareSnap=null;compareSnapCtx=null;}compareLastSource=null;compareLastKey='';compareLastTime=-1;compareLastPlaybackSyncAt=0;const root=$('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot');if(root)root.remove();updateBackdropHole();if(comparePipeline){try{comparePipeline.dispose();}catch(_e){}comparePipeline=null;comparePipelineKey='';}canvasCompareDrag=null;}
  function mountCanvasCompare(){
    removeCanvasCompare();const stage=$('canvasStage'),target=getEnhanceTarget();if(!stage||!target)return;
    const root=document.createElement('div');root.id='ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot';root.dataset.targetId=String(target.model.id||selectedClip||'');
    const before=document.createElement('div');before.className='evcc-layer evcc-before';before.appendChild(makeCompareMedia(target,false));
    const after=document.createElement('div');after.className='evcc-layer evcc-after';after.appendChild(makeCompareMedia(target,true));
    const wipe=document.createElement('div');wipe.className='evcc-wipe';wipe.setAttribute('role','slider');wipe.setAttribute('aria-label','Drag the before and after divider');wipe.setAttribute('tabindex','0');wipe.innerHTML='<span class="evcc-handle" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 6.5l-5.5 5.5 5.5 5.5M14 6.5l5.5 5.5-5.5 5.5"/></svg></span>';
    root.append(before,after,wipe);
    const btag=document.createElement('span');btag.className='evcc-tag before';btag.textContent='BEFORE';const atag=document.createElement('span');atag.className='evcc-tag after';atag.textContent='AFTER';root.append(btag,atag);stage.appendChild(root);
    const updateFromEvent=e=>{const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();if(r.width)setCanvasComparePosition((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width*100);};
    wipe.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      // The divider uses a 64px invisible touch target around the restored black handle.
      // A drag can begin anywhere along the rail, including at 0% and 100%.
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      try{if(typeof window.__evSweepCancel==='function')window.__evSweepCancel();}catch(_e){}
      canvasCompareDrag={id:e.pointerId,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,moved:false};
      try{wipe.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    });
    wipe.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(canvasCompareDrag&&canvasCompareDrag.id===e.pointerId){
      if(!canvasCompareDrag.moved&&Math.hypot(e.clientX-canvasCompareDrag.startX,e.clientY-canvasCompareDrag.startY)<5)return;
      canvasCompareDrag.moved=true;updateFromEvent(e);e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    }});
    const end=e=>{if(canvasCompareDrag&&canvasCompareDrag.id===e.pointerId){canvasCompareDrag=null;try{wipe.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}};
    wipe.addEventListener('pointerup',end);wipe.addEventListener('pointercancel',end);
    wipe.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){setCanvasComparePosition(canvasComparePercent-2);e.preventDefault();}else if(e.key==='ArrowRight'){setCanvasComparePosition(canvasComparePercent+2);e.preventDefault();}});
    canvasComparePercent=50;setCanvasComparePosition(50);
    attachCompareFrameLoop();
  }
  function renderEnhanceSettings(){
    const host=$('evltEnhancePanel');if(!host)return;const target=getEnhanceTarget();if(!target){host.innerHTML='<div class="evlt-status">Select a video or image clip first.</div>';return;}
    const amount=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(target.model.enhanceAmount)||0));
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="text-editor-controls evlt-enhance-settings">
      <div class="evlt-field"><div class="evlt-field-head"><label for="evltEnhanceAmount">Enhance amount</label><output id="evltEnhanceAmountValue">${amount}%</output></div><div class="text-range-line"><input id="evltEnhanceAmount" type="range" min="0" max="100" step="1" value="${amount}" oninput="window.ethvelleSetEnhanceAmount(this.value);document.getElementById('evltEnhanceAmountValue').textContent=this.value+'%'" aria-label="Enhance amount"></div></div>
      <div class="evlt-field"><div class="evlt-field-head"><label for="evltEnhanceQuality">Output quality</label></div><select id="evltEnhanceQuality"><option value="1080">1080p · Full HD</option><option value="1440">1440p · QHD</option><option value="2160">2160p · Ultra HD</option></select></div>
      <div class="evlt-progress" id="evltEnhanceProgress"><i id="evltEnhanceProgressFill"></i></div><div class="evlt-status" id="evltEnhanceStatus" role="status"></div>
      <div class="evlt-enhance-actions"><button type="button" id="evltEnhanceStart" class="evlt-primary" onclick="window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance()">Enhance</button><button type="button" id="evltEnhanceApply" class="evlt-primary evlt-apply" onclick="window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance()" ${target.model.__evltPendingEnhancedSrc?'':'disabled'}>Apply</button></div>
    </div>`;
    try{const q=Number(target.model.enhancedAssetQuality)||1080;$('evltEnhanceQuality').value=String([1080,1440,2160].includes(q)?q:1080);}catch(_e){}
  }
  const oldSetEnhance=window.ethvelleSetEnhanceAmount;
  if(typeof oldSetEnhance==='function'&&!oldSetEnhance.__evCanvasCompare){const w=function(v){const r=oldSetEnhance.apply(this,arguments);syncCanvasCompare();return r;};w.__evCanvasCompare=true;window.ethvelleSetEnhanceAmount=w;}
  const oldStartEnhance=window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof oldStartEnhance==='function'&&!oldStartEnhance.__evCanvasCompare){const w=async function(){const r=await oldStartEnhance.apply(this,arguments);syncCanvasCompare();return r;};w.__evCanvasCompare=true;window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance=w;}
  function positionEnhancePanel(){

  }

  if(typeof oldClosePanel==='function'&&!oldClosePanel.__evCanvasCompare){const w=function(){const panel=$('toolPanel');if(panel&&panel.classList.contains('evlt-enhance-compact')){try{if(typeof window.__evCancelEnhanceAutoPreview==='function')window.__evCancelEnhanceAutoPreview('panel-close',true,true);}catch(_e){}}const r=oldClosePanel.apply(this,arguments);window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId='';panel?.classList.remove('evlt-enhance-compact');panel?.style.removeProperty('--evlt-enhance-panel-top');removeCanvasCompare();return r;};w.__evCanvasCompare=true;window.closePanel=w;}

  const oldRenderFrame=window.renderFrame;

<script id="ethvelle-v7-device-media-and-thumb-drag-fixes">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(n,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n));
  function toast(msg){try{if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(msg,{duration:2800});else console.info('Ethvelle:',msg);}catch(_e){}}

  // Store long local video files as Blob records, never convert them into enormous Base64 strings.
  let sourceDbPromise=null;
  function openSourceDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.reject(new Error('IndexedDB is not available in this browser view.'));
    if(!sourceDbPromise){
      sourceDbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const req=indexedDB.open('ethvelle-source-media-v1',1);
        req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('assets'))db.createObjectStore('assets');};
        req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not access device media storage.'));
      }).catch(err=>{sourceDbPromise=null;throw err;});
    }
    return sourceDbPromise;
  }
  async function storeVideoFile(file,handle=null){
    const key='media-'+Date.now().toString(36)+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,10);
    const db=await openSourceDb();
    // Keep the selected File as a Blob even when File System Access is supported. File handles
    // can become invalid when the original source is moved/deleted; the Blob is the durable copy
    // that lets the clip survive refresh. The handle remains optional metadata only.
    async function writeRecord(record){await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('assets','readwrite');tx.objectStore('assets').put(record,key);tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Could not save this media to device storage.'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Device storage ran out of space or aborted the save.'));});}
    if(navigator.storage&&typeof navigator.storage.estimate==='function'){
      try{const estimate=await navigator.storage.estimate(),remaining=Number(estimate.quota)-Number(estimate.usage||0);if(Number.isFinite(remaining)&&file.size>Math.max(0,remaining-16*1024*1024))throw new Error('Not enough estimated browser storage for this video. Use the direct device-file picker if your browser supports it, or free storage space.');}catch(err){if(err&&/Not enough estimated/.test(err.message||''))throw err;}
    }
    const base={name:file.name,type:file.type||'application/octet-stream',size:file.size,lastModified:file.lastModified||0,savedAt:Date.now()};
    // Do not let the project clip be created until this Blob transaction commits.
    try{await writeRecord({...base,blob:file,handle:handle||null,storageMode:'indexeddb-blob'});}
    catch(err){
      if(!handle)throw err;
      await writeRecord({...base,blob:file,handle:null,storageMode:'indexeddb-blob'});
    }
    return key;
  }
  // Expose the same IndexedDB asset store to the standard Android file-input path too.
  // This keeps image/video/audio overlays as Blob records instead of huge Base64 strings.
  window.__ethvelleStoreMediaFile=(file,handle=null)=>storeVideoFile(file,handle);
  window.__ethvelleDeleteStoredMediaFile=deleteVideoFile;
  async function deleteVideoFile(key){try{const db=await openSourceDb();await new Promise(resolve=>{const tx=db.transaction('assets','readwrite');tx.objectStore('assets').delete(key);tx.oncomplete=tx.onerror=tx.onabort=resolve;});}catch(e){}}
  async function prepareFrames(src,file,key,meta){
    try{
      if(window.__evSourceFrames&&typeof window.__evSourceFrames.prepare==='function'){
        await window.__evSourceFrames.prepare(src,{key,name:file&&file.name,size:file&&file.size,lastModified:file&&file.lastModified,
          duration:Number(meta&&meta.duration)||0,width:Number(meta&&meta.width)||0,height:Number(meta&&meta.height)||0,
          onProgress:p=>{if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportProgress==='function')window.__ethvelleImportProgress(Math.round(24+p*0.70),'Capturing timeline frames… '+Math.round(p)+'%');}});
      }
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle frames before import:',err);}
  }
  async function createFilmstrip(src,file){
    // Metadata only: return as soon as the browser knows duration and aspect ratio.
    // Actual frames are captured later by the single lazy timeline thumbnail decoder.
    return await new Promise(resolve=>{
      const video=document.createElement('video');
      let settled=false,timer=0;
      video.muted=true;video.playsInline=true;video.preload='metadata';
      video.setAttribute('playsinline','');
      if(/^https?:/i.test(String(src||'')))video.crossOrigin='anonymous';
      const finish=()=>{
        if(settled)return;settled=true;clearTimeout(timer);
        const result={frames:[],duration:Number(video.duration)||0,width:Number(video.videoWidth)||0,height:Number(video.videoHeight)||0};
        try{video.pause();video.removeAttribute('src');video.load();}catch(_e){}
        resolve(result);
      };
      video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',finish,{once:true});
      video.addEventListener('error',finish,{once:true});
      timer=setTimeout(finish,15000);
      try{video.src=String(src||'');video.load();}catch(_e){finish();}
    });
  }
  // Prefer a browser-native file handle picker for the normal Clips/Media import path.
  // This grants access to the actual chosen file (where supported) and avoids copying huge
  // videos into IndexedDB. Older Android WebViews use the standard picker + IndexedDB Blob.
  const previousTriggerUpload=window.triggerUpload;
  async function handlePickedWithDeviceAccess(target,appendAfterGroup){
    let handles;
    try{
      handles=await window.showOpenFilePicker({multiple:false,types:[{description:'Common images and videos',accept:{
        'image/jpeg':['.jpg','.jpeg','.jfif'],'image/png':['.png','.apng'],'image/webp':['.webp'],'image/gif':['.gif'],'image/avif':['.avif'],
        'image/bmp':['.bmp','.dib'],'image/tiff':['.tif','.tiff'],'image/svg+xml':['.svg'],'image/heic':['.heic'],'image/heif':['.heif'],'image/vnd.microsoft.icon':['.ico'],'image/jxl':['.jxl'],
        'video/mp4':['.mp4','.m4v','.f4v'],'video/quicktime':['.mov'],'video/webm':['.webm'],'video/x-matroska':['.mkv'],
        'video/x-msvideo':['.avi'],'video/3gpp':['.3gp','.3gpp'],'video/3gpp2':['.3g2'],'video/mpeg':['.mpeg','.mpg','.mpe','.m2v'],'video/ogg':['.ogv'],
        'video/mp2t':['.ts','.mts','.m2ts'],'video/x-ms-wmv':['.wmv'],'video/x-flv':['.flv']
      }}]});
    }catch(err){
      if(err&&err.name==='AbortError')return;
      console.warn('Ethvelle direct device picker unavailable; using normal media picker:',err);
      if(typeof previousTriggerUpload==='function')previousTriggerUpload.call(window,target,appendAfterGroup);
      return;
    }
    const handle=handles&&handles[0];if(!handle)return;
    const file=await handle.getFile();const isVideo=/^video\//i.test(file.type||'')||/\.(mp4|m4v|f4v|mov|webm|mkv|avi|3gp|3gpp|3g2|mpeg|mpg|mpe|m2v|ogv|ts|mts|m2ts|wmv|flv)$/i.test(file.name||'');
    const isImage=/^image\//i.test(file.type||'')||/\.(png|apng|jpe?g|jfif|webp|gif|avif|bmp|dib|heic|heif|tif|tiff|svg|ico|jxl)$/i.test(file.name||'');
    if(!isVideo&&!isImage){toast('Choose an image or video file.');return;}
    if(isVideo&&typeof window.__ethvelleImportBegin==='function')window.__ethvelleImportBegin(file.name,file.size);
    let key=null,src=null,sessionOnly=false;
    try{
      if(isVideo&&typeof window.__ethvelleImportProgress==='function')window.__ethvelleImportProgress(12,'Linking the original video file…');
      try{key=await window.__ethvelleStoreMediaFile(file,handle);}catch(storageError){
        console.warn('Ethvelle device persistence unavailable; using a safe project/session fallback:',storageError);
        if(file.size<=3*1024*1024){
          src=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=()=>resolve(String(reader.result||''));reader.onerror=()=>reject(reader.error||new Error('Could not read the selected media.'));reader.readAsDataURL(file);});
        }else{src=URL.createObjectURL(file);sessionOnly=true;}
      }
      if(!src)src=URL.createObjectURL(file);
      if(isVideo){
        if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportProgress==='function')window.__ethvelleImportProgress(23,'Preparing the timeline preview…');
        const tr=await createFilmstrip(src,file),duration=Number(tr&&tr.duration)||0;
        await prepareFrames(src,file,key,tr);
        if(typeof addMediaOverlay!=='function')throw new Error('The timeline is unavailable.');
        if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportProgress==='function')window.__ethvelleImportProgress(96,'Adding the clip to your timeline…');
        addMediaOverlay({type:'video',src,name:file.name,duration:duration||12,sourceDuration:duration||12,sourceWidth:tr&&tr.width,sourceHeight:tr&&tr.height,thumbs:[],peaks:[],appendAfterGroup:appendAfterGroup||null,deviceMediaKey:key,deviceMediaInfo:{name:file.name,size:file.size,lastModified:file.lastModified}});
        if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportComplete==='function')window.__ethvelleImportComplete();
        if(sessionOnly||!key)toast(sessionOnly?'Video added for this session only. Export a backup before leaving.':'Video added to project data. Keep an exported backup.');
      }else{
        if(typeof addMediaOverlay!=='function')throw new Error('The timeline is unavailable.');
        addMediaOverlay({type:'image',src,name:file.name,duration:5,thumbs:[],peaks:[],appendAfterGroup:appendAfterGroup||null,deviceMediaKey:key,deviceMediaInfo:{name:file.name,size:file.size,lastModified:file.lastModified}});
        if(sessionOnly||!key)toast(sessionOnly?'Image added for this session only. Export a backup before leaving.':'Image added to project data. Keep an exported backup.');
      }
      try{if(typeof closePanel==='function')closePanel();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(typeof window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow==='function')await window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow();}catch(err){console.warn('Device project snapshot save:',err);}
    }catch(err){
      if(key)await deleteVideoFile(key);if(src){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(src);}catch(_e){}}
      if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportFail==='function')window.__ethvelleImportFail(err&&err.message?err.message:'Could not import this media file.');
      console.error('Ethvelle direct device import:',err);
      toast(err&&err.message?err.message:'Could not import this media file.');
    }
  }
  if(typeof previousTriggerUpload==='function'&&!previousTriggerUpload.__evV7DevicePicker){
    const wrappedTriggerUpload=function(target,appendAfterGroup){
      const t=String(target||'');
      try{uploadTarget=t;uploadAppendGroup=appendAfterGroup||null;}catch(_e){}
      if(['overlay','media'].includes(t)&&typeof window.showOpenFilePicker==='function'){
        // The native picker API must be invoked synchronously from the user's button gesture.
        handlePickedWithDeviceAccess(t,appendAfterGroup).catch(err=>{console.error(err);toast('Could not open the device file picker.');});
        return;
      }
      return previousTriggerUpload.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrappedTriggerUpload.__evV7DevicePicker=true;window.triggerUpload=wrappedTriggerUpload;
  }

  const input=$('fileInput');
  if(input)input.addEventListener('change',function(e){
    const file=input.files&&input.files[0];if(!file||(!/^video\//i.test(file.type||'')&&!/\.(mp4|m4v|f4v|mov|webm|mkv|avi|3gp|3gpp|3g2|mpeg|mpg|mpe|m2v|ogv|ts|mts|m2ts|wmv|flv)$/i.test(file.name||'')))return;
    let target='';try{target=String(uploadTarget||'');}catch(_e){}
    if(!['overlay','media','replace-overlay'].includes(target))return;
    // Prevent the old bubbling listener from FileReader-reading the whole video as Base64.
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    const appendGroup=typeof uploadAppendGroup!=='undefined'?uploadAppendGroup:null;
    const replaceId=typeof replaceTargetId!=='undefined'?replaceTargetId:null;
    input.value='';
    if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportBegin==='function')window.__ethvelleImportBegin(file.name,file.size);
    if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportProgress==='function')window.__ethvelleImportProgress(12,'Saving the original video on this device…');
    (async()=>{
      let key=null,src=null;
      try{
        try{key=await window.__ethvelleStoreMediaFile(file);}catch(storageError){console.warn('Ethvelle video persistence unavailable; adding a session-only clip:',storageError);key=null;}
        src=URL.createObjectURL(file);
        if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportProgress==='function')window.__ethvelleImportProgress(23,'Preparing the timeline preview…');
        const thumbsResult=await createFilmstrip(src,file);
        await prepareFrames(src,file,key,thumbsResult);
        const frames=[];
        const duration=Number(thumbsResult&&thumbsResult.duration)||0;
        if(target==='replace-overlay'){
          const ov=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:[]).find(o=>String(o.id)===String(replaceId));
          if(!ov)throw new Error('The selected clip no longer exists.');
          ov.type='video';ov.src=src;ov.deviceMediaKey=key;ov.deviceMediaName=file.name;ov.deviceMediaSize=Number(file.size)||ov.deviceMediaSize||0;ov.deviceMediaLastModified=Number(file.lastModified)||ov.deviceMediaLastModified||0;ov.name=file.name;ov.sourceDuration=duration||ov.sourceDuration||1;ov.sourceWidth=Number(thumbsResult&&thumbsResult.width)||ov.sourceWidth||0;ov.sourceHeight=Number(thumbsResult&&thumbsResult.height)||ov.sourceHeight||0;ov.thumbs=[];ov.peaks=[];
          if(!ov.transform)ov.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
          try{if(typeof renderOverlayLayers==='function')renderOverlayLayers();if(typeof renderOverlayListPanel==='function')renderOverlayListPanel();if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(err){throw err;}
          if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportProgress==='function')window.__ethvelleImportProgress(96,'Updating the selected timeline clip…');
          if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportComplete==='function')window.__ethvelleImportComplete();
          if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(key?'Video replaced and saved on this device':'Video replaced for this session. Export a backup before leaving.',{duration:2800});
        }else{
          if(typeof addMediaOverlay!=='function')throw new Error('The media timeline is unavailable.');
          if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportProgress==='function')window.__ethvelleImportProgress(96,'Adding the clip to your timeline…');
          addMediaOverlay({type:'video',src,name:file.name,duration:duration||12,sourceDuration:duration||12,sourceWidth:thumbsResult&&thumbsResult.width,sourceHeight:thumbsResult&&thumbsResult.height,thumbs:[],peaks:[],appendAfterGroup:appendGroup,deviceMediaKey:key,deviceMediaInfo:{name:file.name,size:file.size,lastModified:file.lastModified}});
          if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportComplete==='function')window.__ethvelleImportComplete();
          if(!key&&typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Video added for this session. Export a backup before leaving.',{duration:2800});
        }
        if(typeof closePanel==='function')closePanel();
      }catch(err){
        const attached=!!(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)&&overlays.some(o=>o&&((key&&String(o.deviceMediaKey||'')===String(key))||(src&&String(o.src||'')===String(src)))));
        if(!attached){if(key)await deleteVideoFile(key);if(src){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(src);}catch(_e){}}}
        if(typeof window.__ethvelleImportFail==='function')window.__ethvelleImportFail(err&&err.message?err.message:'Could not add this video.');
        console.error('Ethvelle long-video import:',err);
        const msg=file.size>25*1024*1024?'This browser view could not save the large video in device storage. Open the HTML in a supported browser or free storage space, then retry.':(err&&err.message?err.message:'Could not add this video.');
        if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(msg,{duration:3500});
      }finally{try{if(typeof uploadAppendGroup!=='undefined')uploadAppendGroup=null;}catch(_e){}try{if(typeof replaceTargetId!=='undefined'&&target==='replace-overlay')replaceTargetId=null;}catch(_e){}}
    })();
  },true);

  // Slider input is guarded by the single shared thumb-only handler earlier in this file.
})();

<!-- Ethvelle V9/V230 repair: short clips eager once; long clips lazy/windowed; persisted frame cache; text loading + scrub-lag fixes. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v9-filmstrip-ratio-slider-css">
  /* Video strips are position-addressed tiles: one tile is lane-height x source aspect.
     The parent remains a neutral placeholder until the sequential decoder paints a frame. */
  #timelineInner .clip-thumb-strip.ev-video-thumbs{
    position:absolute!important;inset:0!important;width:100%!important;height:100%!important;
    display:block!important;overflow:hidden!important;white-space:normal!important;gap:0!important;
    background:#383943!important;background-image:none!important;pointer-events:none!important;
    contain:paint!important;
  }
  #timelineInner .ev-video-thumb-tile{
    position:absolute!important;left:0;top:0;bottom:0;height:100%!important;min-width:0!important;
    max-width:none!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;
    display:block!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;overflow:hidden!important;

  }

  /* v231: capped eager tiles wider than their natural aspect use cover to avoid gaps. */
  .ev-video-thumbs-wide .ev-video-thumb-tile{background-size:cover!important;}
  /* v231: match the base #timelineInner id specificity so cover wins over the stretch rule. */
  #timelineInner .ev-video-thumbs-wide .ev-video-thumb-tile{background-size:cover!important;}
  input[type="range"]{touch-action:none!important;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent!important;}
