<script id="ethvelle-v9-filmstrip-ratio-slider-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  /* v231: retain enough decoded frames for eager strips without evicting visible tiles. */
  const CACHE_LIMIT=900,DOM_TILE_LIMIT=60,DEFAULT_ASPECT=16/9;
  /* v231: every trimmed source span up to five minutes is eager, capped at 240 natural tiles. */
  const SHORT_VIDEO_EAGER_MAX_SOURCE_SEC=300,EAGER_MAX_TILES=240;
  const cache=new Map();                 // LRU object-URL cache: src|source-time -> {url,blob}
  const pending=new Map();               // coalesced seek/capture jobs
  const queue=[];                        // one sequential seek queue for every source
  const sourceMeta=new Map();
  const primed=new Set();
  let activeVideo=null,activeVideoSrc='',workerRunning=false;
  let scheduleTimer=0,scheduleRaf=0,retryTimer=0;
  // Keep the LRU small, but persist completed JPEG blobs so moving a clip or scrolling
  // away and back never decodes an already-captured source-time again in this session.
  let frameDbPromise=null;
  function openFrameDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.resolve(null);
    if(!frameDbPromise){
      frameDbPromise=new Promise(resolve=>{
        let req;
        try{req=indexedDB.open('ethvelle-filmstrip-frames-v1',1);}catch(_e){resolve(null);return;}
        req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('frames'))db.createObjectStore('frames');};
        req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);
        req.onerror=()=>resolve(null);
        req.onblocked=()=>resolve(null);
      });
    }
    return frameDbPromise;
  }
  async function readSavedFrame(key){
    try{
      const db=await openFrameDb();if(!db)return null;
      return await new Promise(resolve=>{
        const tx=db.transaction('frames','readonly'),req=tx.objectStore('frames').get(key);
        req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result instanceof Blob?req.result:null);
        req.onerror=()=>resolve(null);
        tx.onabort=()=>resolve(null);
      });
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  async function saveFrameBlob(key,blob){
    try{
      const db=await openFrameDb();if(!db||!blob)return;
      await new Promise(resolve=>{
        const tx=db.transaction('frames','readwrite');
        tx.objectStore('frames').put(blob,key);
        tx.oncomplete=tx.onerror=tx.onabort=()=>resolve();
      });
    }catch(_e){}
  }

  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  const delay=ms=>new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,ms));
  function sourceOf(ov){
    let src=String(ov&&ov.src||'').trim();
    const m=src.match(/^url\((.*)\)$/i);
    if(m)src=m[1].trim().replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g,'');
    return src;
  }
  function cacheKey(src,t){return src+'|'+(Math.round(Number(t)*1000)/1000).toFixed(3);}
  function cached(key){
    const hit=cache.get(key);if(!hit)return null;
    cache.delete(key);cache.set(key,hit);return hit;
  }
  function cachePut(key,blob){
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    cache.set(key,{url,blob});
    while(cache.size>CACHE_LIMIT){
      const first=cache.keys().next().value,old=cache.get(first);cache.delete(first);
      try{if(old&&old.url)URL.revokeObjectURL(old.url);}catch(_e){}
    }
    return cache.get(key);
  }
  function paintNode(node,key,entry){
    if(!node||!node.isConnected||node.dataset.evThumbKey!==key||!entry)return;
    node.style.backgroundImage='url("'+entry.url.replace(/"/g,'%22')+'")';
    node.classList.add('ev-video-thumb-ready');
  }
  function teardownVideo(){
    const v=activeVideo;activeVideo=null;activeVideoSrc='';
    if(!v)return;
    try{v.pause();v.removeAttribute('src');v.load();}catch(_e){}
    try{v.remove();}catch(_e){}
  }
  function loadedEvent(video,eventName,timeout){
    return new Promise(resolve=>{
      let settled=false,timer=0;
      const finish=ok=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;clearTimeout(timer);video.removeEventListener(eventName,onEvent);video.removeEventListener('error',onError);resolve(ok);};
      const onEvent=()=>finish(true),onError=()=>finish(false);
      video.addEventListener(eventName,onEvent,{once:true});video.addEventListener('error',onError,{once:true});
      timer=setTimeout(()=>finish(false),timeout);
      if((eventName==='loadedmetadata'&&video.readyState>=1)||(eventName==='loadeddata'&&video.readyState>=2))finish(true);
    });
  }
  function externalDecoderCount(){
    let count=0;
    document.querySelectorAll('video').forEach(v=>{
      if(v===activeVideo)return;
      try{if((v.currentSrc||v.src)&&v.readyState>0&&!v.error)count++;}catch(_e){}
    });
    return count;
  }
  function canCreateDecoder(){
    const budget=Number(window.__evDecoderBudget&&window.__evDecoderBudget.pool)||3;
    return externalDecoderCount()<Math.max(1,budget);
  }
  function applyMetadata(src,video){
    const width=Number(video.videoWidth)||0,height=Number(video.videoHeight)||0,duration=Number(video.duration)||0;
    if(width>0&&height>0){
      const prev=sourceMeta.get(src),next={width,height,duration,aspect:width/height};
      sourceMeta.set(src,next);
      let changed=!prev||Math.abs(prev.aspect-next.aspect)>.002;
      try{
        (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(ov=>{
          if(!ov||ov.type!=='video'||sourceOf(ov)!==src)return;
          if(Number(ov.sourceWidth)!==width||Number(ov.sourceHeight)!==height){ov.sourceWidth=width;ov.sourceHeight=height;changed=true;}
          if(!(Number(ov.sourceDuration)>0)&&duration>0)ov.sourceDuration=duration;
        });
      }catch(_e){}
      /* v231: use the eager path immediately after the real aspect ratio arrives. */
      if(changed)schedule(true);
    }
  }
  async function ensureSourceVideo(src,force){
    if(activeVideo&&activeVideoSrc===src&&activeVideo.readyState>=1&&!activeVideo.error)return activeVideo;
    if(activeVideo)teardownVideo();
    if(!force&&!canCreateDecoder())return null;
    const video=document.createElement('video');
    video.muted=true;video.defaultMuted=true;video.volume=0;video.playsInline=true;video.preload='auto';
    video.setAttribute('muted','');video.setAttribute('playsinline','');video.setAttribute('disablepictureinpicture','');
    video.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');video.tabIndex=-1;
    video.style.cssText='position:fixed!important;left:-10000px!important;top:0!important;width:160px!important;height:90px!important;opacity:.001!important;pointer-events:none!important;z-index:-1!important;contain:strict!important;';
    if(/^https?:/i.test(src))video.crossOrigin='anonymous';
    activeVideo=video;activeVideoSrc=src;
    document.body.appendChild(video);
    const meta=loadedEvent(video,'loadedmetadata',15000);
    try{video.src=src;video.load();}catch(_e){teardownVideo();return null;}
    if(!await meta||!video.videoWidth||!video.videoHeight){if(activeVideo===video)teardownVideo();return null;}
    applyMetadata(src,video);
    if(video.readyState<2){
      const data=await loadedEvent(video,'loadeddata',12000);
      if(!data||activeVideo!==video){if(activeVideo===video)teardownVideo();return null;}
    }
    return activeVideo===video?video:null;
  }
  function waitPaint(video){
    return new Promise(resolve=>{
      let settled=false,timer=0;
      const finish=ok=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;clearTimeout(timer);resolve(ok);};
      timer=setTimeout(()=>finish(video.readyState>=2&&activeVideo===video),420);
      if(typeof video.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function'){
        try{video.requestVideoFrameCallback(()=>finish(video.readyState>=2&&activeVideo===video));}catch(_e){}
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>finish(video.readyState>=2&&activeVideo===video)));
    });
  }
  async function seekVideo(video,wanted,attempt){
    if(!video||activeVideo!==video||video.readyState<2)return false;
    const target=clamp(wanted,0,Math.max(0,(Number(video.duration)||.1)-.045));
    if(!video.seeking&&Math.abs((Number(video.currentTime)||0)-target)<.012){
      if(await waitPaint(video))return true;
    }else{
      const ok=await new Promise(resolve=>{
        let settled=false,guard=0,timer=0;
        const finish=value=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;clearInterval(guard);clearTimeout(timer);video.removeEventListener('seeked',onSeek);video.removeEventListener('error',onError);resolve(value);};
        const onSeek=()=>finish(video.readyState>=2&&activeVideo===video&&Math.abs(video.currentTime-target)<.18);
        const onError=()=>finish(false);
        video.addEventListener('seeked',onSeek,{once:true});video.addEventListener('error',onError,{once:true});
        try{video.currentTime=target;}catch(_e){finish(false);return;}
        guard=setInterval(()=>{if(activeVideo!==video)finish(false);else if(video.readyState>=2&&!video.seeking&&Math.abs(video.currentTime-target)<.10)finish(true);},65);
        timer=setTimeout(()=>finish(video.readyState>=2&&!video.seeking&&Math.abs(video.currentTime-target)<.20),attempt===0?1700:1300);
      });
      if(!ok)return false;
      if(await waitPaint(video))return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function isBlackFrame(video){
    try{
      const probe=document.createElement('canvas');probe.width=16;probe.height=9;
      const ctx=probe.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});if(!ctx)return false;
      ctx.drawImage(video,0,0,video.videoWidth,video.videoHeight,0,0,16,9);
      const pixels=ctx.getImageData(0,0,16,9).data;let max=0,sum=0;
      for(let i=0;i<pixels.length;i+=4){const m=Math.max(pixels[i],pixels[i+1],pixels[i+2]);max=Math.max(max,m);sum+=m;}
      return max<12||sum/(16*9)<3.5;
    }catch(_e){return true;}
  }
  async function captureBlob(video,targetH,quality){
    if(!video||activeVideo!==video||!video.videoWidth||!video.videoHeight)return null;
    const sw=video.videoWidth,sh=video.videoHeight,aspect=sw/sh;
    const h=Math.max(16,Math.round(Number(targetH)||144)),w=Math.max(1,Math.round(h*aspect));
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});if(!ctx)return null;
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
    try{ctx.drawImage(video,0,0,sw,sh,0,0,w,h);}catch(_e){return null;}
    return await new Promise(resolve=>{
      try{canvas.toBlob(blob=>resolve(blob&&blob.size?blob:null),'image/jpeg',Number(quality)||.8);}catch(_e){resolve(null);}
    });
  }
  async function decodeFrame(src,time){
    const video=await ensureSourceVideo(src);if(!video)return null;
    let ok=false;
    for(let attempt=0;attempt<2;attempt++){
      if(activeVideo!==video)return null;
      if(await seekVideo(video,time,attempt)){
        if(!isBlackFrame(video)){ok=true;break;}
      }
      if(attempt===0)await delay(110);
    }
    if(!ok)return null;
    return await captureBlob(video);
  }
  /* ===================== v234 source-frame store =====================
     Frames belong to the SOURCE MEDIA (stable id + fixed source-time samples), never to a clip or tile.
     - prepare(): runs BEFORE addMediaOverlay at import (progress callback), persists to IndexedDB.
     - paintAll(): fills every strip synchronously from stored frames (nearest source time). Never decodes.
     - Background fill (sources > 5 min): one shared decoder, per-frame lock, yields to playback. */
  const SF_DB='ethvelle-source-frames-v2',LONG_SOURCE_SEC=300,SHORT_H=216,LONG_H=144,DENSE_MAX=1500;
  const sets=new Map(),aliasMap=new Map(),srcToId=new Map(),loading=new Map(),bgGen=new Set(),filling=new Set();
  const failedSets=new Set();
  const stats={decodes:0,saves:0,paints:0,restored:0};
  let sfDbPromise=null,lockTail=Promise.resolve(),fgBusy=0,restoreDone=false;
  function sfDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.resolve(null);
    if(!sfDbPromise)sfDbPromise=new Promise(resolve=>{
      let req;try{req=indexedDB.open(SF_DB,1);}catch(_e){resolve(null);return;}
      req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('frames'))db.createObjectStore('frames');if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('meta'))db.createObjectStore('meta');};
      req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>resolve(null);req.onblocked=()=>resolve(null);
    });
    return sfDbPromise;
  }
  async function idbGet(store,key){
    try{const db=await sfDb();if(!db)return null;
      return await new Promise(res=>{const r=db.transaction(store,'readonly').objectStore(store).get(key);r.onsuccess=()=>res(r.result||null);r.onerror=()=>res(null);});
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  async function idbPut(store,key,val){
    try{const db=await sfDb();if(!db)return;
      await new Promise(res=>{const tx=db.transaction(store,'readwrite');tx.objectStore(store).put(val,key);tx.oncomplete=tx.onerror=tx.onabort=()=>res();});
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  async function idbFrames(setId){
    try{const db=await sfDb();if(!db)return [];
      return await new Promise(res=>{const r=db.transaction('frames','readonly').objectStore('frames').getAll(IDBKeyRange.bound(setId+'\u0001',setId+'\u0002'));r.onsuccess=()=>res(r.result||[]);r.onerror=()=>res([]);});
    }catch(_e){return [];}
  }
  const fkey=(id,ms)=>id+'\u0001'+ms;
  function withLock(fn){const run=lockTail.then(fn,fn);lockTail=run.catch(()=>{});return run;}
  function fingerprintOf(o){
    const n=o&&(o.deviceMediaName||o.name),s=o&&(o.deviceMediaSize||o.size),m=o&&(o.deviceMediaLastModified||o.lastModified),d=Number(o&&(o.sourceDuration||o.duration))||0;
    return (n&&s)?'f:'+n+'|'+s+'|'+(m||0)+'|'+d.toFixed(2):'';
  }
  function idOf(ov){
    if(ov.deviceMediaKey)return 'k:'+ov.deviceMediaKey;
    const src=sourceOf(ov);if(srcToId.has(src))return srcToId.get(src);
    const fp=fingerprintOf(ov);if(fp)return fp;
    return 'n:'+(ov.name||'')+'|'+(Number(ov.sourceDuration)||0).toFixed(2)+'|'+(ov.sourceWidth||0)+'x'+(ov.sourceHeight||0);
  }
  const resolveId=id=>aliasMap.get(id)||id;
  const setFor=ov=>sets.get(resolveId(idOf(ov)))||null;
  function newSet(meta){
    return {id:meta.id,duration:Number(meta.duration)||0,width:Number(meta.width)||0,height:Number(meta.height)||0,
      aspect:Number(meta.aspect)||0,mode:meta.mode==='long'?'long':'short',complete:!!meta.complete,fillDone:!!meta.fillDone,
      times:[],urls:new Map(),imgs:[],saving:0,fillRetryAt:0};
  }
  function addFrame(set,ms,blob){
    if(set.urls.has(ms)||!blob)return false;
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);set.urls.set(ms,url);
    const a=set.times;let lo=0,hi=a.length;while(lo<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(a[mid]<ms)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid;}a.splice(lo,0,ms);
    try{const img=new Image();img.decoding='async';img.src=url;if(img.decode)img.decode().catch(()=>{});set.imgs.push(img);}catch(_e){}
    return true;
  }
  function nearest(set,t){
    const a=set.times,n=a.length;if(!n)return '';const ms=t*1000;
    let lo=0,hi=n-1;while(lo<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(a[mid]<ms)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid;}
    if(lo>0&&Math.abs(a[lo-1]-ms)<=Math.abs(a[lo]-ms))lo--;
    return set.urls.get(a[lo])||'';
  }
  function saveMeta(set){
    return idPutMeta(set);
  }
  function idPutMeta(set){
    return idbPut('meta',set.id,{id:set.id,duration:set.duration,width:set.width,height:set.height,aspect:set.aspect,mode:set.mode,
      complete:set.complete,fillDone:set.fillDone,count:set.times.length,updated:Date.now()});
  }
  async function loadSet(id){
    const rid=resolveId(id);if(sets.has(rid))return sets.get(rid);
    if(loading.has(id))return loading.get(id);
    const job=(async()=>{
      let setId=id;
      const al=await idbGet('meta','alias|'+id);if(al&&al.target){setId=String(al.target);aliasMap.set(id,setId);}
      if(sets.has(setId))return sets.get(setId);
      const meta=await idbGet('meta',setId);if(!meta||!meta.id)return null;
      const set=newSet(meta);
      const rows=await idbFrames(setId);
      rows.forEach(r=>{if(r&&r.blob&&Number.isFinite(r.ms))addFrame(set,r.ms,r.blob);});
      if(!set.times.length)return null;
      sets.set(setId,set);stats.restored++;return set;
    })().finally(()=>loading.delete(id));
    loading.set(id,job);return job;
  }
  async function restoreForProjects(projects){
    const ids=new Set(),walk=ovs=>{(Array.isArray(ovs)?ovs:[]).forEach(o=>{if(o&&o.type==='video'&&(o.deviceMediaKey||o.src))ids.add(idOf(o));});};
    try{(Array.isArray(projects)?projects:[]).forEach(p=>{if(p&&p.savedState)walk(p.savedState.overlays);});}catch(_e){}
    try{walk(overlays);}catch(_e){}
    await Promise.race([Promise.all([...ids].map(id=>loadSet(id).catch(()=>null))),delay(6000)]);
    restoreDone=true;
  }
  /* ---- decoding (single shared decoder; every real seek is counted in stats.decodes) ---- */
  async function grab(src,t,h,q){
    const video=await ensureSourceVideo(src,true);if(!video)return null;
    for(let attempt=0;attempt<2;attempt++){
      if(activeVideo!==video)return null;
      stats.decodes++;
      if(await seekVideo(video,t,attempt)){if(!isBlackFrame(video))return await captureBlob(video,h,q);}
      if(attempt===0)await delay(110);
    }
    return null;
  }
  function playbackActive(){
    let busy=false;
    document.querySelectorAll('video').forEach(v=>{if(v!==activeVideo&&!v.paused&&!v.ended&&v.readyState>=2)busy=true;});
    return busy;
  }
  async function idleWait(){while(playbackActive()||document.hidden||fgBusy>0)await delay(500);}
  const msList=(span,n,from)=>{const o=[];for(let i=0;i<n;i++)o.push(Math.round(1000*((from||0)+(n===1?0:span*i/(n-1)))));return o;};
  function planShort(dur){const n=Math.round(clamp(Math.ceil(dur*3),6,60));return msList(Math.max(.08,dur-.08),n,0);}
  function planCoarse(dur){return msList(Math.max(.08,dur-.08),24,0);}
  function denseStep(dur){return Math.max(2,dur/DENSE_MAX);}
  function planDense(dur){const st=denseStep(dur),o=[];for(let t=0;t<dur-.08;t+=st)o.push(Math.round(t*1000));return o;}
  async function persistFrame(set,ms,blob){stats.saves++;await idbPut('frames',fkey(set.id,ms),{ms,blob});}
  /* Runs a capture plan. fg=true holds the decoder for the whole run (import); fg=false takes the lock per frame and yields to playback. */
  async function runPlan(set,src,times,opt){
    const h=opt.h,q=opt.q,total=times.length;let done=0,okCount=0;const retry=[];
    const queue=times.filter(ms=>!set.urls.has(ms));const skipped=total-queue.length;done=skipped;
    const step=async ms=>{
      if(!opt.fg)await idleWait();
      const blob=opt.fg?await grab(src,ms/1000,h,q):await withLock(()=>grab(src,ms/1000,h,q));
      if(blob){if(addFrame(set,ms,blob)){await persistFrame(set,ms,blob);okCount++;}}else return false;
      return true;
    };
    let failStreak=0;
    for(let i=0;i<queue.length;i++){
      if(opt.deadline&&Date.now()>opt.deadline)break;
      if(opt.shouldStop&&opt.shouldStop())break;
      const ok=await step(queue[i]);
      if(!ok){retry.push(queue[i]);failStreak++;}else failStreak=0;
      done++;
      try{if(opt.onProgress)opt.onProgress(Math.min(100,done/Math.max(1,total)*100));}catch(_e){}
      if(opt.onBatch&&okCount&&okCount%25===0)opt.onBatch();
      if(failStreak>=6&&!opt.fg){opt.aborted=true;break;}
      await delay(opt.fg?0:40);
    }
    if(!opt.aborted&&retry.length&&!(opt.deadline&&Date.now()>opt.deadline)){
      for(const ms of retry){if(opt.shouldStop&&opt.shouldStop())break;await step(ms);}
    }
    return okCount;
  }
  function liveSrcFor(set){
    try{const o=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(x=>x&&x.type==='video'&&resolveId(idOf(x))===set.id&&sourceOf(x)&&sourceOf(x).indexOf('ethvelle-device-media://')!==0);return o?sourceOf(o):'';}catch(_e){return '';}
  }
  function usedWindows(set){
    const w=[];try{(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(o=>{if(o&&o.type==='video'&&resolveId(idOf(o))===set.id){
      const sp=Math.max(0,((Number(o.trimEnd)||0)-(Number(o.trimStart)||0))*(Math.max(.05,Number(o.speed)||1)));const s=Math.max(0,Number(o.sourceOffset)||0);w.push([s,s+sp]);}});}catch(_e){}
    return w.length?w:[[0,0]];
  }
  async function ensureFill(set){
    if(!set||set.fillDone||filling.has(set.id)||Date.now()<set.fillRetryAt||set.mode!=='long')return;
    filling.add(set.id);
    try{
      const src=liveSrcFor(set);if(!src)return;
      const win=usedWindows(set),dist=ms=>{const t=ms/1000;let d=1e9;win.forEach(([a,b])=>{d=Math.min(d,t<a?a-t:t>b?t-b:0);});return d;};
      const times=planDense(set.duration).filter(ms=>!set.urls.has(ms)).sort((a,b)=>dist(a)-dist(b));
      const opt={fg:false,h:LONG_H,q:.7,shouldStop:()=>!liveSrcFor(set),onBatch:()=>{idPutMeta(set);schedule();}};
      await runPlan(set,src,times,opt);
      if(opt.aborted){set.fillRetryAt=Date.now()+30000;}
      else if(liveSrcFor(set)){set.fillDone=true;}
      await idPutMeta(set);
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle background frame fill:',err);set.fillRetryAt=Date.now()+30000;}
    finally{filling.delete(set.id);try{if(!playbackActive())teardownVideo();}catch(_e){}schedule();}
  }
  /* Import-time preparation. Resolves (never rejects) once the first strip is ready. */
  async function prepare(src,info){
    info=info||{};
    const clean=sourceOf({src}),fp=fingerprintOf(info),key=info.key?'k:'+info.key:'';
    const id=key||fp||'s:'+clean;
    srcToId.set(clean,id);
    try{
      let existing=await loadSet(id);
      if(!existing&&fp&&fp!==id)existing=await loadSet(fp);
      if(existing&&existing.complete){
        if(existing.id!==id){aliasMap.set(id,existing.id);await idbPut('meta','alias|'+id,{target:existing.id});}
        if(info.onProgress)try{info.onProgress(100);}catch(_e){}
        schedule(true);
        return {id:existing.id,reused:true,frames:existing.times.length};
      }
      if(failedSets.has(id)&&info.background)return {id,failed:true};
      const bg=!!info.background;
      if(!bg)fgBusy++;
      let set=existing||null;
      try{
        const probe=await withLock(async()=>{const v=await ensureSourceVideo(clean,true);return v?{d:Number(v.duration)||0,w:v.videoWidth,h:v.videoHeight}:null;});
        if(!probe){failedSets.add(id);return {id,failed:true};}
        const dur=probe.d>0?probe.d:(Number(info.duration)||1),w=probe.w||Number(info.width)||0,h=probe.h||Number(info.height)||0;
        const mode=dur>LONG_SOURCE_SEC?'long':'short';
        if(!set){set=newSet({id,duration:dur,width:w,height:h,aspect:w>0&&h>0?w/h:16/9,mode,complete:false,fillDone:mode==='short'});sets.set(id,set);}
        const started=Date.now();
        let times,deadline=0,fg=!bg;
        if(mode==='short'){times=planShort(dur);}
        else{
          const dense=planDense(dur).filter(ms=>ms<=22000);
          times=planCoarse(dur).concat(dense);deadline=bg?0:started+7000;
        }
        const ok=await (fg?withLock(()=>runPlan(set,clean,times,{fg:true,h:mode==='short'?SHORT_H:LONG_H,q:mode==='short'?.86:.72,deadline,onProgress:info.onProgress,onBatch:()=>idPutMeta(set)}))
                          :runPlan(set,clean,times,{fg:false,h:mode==='short'?SHORT_H:LONG_H,q:mode==='short'?.86:.72,deadline,onProgress:info.onProgress,onBatch:()=>idPutMeta(set)}));
        if(!set.times.length){sets.delete(id);failedSets.add(id);return {id,failed:true};}
        set.complete=true;if(mode==='short')set.fillDone=true;
        await idPutMeta(set);
        if(fp&&fp!==id)await idbPut('meta','alias|'+fp,{target:id});
        if(key)await idbPut('meta','alias|'+key,{target:id});
        if(info.onProgress)try{info.onProgress(100);}catch(_e){}
        return {id,reused:false,frames:set.times.length,mode};
      }finally{
        if(!bg)fgBusy--;
        try{if(!playbackActive())teardownVideo();}catch(_e){}
        schedule(true);
        if(set&&set.mode==='long'&&!set.fillDone)setTimeout(()=>ensureFill(set),800);
      }
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle frame preparation:',err);failedSets.add(id);return {id,failed:true};}
  }
  function ensureSetFor(ov,src){
    const id=idOf(ov);if(failedSets.has(id)||bgGen.has(id)||sets.has(resolveId(id)))return;
    bgGen.add(id);
    (async()=>{
      try{
        const found=await loadSet(id);
        if(found){if(found.mode==='long'&&!found.fillDone)ensureFill(found);return;}
        if(!restoreDone)await Promise.race([window.__ethvelleProjectsRestorePromise||Promise.resolve(),delay(4000)]);
        if(sets.has(resolveId(id)))return;
        await prepare(src,{key:ov.deviceMediaKey,name:ov.deviceMediaName||ov.name,size:ov.deviceMediaSize,lastModified:ov.deviceMediaLastModified,
          duration:ov.sourceDuration,width:ov.sourceWidth,height:ov.sourceHeight,background:true});
      }catch(_e){}finally{bgGen.delete(id);schedule(true);}
    })();
  }
  /* ---- synchronous painter (no decode, no debounce, no placeholder-then-fill) ---- */
  let hasLong=false,painting=false,paintRaf=0;
  function paintAll(){
    if(painting)return;painting=true;
    try{
      const inner=document.getElementById('timelineInner');if(!inner)return;
      const scroller=document.getElementById('timelineScroll');
      const dragging=inner.classList.contains('clip-drag-active');
      const pps=Math.max(1,Number(typeof window.pxPerSecond==='function'?window.pxPerSecond():90)||90);
      const sr=scroller?scroller.getBoundingClientRect():{left:0,right:window.innerWidth||360};
      const screenW=Math.max(1,(scroller&&scroller.clientWidth)||window.innerWidth||360);
      const expandedLeft=sr.left-screenW,expandedRight=sr.left+screenW*2,viewportCenter=sr.left+screenW/2;
      const clips=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.filter(ov=>ov&&ov.type==='video'&&ov.src):[];
      const recs=[],frozen=new Set();let anyLong=false;
      clips.forEach(ov=>{
        const id=String(ov.id),safeId=id.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"');
        const clipEl=inner.querySelector('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id="'+safeId+'"]');if(!clipEl)return;
        const strip=clipEl.querySelector('.ev-video-thumbs');if(!strip)return;
        const src=sourceOf(ov);if(!src||src.indexOf('ethvelle-device-media://')===0)return;
        const set=setFor(ov);
        if(!set||!set.times.length){ensureSetFor(ov,src);return;}
        if(set.mode==='long'&&!set.fillDone)ensureFill(set);
        // A normal clip move keeps its existing filmstrip frozen to avoid spending work
        // repainting tiles while the whole clip is being repositioned. A trim/extend is
        // different: its source-time window and visible width are changing under the finger,
        // so let the already-decoded frame tiles repaint in real time for this clip only.
        const trimPreviewActive=clipEl.classList.contains('ev-trim-preview-active');
        if(dragging&&strip.firstChild&&!trimPreviewActive){frozen.add(strip);return;}
        const cr=clipEl.getBoundingClientRect();
        const sw=Number(ov.sourceWidth)||set.width||0,sh=Number(ov.sourceHeight)||set.height||0;
        const aspect=sw>0&&sh>0?sw/sh:(set.aspect||DEFAULT_ASPECT);
        const laneH=Math.max(1,strip.clientHeight||clipEl.clientHeight||30);
        const timeStart=Number(ov.trimStart)||0,timeEnd=Math.max(timeStart,Number(ov.trimEnd)||timeStart);
        const speed=Math.max(.05,Number(ov.speed)||1),srcOffset=Math.max(0,Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0);
        const sourceSpan=Math.max(0,(timeEnd-timeStart)*speed);
        const knownDuration=Number(ov.sourceDuration)||set.duration||0;
        const isShort=sourceSpan<=SHORT_VIDEO_EAGER_MAX_SOURCE_SEC;if(!isShort)anyLong=true;
        const clipW=Math.max(1,clipEl.clientWidth||cr.width||((timeEnd-timeStart)*pps));
        recs.push({ov,strip,set,cr,aspect,laneH,timeStart,timeEnd,srcOffset,sourceSpan,knownDuration,isShort,clipW});
      });
      hasLong=anyLong;
      const keep=new Map(),writes=[];
      recs.forEach(r=>{
        const {ov,strip,set,cr,aspect,laneH,timeStart,timeEnd,srcOffset,sourceSpan,knownDuration,isShort,clipW}=r;
        const naturalTileW=Math.max(1,laneH*aspect);let tileW=naturalTileW,tileCount=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(clipW/tileW));
        if(isShort&&tileCount>EAGER_MAX_TILES){tileCount=EAGER_MAX_TILES;tileW=clipW/tileCount;}
        strip.classList.toggle('ev-video-thumbs-short',isShort);
        strip.classList.toggle('ev-video-thumbs-wide',tileW>naturalTileW*1.02);
        if(!isShort&&(cr.right<expandedLeft||cr.left>expandedRight)){keep.set(strip,new Set());return;}
        const first=isShort?0:Math.max(0,Math.floor(clamp(expandedLeft-cr.left,0,clipW)/tileW));
        const last=isShort?tileCount-1:Math.min(tileCount-1,Math.ceil(clamp(expandedRight-cr.left,0,clipW)/tileW));
        const timeOf=index=>{
          const fraction=(index*tileW/pps)/Math.max(.0001,timeEnd-timeStart);
          const elapsed=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,fraction))*sourceSpan;
          const raw=ov.reversePlayback?srcOffset+sourceSpan-elapsed:srcOffset+elapsed;
          return clamp(raw,0,knownDuration>0?Math.max(0,knownDuration-.045):Math.max(raw,0));
        };
        let idxs=[];for(let i=first;i<=last;i++)idxs.push(i);
        if(!isShort&&idxs.length>DOM_TILE_LIMIT){
          idxs.sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(cr.left+a*tileW+tileW/2-viewportCenter)-Math.abs(cr.left+b*tileW+tileW/2-viewportCenter));
          idxs=idxs.slice(0,DOM_TILE_LIMIT);
        }
        keep.set(strip,new Set(idxs));
        writes.push({strip,set,idxs,tileW,timeOf});
      });
      inner.querySelectorAll('.ev-video-thumbs').forEach(strip=>{
        if(frozen.has(strip))return;
        const ids=keep.get(strip);if(!ids)return;
        if(strip.__evTiles){strip.__evTiles.forEach((node,index)=>{if(!ids.has(index)){try{node.remove();}catch(_e){}strip.__evTiles.delete(index);}});}
      });
      writes.forEach(w=>{
        const {strip,set,idxs,tileW,timeOf}=w;
        let tiles=strip.__evTiles;if(!tiles||!tiles.size||![...tiles.values()].every(n=>n.parentNode===strip)){strip.textContent='';tiles=new Map();strip.__evTiles=tiles;}
        const frag=document.createDocumentFragment();
        idxs.forEach(index=>{
          let node=tiles.get(index);
          if(!node){node=document.createElement('div');node.className='ev-video-thumb-tile';node.dataset.evThumbIndex=String(index);tiles.set(index,node);frag.appendChild(node);}
          const left=(index*tileW)+'px',width=(tileW+.35)+'px';
          if(node.style.left!==left)node.style.left=left;
          if(node.style.width!==width)node.style.width=width;
          const url=nearest(set,timeOf(index));
          if(url&&node.dataset.evThumbUrl!==url){node.style.backgroundImage='url("'+url+'")';node.dataset.evThumbUrl=url;node.classList.add('ev-video-thumb-ready');}
        });
        if(frag.firstChild)strip.appendChild(frag);
      });
      stats.paints++;
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle frame paint:',err);}
    finally{painting=false;}
  }
  function schedule(immediate){
    if(immediate===true){paintAll();return;}
    if(paintRaf)return;paintRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{paintRaf=0;paintAll();});
  }
  function onScrollPaint(){if(hasLong)schedule();}
  function observeStrips(){
    const host=document.getElementById('timelineScroll')||document.body;
    const isStrip=n=>n&&n.nodeType===1&&(n.classList.contains('ev-video-thumbs')||(n.querySelector&&n.querySelector('.ev-video-thumbs')));
    new MutationObserver(records=>{
      for(const rec of records){for(const n of rec.addedNodes){if(n.nodeType===1&&n.classList.contains('ev-video-thumb-tile'))continue;if(isStrip(n)){paintAll();return;}}}
    }).observe(host,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }

  function wire(){
    const scroller=document.getElementById('timelineScroll');
    if(scroller&&!scroller.dataset.evLazyThumbScroll){scroller.dataset.evLazyThumbScroll='1';scroller.addEventListener('scroll',onScrollPaint,{passive:true});}
    window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
    // Playback owns the active decoder budget. Release our paused thumbnail decoder as soon
    // as another video starts so the app's three-decoder playback pool remains available.
    document.addEventListener('play',event=>{
      if(event.target!==activeVideo){teardownVideo();setTimeout(function(){try{schedule();}catch(_e){}},180);}
    },true);
    // If another part of the editor claims the final decoder slot while a thumbnail
    // source is paused, release the thumbnail decoder as soon as that video has metadata.
    document.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',event=>{
      if(activeVideo&&event.target!==activeVideo){
        const budget=Number(window.__evDecoderBudget&&window.__evDecoderBudget.pool)||3;
        if(externalDecoderCount()>=budget){teardownVideo();setTimeout(function(){try{schedule();}catch(_e){}},180);}
      }
    },true);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden){teardownVideo();}else schedule();});
    window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{teardownVideo();});
    const oldRender=window.renderOverlayLayers;
    if(typeof oldRender==='function'&&!oldRender.__evV9LazyVideoThumbs){
      /* v231: the first render containing a new video id kicks off the eager strip now. */
      const seenVideoOverlayIds=new Set();
      const wrapped=function(){
        const result=oldRender.apply(this,arguments);let hasNewVideo=false;
        try{
          const list=Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]);
          list.forEach(ov=>{if(!ov||ov.type!=='video'||!ov.src)return;const id=String(ov.id);if(!seenVideoOverlayIds.has(id)){seenVideoOverlayIds.add(id);hasNewVideo=true;}});
        }catch(_e){}
        paintAll();
        return result;
      };
      wrapped.__evV9LazyVideoThumbs=true;window.renderOverlayLayers=wrapped;
    }
    observeStrips();
    schedule();
  }
  window.__evSourceFrames={prepare,restoreForProjects,sets,stats,idOf,setFor,ensureFill,paintAll,nearest};
  window.__evTimelineVideoThumbs={schedule,paintAll,stats,sets,debug:()=>{const tiles=[...document.querySelectorAll('.ev-video-thumb-tile')];return {sets:sets.size,frames:[...sets.values()].reduce((n,s)=>n+s.times.length,0),decodes:stats.decodes,saves:stats.saves,paints:stats.paints,domTiles:tiles.length,blankTiles:tiles.filter(n=>!n.classList.contains('ev-video-thumb-ready')).length,decoderAlive:!!activeVideo,src:activeVideoSrc,filling:filling.size};}};
  try{if(window.__evFramesGateResolve)window.__evFramesGateResolve();}catch(_e){}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',wire,{once:true});else wire();
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle V10: hard drag-only range controls. The native input is kept for existing
     application code/value events, but never receives pointer input. A transparent sibling
     intercepts all gestures and changes the value only after a real thumb-originating drag. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v10-drag-only-sliders-css">
  .ev-range-drag-only-wrap {
    position:relative!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;overflow:visible!important;
    align-items:stretch!important;vertical-align:middle!important;isolation:isolate!important;
  }
  .ev-range-drag-only-wrap > input[type="range"] {
    position:relative!important;left:auto!important;right:auto!important;top:auto!important;bottom:auto!important;
    transform:none!important;margin:0!important;width:100%!important;height:100%!important;
    min-width:0!important;max-width:none!important;flex:none!important;pointer-events:none!important;
    touch-action:none!important;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent!important;
  }
  .ev-range-drag-only-surface {
    position:absolute!important;inset:0!important;display:block!important;width:100%!important;height:100%!important;
    margin:0!important;padding:0!important;border:0!important;border-radius:0!important;
    background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;opacity:1!important;visibility:visible!important;
    z-index:2!important;pointer-events:auto!important;touch-action:none!important;
    user-select:none!important;-webkit-user-select:none!important;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent!important;
    cursor:grab!important;outline:none!important;
  }
  .ev-range-drag-only-surface.ev-dragging {cursor:grabbing!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v10-drag-only-sliders-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV10NativeRangeBridge)return;
  window.__ethvelleV10NativeRangeBridge=true;
  function normalize(root){
    if(!root)return;
    const ranges=[];
    if(root.nodeType===1&&root.matches?.('input[type="range"]'))ranges.push(root);
    root.querySelectorAll?.('input[type="range"]').forEach(el=>ranges.push(el));
    ranges.forEach(input=>{
      // v191: text-panel sliders are owned by ethvelle-v191-text-slider-js (row-level drag).
      if(input.closest&&input.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock,#ethvelleTextStylePanel,#ethvelleTextComposer,#evpaCustomEffectEditor,#ethvelleColorPicker,#textPanelBody')){
        input.style.setProperty('pointer-events','none','important');
        input.style.setProperty('touch-action','pan-y','important');
        return;
      }
      // A single capture-phase controller handles thumb drags for every range in the editor.
      // Keeping the real input as the hit target avoids invisible overlays and stale geometry.
      input.style.setProperty('pointer-events','auto','important');
      input.style.setProperty('touch-action','none','important');
      input.style.setProperty('-webkit-tap-highlight-color','transparent','important');
    });
  }
  function boot(){
    normalize(document);
    const observer=new MutationObserver(records=>{
      for(const record of records)for(const node of record.addedNodes||[])if(node.nodeType===1)normalize(node);
    });
    observer.observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    const previous=window.__ethvelleRefreshRangeOverlays;
    window.__ethvelleRefreshRangeOverlays=function(){
      try{if(typeof previous==='function')previous();}catch(_e){}
      normalize(document);
    };
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-export-import-v10-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const backdrop=$('exportModalBackdrop');
  const sheet=backdrop&&backdrop.querySelector('.modal-sheet');

    backdrop.classList.add('evlt-export-fullscreen');

      sheet.insertBefore(head,sheet.firstChild);
      const settings=document.createElement('section');settings.className='evlt-export-settings';settings.id='evltExportAdvancedSettings';
      settings.innerHTML='<h3 class="evlt-export-section-title">Output settings</h3><div class="evlt-export-controls"><div class="evlt-export-field"><label for="evltExportFrameRate">Frame rate</label><select id="evltExportFrameRate"><option value="24">24 fps · Cinematic</option><option value="30" selected>30 fps · Standard</option><option value="60">60 fps · Smooth</option></select></div><div class="evlt-export-field"><label for="evltExportBitrate">Bitrate</label><select id="evltExportBitrate"><option value="recommended" selected>Recommended</option><option value="high">High quality</option></select></div></div>'+
        '<div class="evlt-export-info-card"><div class="evlt-export-info-title">Video details <span>Output estimate</span></div><div class="evlt-export-info-grid"><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Resolution</span><strong id="evltExportResolution">720 × 1280</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Duration</span><strong id="evltExportDuration">0:00</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Frame rate</span><strong id="evltExportFrameRateReadout">30 fps</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Estimated file size</span><strong id="evltExportSize">—</strong></div></div></div>'+
        '<div class="evlt-export-info-card"><div class="evlt-export-info-title">Credits & plan <span>Account</span></div><div class="evlt-export-info-grid"><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Current plan</span><strong id="evltExportPlan">Free</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Available credits</span><strong id="evltExportBalance">—</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Estimated export cost</span><strong class="evlt-info-accent" id="evltExportCost">—</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Monthly exports</span><strong id="evltExportMonthly">—</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Maximum quality on plan</span><strong id="evltExportMaxQuality">720p</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-info-item"><span>Encoding</span><strong id="evltExportFormat">WebM video</strong></div></div></div>';
      const confirm=$('exportConfirmBtn');if(confirm&&confirm.parentNode)confirm.parentNode.insertBefore(settings,confirm);
      const fps=$('evltExportFrameRate');if(fps)fps.addEventListener('change',()=>{window.exportFrameRate=Number(fps.value)||30;refreshExportInfo();});
      const bitrate=$('evltExportBitrate');if(bitrate)bitrate.addEventListener('change',()=>{window.exportBitrateMode=bitrate.value||'recommended';refreshExportInfo();});
    }
  }

  // Keep output controls scrollable while the primary Export button stays attached to
  // a full-width bottom action bar (not floating over the settings content).
  if(sheet&&$('exportConfirmBtn')&&!sheet.querySelector('.evlt-export-body')){
    const body=document.createElement('div');body.className='evlt-export-body';
    const footer=document.createElement('div');footer.className='evlt-export-footer';
    const confirm=$('exportConfirmBtn');
    const protectedNodes=new Set([sheet.querySelector('.evlt-export-head'),confirm,...Array.from(sheet.querySelectorAll('.sheet-handle,.sheet-title,.sheet-sub'))].filter(Boolean));
    Array.from(sheet.children).forEach(child=>{if(!protectedNodes.has(child))body.appendChild(child);});
    footer.appendChild(confirm);sheet.appendChild(body);sheet.appendChild(footer);
  }

  function clamp(v,a,b){return Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));}
  function fmtTime(seconds){seconds=Math.max(0,Math.round(Number(seconds)||0));const m=Math.floor(seconds/60),s=seconds%60;return m+':'+String(s).padStart(2,'0');}
  function effectiveBitrate(){const custom=Number(window.exportBitrateMbps);if(custom>0)return Math.round(custom*1000000);const q=Number(typeof exportQuality!=='undefined'?exportQuality:720);const base=q>=2160?16000000:q>=1440?12000000:q>=1080?8000000:q>=720?5000000:2500000;return Math.round(base*(window.exportBitrateMode==='high'?1.4:1)*(Math.max(24,Math.min(60,Number(window.exportFrameRate)||30))/30));}
  function refreshExportInfo(){
    try{
      const duration=typeof getExportContentDurationSec==='function'?getExportContentDurationSec():0.1;
      const q=Number(typeof exportQuality!=='undefined'?exportQuality:720),fps=Math.max(24,Math.min(60,Number(window.exportFrameRate)||30));
      let dims={w:q,h:Math.round(q*16/9)};try{if(typeof qualityToHeight==='function')dims=qualityToHeight(q);}catch(_e){}
      const set=(id,value)=>{const el=$(id);if(el)el.textContent=value;};
      set('evltExportResolution',`${dims.w} × ${dims.h}`);set('evltExportDuration',fmtTime(duration));set('evltExportFrameRateReadout',fps+' fps');
      const mb=effectiveBitrate()*duration/8/1024/1024;set('evltExportSize',mb>=1024?(mb/1024).toFixed(2)+' GB':Math.max(.1,mb).toFixed(1)+' MB');
      const plan=typeof getMyPlanData==='function'?getMyPlanData():{name:'Free',maxQuality:720};
      const cost=typeof calculateExportCreditCost==='function'?calculateExportCreditCost(q,duration):0;
      const enabled=typeof getCreditSettings==='function'?getCreditSettings().enabled:true;
      set('evltExportPlan',String(plan.name||((typeof getMyActivePlanName==='function'&&getMyActivePlanName())||'Free')));
      set('evltExportBalance',typeof currentUser!=='undefined'&&currentUser?Number(typeof myCreditsBalance!=='undefined'?myCreditsBalance:0).toLocaleString()+' credits':'Sign in required');
      set('evltExportCost',enabled?Number(cost).toLocaleString()+' credits':'Credits disabled');
      const used=typeof getExportsUsedThisMonth==='function'?getExportsUsedThisMonth():0;
      const cap=plan.maxExports;
      set('evltExportMonthly',plan.unlimitedExports?'Unlimited · '+used+' used':(cap==null?'No monthly cap · '+used+' used':Math.max(0,Number(cap)-used)+' left · '+used+'/'+cap));
      set('evltExportMaxQuality',(Number(plan.maxQuality)||720)+'p');
      set('evltExportFormat','WebM · '+(window.exportBitrateMode==='high'?'High bitrate':'Recommended bitrate'));
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle export details:',err);}
  }
  const prevEstimate=window.renderExportCreditEstimate;
  if(typeof prevEstimate==='function'&&!prevEstimate.__evltInfoWrapped){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=prevEstimate.apply(this,arguments);refreshExportInfo();return out;};wrapped.__evltInfoWrapped=true;window.renderExportCreditEstimate=wrapped;
  }
  const prevOpen=window.openExportSheet;
  if(typeof prevOpen==='function'&&!prevOpen.__evltFullPage){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=prevOpen.apply(this,arguments);refreshExportInfo();return out;};wrapped.__evltFullPage=true;window.openExportSheet=wrapped;
  }
  const prevQuality=window.selectExportQuality;
  if(typeof prevQuality==='function'&&!prevQuality.__evltInfoWrapped){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=prevQuality.apply(this,arguments);refreshExportInfo();return out;};wrapped.__evltInfoWrapped=true;window.selectExportQuality=wrapped;
  }

  // Create persistent dark overlays, not toast replacements, so progress stays visible on phones.
  if(!$('evltImportProgressBackdrop')){
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.id='evltImportProgressBackdrop';el.setAttribute('role','status');el.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
    el.innerHTML='<div class="evlt-import-card"><div class="evlt-import-spinner" id="evltImportSpinner"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="3" y="5" width="13" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M16 10l5-3v10l-5-3"/></svg></div><p id="evltImportPercent">0%</p><h2 id="evltImportTitle">Adding video</h2><div class="evlt-import-bar"><div id="evltImportFill"></div></div><div id="evltImportFileName" aria-live="polite"></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
  window.__ethvelleImportBegin=function(fileName){const el=$('evltImportProgressBackdrop');if(!el)return;el.dataset.startedAt=String(Date.now());el.classList.add('active');el.style.display='flex';const title=$('evltImportTitle');if(title)title.textContent='Adding video to project';const nameEl=$('evltImportFileName');if(nameEl){nameEl.textContent=String(fileName||'').trim();nameEl.style.display=nameEl.textContent?'block':'none';}window.__ethvelleImportProgress(4,'');};
  window.__ethvelleImportProgress=function(pct,phase){const val=clamp(pct,0,100),el=$('evltImportProgressBackdrop');if(!el)return;const percent=$('evltImportPercent'),fill=$('evltImportFill'),spinner=$('evltImportSpinner'),text=$('evltImportPhase');if(percent)percent.textContent=Math.round(val)+'%';if(fill)fill.style.width=val+'%';if(spinner)spinner.style.setProperty('--evlt-import-deg',(val*3.6)+'deg');if(text&&phase)text.textContent=phase;};
  window.__ethvelleImportComplete=function(){window.__ethvelleImportProgress(100,'Clip added — finishing project update…');const el=$('evltImportProgressBackdrop');if(el){const title=$('evltImportTitle');if(title)title.textContent='Video added';setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.remove('active');el.style.display='none';},420);}};
  window.__ethvelleImportFail=function(message){const el=$('evltImportProgressBackdrop');if(!el)return;const title=$('evltImportTitle'),phase=$('evltImportPhase');if(title)title.textContent='Could not add video';if(phase)phase.textContent=message||'Please try choosing the clip again.';setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.remove('active');el.style.display='none';},1200);};

  if(!$('evltExportProgressScreen')){
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.id='evltExportProgressScreen';el.setAttribute('role','status');el.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
    el.innerHTML='<div class="evlt-export-progress-top"><div class="evlt-export-progress-brand">ETHVELLE STUDIO</div><div class="evlt-export-live-pill"><i class="evlt-export-live-dot"></i><span>RENDERING</span></div></div><div class="evlt-export-center"><div class="evlt-render-art"><div class="evlt-render-ring"></div><div class="evlt-render-ring r2"></div><div class="evlt-render-particles"><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i></div><div class="evlt-render-core"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="3" y="5" width="13" height="14" rx="2"/><path d="M16 10l5-3v10l-5-3"/><path d="M7 9h5M7 12h3"/></svg></div><div class="evlt-export-complete-icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M5 12.5l4.4 4.4L19 7.5"/></svg></div></div><div class="evlt-render-overline">YOUR VIDEO IS BEING CREATED</div><h1 id="evltExportProgressTitle">Creating your video</h1><p id="evltExportProgressSubtitle">Keep Ethvelle open while your video is being rendered and saved.</p></div><div class="evlt-export-progress-card"><div class="evlt-export-progress-row"><span id="evltExportProgressPhase">Preparing your project…</span><strong id="evltExportProgressPercent">0%</strong></div><div class="evlt-export-progress-track"><div id="evltExportProgressFill"></div></div><div class="evlt-export-specs"><span id="evltProgressQuality">720p</span><span id="evltProgressFps">30 fps</span><span id="evltProgressDuration">00:00</span></div></div><div class="evlt-export-bottom" id="evltExportProgressBottom">Do not close this page until your video is ready.</div>';
    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
  window.showExportProgress=function(pct){
    const val=clamp(pct,0,100),el=$('evltExportProgressScreen');if(!el)return;
    el.classList.add('active');el.classList.remove('done');
    const percent=$('evltExportProgressPercent'),fill=$('evltExportProgressFill');if(percent)percent.textContent=Math.round(val)+'%';if(fill)fill.style.width=val+'%';
    const phase=$('evltExportProgressPhase'),title=$('evltExportProgressTitle'),subtitle=$('evltExportProgressSubtitle'),live=el.querySelector('.evlt-export-live-pill span'),bottom=$('evltExportProgressBottom');
    let ph='Preparing project…',ti='Creating your video',su='Keep Ethvelle open while your video is being rendered and saved.',li='RENDERING',bo='Do not close this page until your video is ready.';
    if(val>=1&&val<20){ph='Preparing layers and media…';ti='Preparing your video';}
    else if(val>=20&&val<65){ph='Rendering video frames…';ti='Bringing your scenes together';}
    else if(val>=65&&val<94){ph='Encoding the video…';ti='Almost there';}
    else if(val>=94&&val<100){ph='Finalizing your video file…';ti='Finishing export';}
    if(val>=100){ph='Export complete';ti='Your video is ready';su='Your video has been rendered and the download has started.';li='COMPLETE';bo='You can now return to your project.';el.classList.add('done');if(bottom)bottom.style.color='#8EE7C5';}
    else if(bottom)bottom.style.color='';
    if(phase)phase.textContent=ph;if(title)title.textContent=ti;if(subtitle)subtitle.textContent=su;if(live)live.textContent=li;if(bottom)bottom.textContent=bo;
    const q=$('evltProgressQuality');if(q)q.textContent=(typeof exportQuality!=='undefined'?exportQuality:720)+'p';const fps=$('evltProgressFps');if(fps)fps.textContent=(Number(window.exportFrameRate)||30)+' fps';let dur=0;try{dur=typeof getExportContentDurationSec==='function'?getExportContentDurationSec():0.1;}catch(_e){}const d=$('evltProgressDuration');if(d)d.textContent=fmtTime(dur);
  };
  let exportProgressHideTimer=0;
  window.hideExportProgress=function(){const el=$('evltExportProgressScreen');if(!el)return;clearTimeout(exportProgressHideTimer);if(el.classList.contains('done')){exportProgressHideTimer=setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.remove('active');el.classList.remove('done');},1000);}else{el.classList.remove('active');el.classList.remove('done');}};

  // Scrubbing is seek-only. A clip is selected only by a direct tap, never by crossing it with the playhead.
  // Refresh the summary after plan/credit listeners finish updating their values.
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#exportQualityGrid'))setTimeout(refreshExportInfo,0);},true);
  setTimeout(refreshExportInfo,0);
})();
</script>

  const readSaved=()=>{
    let stored=[];

    return [...new Set([...memory,...stored])].slice(0,12);
  };
  let buildingFont=false, paletteObserver=null, observedPalette=null, layoutQueued=false;

  function setSelectedMarkers(color){
    const c=norm(color),dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    dock?.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===c));
  }
  function makeSwatch(color,source){
    const b=source?source.cloneNode(false):document.createElement('button');
    /* Reused palette buttons can carry inline translate/offset/size styles from older layouts.
       Copying those styles was clipping the first orange/white chips at the left edge. */
    b.removeAttribute('style');
    b.type='button';b.className='evpa-swatch';b.dataset.color=color;b.dataset.customColor=readSaved().includes(color)?'true':'false';
    b.style.backgroundColor=color;b.title=color;b.setAttribute('aria-label','Text colour '+color);b.removeAttribute('data-evpaFontOrder');b.removeAttribute('data-evV33Order');
    b.onclick=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColor('textColor',color);};
    return b;
  }
  function buildFontPalette(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock||dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer'))return;
    const font=activeTab(dock)==='font';dock.classList.toggle('evpa-font-palette-mode',font);
    if(!font)return;
    const palette=$('evpaPalette'),swatches=palette?.querySelector('.evpa-swatches');if(!swatches||buildingFont)return;
    const wheel=swatches.querySelector('.evpa-color-wheel')||dock.querySelector('.evpa-color-title-actions .evpa-color-wheel');
    const transparent=swatches.querySelector('.evpa-transparent')||dock.querySelector('.evpa-color-title-actions .evpa-transparent');
    const old=[...swatches.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]')];
    const sourceByColor=new Map();old.forEach(b=>{const c=norm(b.dataset.color);if(!sourceByColor.has(c))sourceByColor.set(c,b);});
    // Preserve the existing palette order, including saved custom colours at the beginning.
    const allColors=[...new Set([...BASE,...readSaved()])];
    const colors=allColors;
    const split=Math.ceil(colors.length/2),topColors=colors.slice(0,split),bottomColors=colors.slice(split);
    const swatchSignature=colors.join('|');
    if(swatches.dataset.evpaV20Built===swatchSignature&&swatches.classList.contains('evpa-v20-font-built')){
      setSelectedMarkers(norm((window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.()||{}).textColor||'#FFFFFF'));return;
    }
    buildingFont=true;
    try{
      const tools=document.createElement('div');tools.className='evpa-font-color-tools';tools.setAttribute('aria-label','Custom color and first row of text colours');
      const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='evpa-font-color-row';row.setAttribute('aria-label','Second row of text colours; swipe right-to-left to see more');
      if(wheel){wheel.classList.add('evpa-color-wheel');wheel.setAttribute('aria-label','Custom color');tools.appendChild(wheel);}
      if(transparent){transparent.classList.add('evpa-transparent');transparent.setAttribute('aria-label','Transparent');tools.appendChild(transparent);}
      topColors.forEach(c=>tools.appendChild(makeSwatch(c,sourceByColor.get(c))));
      // Start the lower row at column one. The custom and transparent buttons occupy the
      // first two cells of row one only, so the first lower-row colour (often maroon) aligns
      // under the custom-colour button rather than being shifted right by a blank spacer.
      bottomColors.forEach(c=>row.appendChild(makeSwatch(c,sourceByColor.get(c))));
      swatches.replaceChildren(tools,row);swatches.classList.add('evpa-v20-font-built');swatches.dataset.evpaV20Built=swatchSignature;
      swatches.scrollLeft=0;
      setSelectedMarkers(norm((window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.()||{}).textColor||'#FFFFFF'));
    }finally{buildingFont=false;}
  }
  function layoutColorTitle(){

    const transparent=group?.querySelector('.evpa-transparent')||utilities?.querySelector('.evpa-transparent')||grid?.querySelector('.evpa-transparent')||swatches.querySelector('.evpa-transparent');
    if(grid){

    }

  }
  function restoreColorTitleClones(){

  }
  function queueLayout(){attachPaletteObserver();/* v25 owns stable, signature-based layout; avoid the older competing reorder pass. */}
  function attachPaletteObserver(){
    const palette=$('evpaPalette');if(!palette||palette===observedPalette)return;
    if(paletteObserver)paletteObserver.disconnect();observedPalette=palette;
    paletteObserver=new MutationObserver(records=>{
      if(buildingFont)return;
      // Only respond to updateDockControls replacing the palette's direct child. Ignore
      // our own internal row construction so opening the panel cannot trigger a repaint loop.
      if(records.some(r=>r.target===palette&&r.type==='childList'))queueLayout();
    });
    paletteObserver.observe(palette,{childList:true});
  }

    rows.forEach((rowColors,index)=>{const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='evce-pop-row';row.setAttribute('aria-label','Gradient colors row '+(index+1));rowColors.forEach(color=>{const sw=document.createElement('button');sw.type='button';sw.className='evce-pop-swatch';sw.style.backgroundColor=color;sw.title=color;sw.setAttribute('aria-label','Use '+color);sw.onclick=()=>{dispatchStopColor(input,color);closeStopPopover();};row.appendChild(sw);});pop.appendChild(row);});
    document.body.appendChild(pop);stopPopover=pop;
    const rect=button.getBoundingClientRect();const pr=pop.getBoundingClientRect();const left=Math.max(12,Math.min(window.innerWidth-pr.width-12,rect.left+rect.width/2-pr.width/2));
    let top=rect.top-pr.height-10;if(top<8)top=Math.min(window.innerHeight-pr.height-8,rect.bottom+8);top=Math.max(8,top);
    pop.style.left=Math.round(left)+'px';pop.style.top=Math.round(top)+'px';
    outsideHandler=e=>{if(!pop.contains(e.target)&&e.target!==button&&!button.contains(e.target))closeStopPopover();};
    escapeHandler=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')closeStopPopover();};
    setTimeout(()=>{if(stopPopover){document.addEventListener('pointerdown',outsideHandler,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',escapeHandler,true);}},0);
  }
  if(typeof nativeOpenStop==='function')window.openEthvelleGradientStopColor=openStopPalette;
  // Re-run the panel simplification on each open. Keep the real Apply action; never re-label
  // Save preset as Apply because that accidentally stores a preset on every tap.
  function simplifyGradientActions(){
    const editor=$('evpaCustomEffectEditor');if(!editor||!editor.querySelector('.evce-stops'))return;
    const actions=editor.querySelector('.evce-actions');if(!actions)return;
    const apply=actions.querySelector(':scope > [data-evce-action="apply"]');if(!apply)return;
    [...actions.children].forEach(b=>{if(b!==apply)b.remove();});
    apply.textContent='Apply';apply.classList.add('primary');apply.style.gridColumn='1';apply.style.display='flex';
    actions.style.gridTemplateColumns='minmax(0,1fr)';
  }
  const oldOpenGradient=window.openEthvelleCustomGradientEditor;
  if(typeof oldOpenGradient==='function'&&!oldOpenGradient.__evV20ApplyOnly){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=oldOpenGradient.apply(this,arguments);simplifyGradientActions();requestAnimationFrame(simplifyGradientActions);return out;};
    wrapped.__evV20ApplyOnly=true;window.openEthvelleCustomGradientEditor=wrapped;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const tab=e.target?.closest?.('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaTabs .evpa-tab');if(!tab)return;
    if(tab.dataset.tab!=='color')restoreColorTitleClones();
    queueLayout();
  },true);
  function boot(){install();queueLayout();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  // No body-wide MutationObserver: it caused repeated palette rebuilds and visible flicker.

})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-color-palette-controller-v25-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const BASE=['#FFFFFF','#F5F5F7','#D9D9E0','#A3A3A3','#70717A','#414141','#17171C','#000000','#FFD9E2','#FFC5CF','#FF8C9A','#FA847C','#FF4E57','#FF174D','#D70D20','#8B0000','#FFE0B2','#FFB74D','#FF7B24','#FFD43B','#FFF176','#C6E64D','#B5E53B','#66BB6A','#25C98A','#16D9D0','#80DEEA','#13A7FF','#2F5CE8','#605DFF','#875BFA','#D946EF'];
  const norm=v=>/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(v||''))?String(v).toUpperCase():null;
  const dock=()=> $('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
  const palette=()=> $('evpaPalette');
  const currentTab=()=>dock()?.querySelector('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';
  const currentTarget=()=>{try{return typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const keyForTab=()=>{const t=currentTab();return t==='stroke'?'strokeColor':t==='shadow'?'shadowColor':(t==='highlight'||t==='background')?'textBackgroundColor':'textColor';};
  const savedColors=()=>{try{const a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ethvelleCustomTextColors')||'[]');return Array.isArray(a)?a.map(norm).filter(Boolean):[];}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const getSwatches=()=>palette()?.querySelector('.evpa-swatches')||null;
  const uniqueColors=nodes=>{const seen=new Set(),out=[];nodes.forEach(n=>{const c=norm(n?.dataset?.color);if(c&&!seen.has(c)){seen.add(c);out.push(c);}});return out;};
  function findSpecial(cls,swatches){return swatches?.querySelector('.'+cls)||dock()?.querySelector('.evpa-color-title-actions .'+cls)||dock()?.querySelector('#evpaPalette .'+cls)||null;}
  function makeSpecial(cls,label,title){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className=cls;b.setAttribute('aria-label',label);b.title=title;return b;}
  function ensureSpecials(swatches){
    // v219-fix: prefer the real canvas-eyedropper button (ev-eyedrop-text) so a rebuilt row already contains it.
    let wheel=findSpecial('evpa-color-wheel',swatches),transparent=findSpecial('evpa-transparent',swatches),eye=findSpecial('ev-eyedrop-text',swatches)||findSpecial('evpa-eye',swatches);
    if(!wheel)wheel=makeSpecial('evpa-color-wheel','Custom color','Custom color');
    if(!transparent)transparent=makeSpecial('evpa-transparent','Transparent / none','Transparent / none');
    if(!eye){
      eye=makeSpecial('ev-eyedrop-button ev-eyedrop-text','Pick color from canvas','Pick color from canvas');
      eye.dataset.evEyedropButton='text';eye.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');
      eye.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m2 22 1-1h3l9-9"/><path d="M3 21v-3l9-9"/><path d="m15 6 3.4-3.4a2.1 2.1 0 1 1 3 3L18 9l.4.4a2.1 2.1 0 1 1-3 3l-3.8-3.8a2.1 2.1 0 1 1 3-3l.4.4Z"/></svg>';
    }
    // Remove any legacy plus/save button whenever this palette is rebuilt.
    swatches?.querySelectorAll('.evpa-save-swatch').forEach(b=>b.remove());
    dock()?.querySelectorAll('#evpaPalette .evpa-save-swatch').forEach(b=>b.remove());
    wheel.type='button';transparent.type='button';eye.type='button';
    wheel.setAttribute('aria-label','Custom color');wheel.title='Custom color';
    transparent.setAttribute('aria-label','Transparent');transparent.title='Transparent / none';
    if(!eye.classList.contains('ev-eyedrop-text')){
      eye.setAttribute('aria-label','Pick a color from the canvas');eye.title='Pick a color from the canvas';
      eye.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m2 22 1-1h3l9-9"/><path d="M3 21v-3l9-9"/><path d="m14 5 5 5"/><path d="m14 5 3-3a2.12 2.12 0 0 1 3 3l-3 3"/><path d="m9 9 6 6"/></svg>';
    }
    wheel.style.setProperty('background','conic-gradient(#ff1744,#ffb600,#d7ff00,#00d889,#00c9ff,#2f5ce8,#8955ff,#d946ef,#ff1744)','important');
    const group=dock()?.querySelector('.evpa-color-title-actions');
    if(group)group.remove();
    return {wheel,transparent,eye};
  }
  function makeColorSwatch(color,source){
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='evpa-swatch';b.dataset.color=color;
    b.style.backgroundColor=color;b.title=color;b.setAttribute('aria-label','Text colour '+color);
    if(source?.dataset?.customColor)b.dataset.customColor=source.dataset.customColor;
    return b;
  }
  function collectPaletteColors(swatches){
    // v219-fix: project custom colors (picked from the canvas) sit right beside Transparent, newest first;
    // the fixed base palette follows. A saved colour already in the base palette is not duplicated.
    const front=[...new Set(savedColors().filter(c=>!BASE.includes(c)))].reverse();
    return [...front,...BASE];
  }
  function syncMarkers(){
    const d=dock();if(!d)return;
    const t=currentTarget()||{};const key=keyForTab();
    const value=norm(t[key]||t.textColor||'#FFFFFF')||'#FFFFFF';
    const featureEnabled=key==='strokeColor'?(t.strokeEnabled===true||Number(t.strokeWidth)>0):key==='shadowColor'?!!t.shadowEnabled:key==='textBackgroundColor'?!!t.textBackgroundEnabled:true;
    const opacity=key==='textColor'?t.textOpacity:key==='strokeColor'?t.strokeOpacity:key==='shadowColor'?t.shadowOpacity:key==='textBackgroundColor'?t.textBackgroundOpacity:100;
    /* v225-fix: a chip the user just tapped stays marked even while Stroke/Shadow/Background is still switched off
       (before, it flashed blue for one frame and then lost its border). Nothing is marked by default while the effect is off. */
    const picked=(window.__ev224Picked||{})[String(t.id||'')+'|'+key];
    const showSel=!!featureEnabled||picked!==undefined;
    const transparentActive=showSel&&Number(opacity)===0&&(!!featureEnabled||picked==='__none__');
    /* v223-fix: exactly ONE selected chip - Transparent excludes every colour swatch and vice versa. */
    d.querySelectorAll('#evpaPalette .evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',showSel&&!transparentActive&&norm(b.dataset.color)===value&&(!!featureEnabled||picked===value)));
    d.querySelectorAll('#evpaPalette .evpa-transparent').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',transparentActive));
  }
  window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches=syncMarkers;
  function fontLayout(){
    const d=dock(),swatches=getSwatches();if(!d||!swatches||currentTab()!=='font')return;
    const colors=collectPaletteColors(swatches),signature=colors.join('|');
    const existingTools=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-tools');
    const existingRow=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-row');
    if(existingTools&&existingRow&&swatches.classList.contains('evpa-v25-font-built')&&
       swatches.dataset.evpaV25FontColors===signature&&existingTools.querySelector('.evpa-color-wheel')&&
       existingTools.querySelector('.evpa-transparent')){syncMarkers();return;}
    const oldScrollLeft=swatches.scrollLeft;
    const special=ensureSpecials(swatches);
    const priorColorButtons=[...swatches.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]')];
    const sourceMap=new Map();priorColorButtons.forEach(b=>{const c=norm(b.dataset.color);if(c&&!sourceMap.has(c))sourceMap.set(c,b);});
    const maroonIndex=colors.indexOf('#8B0000');
    const split=maroonIndex>0?maroonIndex:Math.ceil(colors.length/2);
    const top=colors.slice(0,split),bottom=colors.slice(split);
    const tools=document.createElement('div');tools.className='evpa-font-color-tools';tools.setAttribute('aria-label','Custom, transparent and first row of text colours');
    const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='evpa-font-color-row';row.setAttribute('aria-label','Second row of text colours; swipe right-to-left to see more');
    if(special.eye&&special.eye.classList.contains('ev-eyedrop-text'))tools.appendChild(special.eye);
    tools.appendChild(special.wheel);tools.appendChild(special.transparent);
    top.forEach(c=>tools.appendChild(makeColorSwatch(c,sourceMap.get(c))));
    bottom.forEach(c=>row.appendChild(makeColorSwatch(c,sourceMap.get(c))));
    swatches.replaceChildren(tools,row);
    swatches.classList.add('evpa-v20-font-built','evpa-v25-font-built');
    swatches.dataset.evpaV25FontBuilt='1';
    swatches.dataset.evpaV25FontColors=signature;
    swatches.dataset.evpaV20Built=signature;
    const restore=()=>{if(swatches.isConnected)swatches.scrollLeft=Math.min(oldScrollLeft,Math.max(0,swatches.scrollWidth-swatches.clientWidth));};
    restore();requestAnimationFrame(restore);
    syncMarkers();
  }
  function colorLayout(){
    const d=dock(),swatches=getSwatches();if(!d||!swatches||currentTab()!=='color')return;
    const colors=collectPaletteColors(swatches),signature=colors.join('|');
    const existingGrid=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-grid.evpa-v25-color-grid');
    const existingWheel=existingGrid?.querySelector('.evpa-color-wheel');
    const existingTransparent=existingGrid?.querySelector('.evpa-transparent');
    if(existingGrid&&existingWheel&&existingTransparent&&existingGrid.dataset.evpaV25ColorColors===signature){
      swatches.classList.add('evpa-v25-color-built');syncMarkers();return;
    }
    const oldScrollTop=existingGrid?existingGrid.scrollTop:0;
    const special=ensureSpecials(swatches);
    const sourceMap=new Map();[...swatches.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]')].forEach(b=>{const c=norm(b.dataset.color);if(c&&!sourceMap.has(c))sourceMap.set(c,b);});
    const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='evpa-color-grid evpa-v25-color-grid';grid.setAttribute('aria-label','Color palette; swipe up and down for more colours');
    const realEye=!!(special.eye&&special.eye.classList.contains('ev-eyedrop-text'));
    if(realEye)grid.appendChild(special.eye);
    grid.appendChild(special.wheel);grid.appendChild(special.transparent);
    colors.forEach(c=>grid.appendChild(makeColorSwatch(c,sourceMap.get(c))));
    if(special.eye&&!realEye)grid.appendChild(special.eye);
    grid.dataset.evpaV25ColorBuilt='1';
    grid.dataset.evpaV25ColorColors=signature;
    swatches.replaceChildren(grid);
    swatches.classList.add('evpa-v25-color-built');
    const restore=()=>{if(grid.isConnected)grid.scrollTop=Math.min(oldScrollTop,Math.max(0,grid.scrollHeight-grid.clientHeight));};
    restore();requestAnimationFrame(restore);
    syncMarkers();
  }
  function syncModes(){
    const d=dock();if(!d)return;
    const tab=currentTab();
    d.classList.toggle('evpa-v25-font-mode',tab==='font');
    d.classList.toggle('evpa-v25-color-mode',tab==='color');
    if(tab==='font')fontLayout();else if(tab==='color')colorLayout();
    syncMarkers();
  }
  let layoutQueued=false;
  function queueLayout(){if(layoutQueued)return;layoutQueued=true;(window.requestAnimationFrame||((fn)=>setTimeout(fn,16)))(()=>{layoutQueued=false;try{syncModes();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle palette layout:',err);}});}
  window.addEventListener('ethvelle-project-color-scope',queueLayout);

  /* Two-way horizontal touch drag: both Font colour rows move together in either direction. */
  let drag=null,suppressClickUntil=0,suppressClickTarget=null;
  window.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const scroller=e.target?.closest?.('#ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-v25-font-mode #evpaPalette .evpa-swatches.evpa-v25-font-built');
    if(!scroller||e.isPrimary===false||(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0))return;
    drag={id:e.pointerId,scroller,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,startScroll:scroller.scrollLeft,startButton:e.target?.closest?.('button')||null,moved:false};
  },{capture:true,passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
    const dx=e.clientX-drag.startX,dy=e.clientY-drag.startY;
    if(!drag.moved&&Math.abs(dx)>7&&Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)*1.15)drag.moved=true;
    if(!drag.moved)return;
    const maxScroll=Math.max(0,drag.scroller.scrollWidth-drag.scroller.clientWidth);
    drag.scroller.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxScroll,drag.startScroll-dx));
    suppressClickUntil=Date.now()+650;
    if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
  },{capture:true,passive:false});
  const finishDrag=e=>{if(drag&&(!e||drag.id===e.pointerId)){if(drag.moved){suppressClickUntil=Date.now()+220;suppressClickTarget=drag.startButton;const oldTarget=suppressClickTarget;setTimeout(()=>{if(suppressClickTarget===oldTarget&&Date.now()>=suppressClickUntil)suppressClickTarget=null;},240);}drag=null;}};
  window.addEventListener('pointerup',finishDrag,{capture:true,passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('pointercancel',finishDrag,{capture:true,passive:true});

  /* Delegate palette taps before legacy inline handlers: every swatch uses the same live target API,
     so first-time text, existing text, duplicate Font swatches and the Color grid stay in sync. */
  window.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const d=dock();if(!d||!e.target?.closest)return;
    const swatch=e.target.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch[data-color],#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-color-wheel,#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-transparent,#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-eye');
    if(!swatch)return;
    if(Date.now()<suppressClickUntil&&suppressClickTarget&&(e.target===suppressClickTarget||suppressClickTarget.contains(e.target))){suppressClickTarget=null;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();return;}
    if(Date.now()>=suppressClickUntil)suppressClickTarget=null;
    const key=keyForTab();
    e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColorTarget(key);}catch(_e){}
    if(swatch.classList.contains('evpa-swatch')&&swatch.hasAttribute('data-color')){
      const color=norm(swatch.dataset.color);if(!color)return;
      try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,color);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle swatch selection:',err);}
      syncMarkers();return;
    }
    if(swatch.classList.contains('evpa-color-wheel')){
      try{if(typeof window.openEthvelleColorPicker==='function')window.openEthvelleColorPicker(key);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle custom color picker:',err);}
      return;
    }
    if(swatch.classList.contains('evpa-transparent')){
      try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetNone==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetNone(key);else if(key==='textColor'&&typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetValue==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSetValue('textOpacity',0);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle transparent swatch:',err);}
      syncMarkers();return;
    }
    if(swatch.classList.contains('evpa-eye')){
      try{if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor==='function')window.ethvellePicsartSampleColor();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle canvas eyedropper:',err);}
    }
  },true);

  const originalSetColor=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof originalSetColor==='function'&&!originalSetColor.__evV25PaletteSync){
    const wrappedSetColor=function(){const out=originalSetColor.apply(this,arguments);queueLayout();return out;};
    wrappedSetColor.__evV25PaletteSync=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrappedSetColor;
  }
  const originalSetTool=window.ethvellePicsartSetTool;
  if(typeof originalSetTool==='function'&&!originalSetTool.__evV25PaletteSync){
    const wrappedSetTool=function(){const out=originalSetTool.apply(this,arguments);queueLayout();return out;};
    wrappedSetTool.__evV25PaletteSync=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetTool=wrappedSetTool;
  }
  const originalOpenComposer=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  if(typeof originalOpenComposer==='function'&&!originalOpenComposer.__evV25PaletteSync){
    const wrappedOpenComposer=function(){const out=originalOpenComposer.apply(this,arguments);queueLayout();return out;};
    wrappedOpenComposer.__evV25PaletteSync=true;window.openEthvelleTextComposer=wrappedOpenComposer;
  }
  let observed=null,observer=null;
  function attach(){const p=palette();if(!p||p===observed)return;if(observer)observer.disconnect();observed=p;observer=new MutationObserver(()=>queueLayout());observer.observe(p,{childList:true,subtree:true});queueLayout();}
  function boot(){attach();syncModes();syncMarkers();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  const bodyObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(dock()&&palette()!==observed)attach();});
  if(document.body)bodyObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  window.addEventListener('resize',queueLayout,{passive:true});
})();
</script>
