<script id="ethvelle-v30-clip-animations-and-filters">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const defaults = {brightness:100,contrast:100,saturation:100,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0};
  let animCategory = 'in';
  let panelPersistTimer = 0;
  let previewRenderRaf = 0;
  const visualTypes = new Set(['image','video','text']);
  const clamp = (v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):min));
  const getOverlays = ()=>typeof overlays!=='undefined' && Array.isArray(overlays) ? overlays : [];
  const getBackgrounds = ()=>typeof backgrounds!=='undefined' && Array.isArray(backgrounds) ? backgrounds : [];
  const selectedId = ()=>String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined' || selectedClip==null ? '' : selectedClip);
  function overlayForSelected(){return getOverlays().find(o=>String(o.id)===selectedId())||null;}
  function backgroundForSelected(){return getBackgrounds().find(b=>String(b.id)===selectedId())||null;}
  function currentVisualTarget(){
    const ov=overlayForSelected();
    if(ov && visualTypes.has(ov.type)) return {kind:'overlay',model:ov};
    const bg=backgroundForSelected();
    if(bg) return {kind:'background',model:bg};
    return null;
  }
  function getAnimationConfig(ov){
    if(!ov.clipAnimations || typeof ov.clipAnimations!=='object') ov.clipAnimations={};
    const c=ov.clipAnimations;
    c.in = c.in || 'none'; c.out = c.out || 'none'; c.overall = c.overall || 'none';
    c.inDuration = clamp(c.inDuration==null?0.45:c.inDuration,0.1,2.5);
    c.outDuration = clamp(c.outDuration==null?0.45:c.outDuration,0.1,2.5);
    c.overallDuration = clamp(c.overallDuration==null?1.2:c.overallDuration,0.25,4);
    return c;
  }
  const eased = p=>{p=clamp(p,0,1);return p*p*(3-2*p);};
  function phaseState(name,phase,progress,w,h){
    const p=eased(progress), out={opacity:1,scale:1,x:0,y:0,rotation:0};
    if(!name||name==='none') return out;
    if(name==='fade'){out.opacity=phase==='in'?p:1-p;return out;}
    if(name==='zoom-in'){out.scale=phase==='in'?(0.58+0.42*p):(1+0.28*p);return out;}
    if(name==='zoom-out'){out.scale=phase==='in'?(1.32-0.32*p):(1-0.58*p);return out;}
    if(name==='pop'){out.scale=phase==='in'?(0.22+0.78*p):(1-0.62*p);out.opacity=phase==='in'?(0.45+0.55*p):(1-0.25*p);return out;}
    if(name==='rotate'){out.rotation=phase==='in'?-150*(1-p):100*p;out.scale=phase==='in'?(0.86+0.14*p):(1-0.12*p);return out;}
    const dx=Math.max(36,w*0.18),dy=Math.max(36,h*0.18);
    if(name==='slide-left') out.x=phase==='in'?-dx*(1-p):-dx*p;
    else if(name==='slide-right') out.x=phase==='in'?dx*(1-p):dx*p;
    else if(name==='slide-up') out.y=phase==='in'?dy*(1-p):-dy*p;
    else if(name==='slide-down') out.y=phase==='in'?-dy*(1-p):dy*p;
    return out;
  }
  window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState = function(ov,tSec){
    const neutral={opacity:1,scale:1,x:0,y:0,rotation:0};
    if(!ov || !ov.clipAnimations || typeof ov.clipAnimations!=='object') return neutral;
    const c=ov.clipAnimations;
    const start=Number(ov.trimStart)||0;
    const end=Number.isFinite(Number(ov.trimEnd))?Number(ov.trimEnd):start;
    const t=Number.isFinite(Number(tSec))?Number(tSec):start;
    const len=Math.max(0.05,end-start);
    const w=Math.max(120,Number(typeof stageW!=='undefined'?stageW:0)||360);
    const h=Math.max(120,Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:0)||640);
    const result={...neutral};
    const add=(v)=>{result.opacity*=v.opacity;result.scale*=v.scale;result.x+=v.x;result.y+=v.y;result.rotation+=v.rotation;};
    const inName=c.in||'none',outName=c.out||'none';
    const inDur=Math.min(len,clamp(c.inDuration==null?0.45:c.inDuration,0.1,2.5));
    const outDur=Math.min(len,clamp(c.outDuration==null?0.45:c.outDuration,0.1,2.5));
    if(inName!=='none' && t<start+inDur) add(phaseState(inName,'in',(t-start)/Math.max(0.001,inDur),w,h));
    if(outName!=='none' && t>=end-outDur) add(phaseState(outName,'out',(t-(end-outDur))/Math.max(0.001,outDur),w,h));
    const overall=c.overall||'none';
    if(overall!=='none' && t>=start && t<end){
      const period=clamp(c.overallDuration==null?1.2:c.overallDuration,0.25,4);
      const phase=((Math.max(0,t-start)%period)/period)*Math.PI*2;
      if(overall==='pulse') result.scale*=1+0.055*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(phase));
      else if(overall==='zoom') result.scale*=1+0.075*(0.5+0.5*Math.sin(phase));
      else if(overall==='float') result.y+=Math.sin(phase)*Math.min(12,h*0.025);
      else if(overall==='shake') result.x+=Math.sin(phase*3)*Math.min(5,w*0.012);
      else if(overall==='rotate') result.rotation+=Math.sin(phase)*4;
    }
    result.opacity=clamp(result.opacity,0,1);result.scale=clamp(result.scale,0.05,3);
    return result;
  };
  function schedulePreview(){
    if(previewRenderRaf) return;
    previewRenderRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      previewRenderRaf=0;
      try{if(typeof renderTimelineScrubPreview==='function')renderTimelineScrubPreview();else if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle preview update:',e);}
    });
  }
  let clipAnimationPreviewRaf = 0;
  let clipAnimationPreviewToken = 0;
  function renderClipAnimationPreviewFrame(){
    try{if(typeof renderTimelineScrubPreview==='function')renderTimelineScrubPreview();else if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}
    catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle clip preview frame:',e);}
  }
  function previewClipAnimationWindow(ov, category){
    // This preview belongs to an image/video/text clip, not to the character rig. A
    // media-only project therefore uses the same clock and compositor without a character.
    if(!ov || !visualTypes.has(ov.type)) return;
    const c=getAnimationConfig(ov);
    const start=Math.max(0,Number(ov.trimStart)||0);
    const end=Number.isFinite(Number(ov.trimEnd))?Number(ov.trimEnd):start;
    const len=Math.max(0,end-start);
    if(len<0.05) return;

    let from=start, to=end;
    if(category==='in'){
      // In: preview exactly from the clip's first frame for the chosen In duration.
      const span=Math.min(len,clamp(c.inDuration==null?0.45:c.inDuration,0.1,2.5));
      from=start;
      to=start+span;
    }else if(category==='out'){
      // Out: preview the final part of the clip. Example: a 5s clip / 1s Out = 4s to 5s.
      const span=Math.min(len,clamp(c.outDuration==null?0.45:c.outDuration,0.1,2.5));
      from=end-span;
      to=end;
    }else{
      const cycle=clamp(c.overallDuration==null?1.2:c.overallDuration,0.25,4);
      to=Math.min(end,start+Math.max(0.65,cycle));
    }
    from=Math.max(start,Math.min(end-0.04,from));
    to=Math.max(from+0.04,Math.min(end,to));
    const stopAt=Math.max(from,to-0.001);

    // Keep the shared timeline long enough even in projects with no character at all.
    if(typeof timelineDuration!=='undefined') timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,end);

    // Stop a previous preview, but keep the current media clip selected in the timeline.
    if(isPlaying) pausePlayback();
    if(clipAnimationPreviewRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(clipAnimationPreviewRaf);
    clipAnimationPreviewRaf=0;
    const token=++clipAnimationPreviewToken;

    seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(from);
    isPlaying=true;
    playbackStartedAtMs=performance.now();
    playbackStartTimeSec=from;
    const icon=document.getElementById('playIcon');
    if(icon) icon.innerHTML='<path d="M6 5h4v14H6zM14 5h4v14h-4z"/>';

    const tick=(timestamp)=>{
      if(token!==clipAnimationPreviewToken || !isPlaying) return;
      const t=playbackStartTimeSec+(timestamp-playbackStartedAtMs)/1000;
      if(t>=to){
        setCurrentAbsoluteTime(stopAt);
        isPlaying=false;
        clipAnimationPreviewRaf=0;
        playbackRaf=null;
        const playIcon=document.getElementById('playIcon');
        if(playIcon) playIcon.innerHTML='<path d="M8 5v14l11-7z"/>';
        renderClipAnimationPreviewFrame();
        return;
      }
      setCurrentAbsoluteTime(t);
      renderClipAnimationPreviewFrame();
      clipAnimationPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      playbackRaf=clipAnimationPreviewRaf;
    };
    clipAnimationPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    playbackRaf=clipAnimationPreviewRaf;
  }

  function updateClipPanelRangeProgress(input){
    if(!input || input.type!=='range') return;
    const min=Number(input.min)||0;
    const max=Number(input.max)||100;
    const value=Number(input.value)||0;
    const pct=max<=min?0:Math.max(0,Math.min(100,(value-min)/(max-min)*100));
    input.style.setProperty('--ev-range-progress',pct+'%');
  }
  function initClipPanelRanges(root){
    if(!root) return;
    root.querySelectorAll('.evca-panel input[type="range"]').forEach(updateClipPanelRangeProgress);
  }
  document.addEventListener('input',function(e){
    if(e.target && e.target.matches && e.target.matches('.evca-panel input[type="range"]')) updateClipPanelRangeProgress(e.target);
  },true);

  function schedulePersist(){
    clearTimeout(panelPersistTimer);
    panelPersistTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle filter/animation save:',e);}
    },220);
  }
  function commitModelChange(model){
    try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle save:',e);}
  }
  const animationOptions={
    in:[['none','None'],['fade','Fade'],['zoom-in','Zoom in'],['zoom-out','Zoom out'],['slide-left','Slide left'],['slide-right','Slide right'],['slide-up','Slide up'],['slide-down','Slide down'],['pop','Pop'],['rotate','Rotate']],
    out:[['none','None'],['fade','Fade'],['zoom-in','Zoom in'],['zoom-out','Zoom out'],['slide-left','Slide left'],['slide-right','Slide right'],['slide-up','Slide up'],['slide-down','Slide down'],['pop','Pop'],['rotate','Rotate']],
    overall:[['none','None'],['pulse','Pulse'],['float','Float'],['shake','Shake'],['zoom','Zoom'],['rotate','Rotate']]
  };
  window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationCategory=function(cat){
    if(!animationOptions[cat])return;
    if(isPlaying) pausePlayback();
    animCategory=cat;
    renderClipAnimationPanel();
    const root=document.getElementById('clipAnimSettingsBody');
    const activeTab=root&&root.querySelector('.evca-tab.active');
    if(activeTab){
      activeTab.classList.add('evca-flash-selected');
      setTimeout(()=>{if(activeTab.isConnected)activeTab.classList.remove('evca-flash-selected');},500);
    }
  };
  window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationPreset=function(preset){
    const ov=overlayForSelected();if(!ov||!visualTypes.has(ov.type))return;
    const c=getAnimationConfig(ov);c[animCategory]=preset;
    if(animCategory==='in' && c.in!=='none' && c.inDuration==null)c.inDuration=0.45;
    commitModelChange(ov);
    renderClipAnimationPanel();
    if(preset!=='none'){
      try{previewClipAnimationWindow(ov,animCategory);}
      catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle animation playback preview:',e);}
    }else{
      try{if(isPlaying)pausePlayback();seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(Math.max(0,Number(ov.trimStart)||0));}
      catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle animation reset preview:',e);}
    }
  };
  window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationDuration=function(value){
    const ov=overlayForSelected();if(!ov||!visualTypes.has(ov.type))return;
    const c=getAnimationConfig(ov);const key=animCategory==='overall'?'overallDuration':animCategory+'Duration';
    c[key]=clamp(value,animCategory==='overall'?0.25:0.1,animCategory==='overall'?4:2.5);
    const output=document.getElementById('evca-duration-value');if(output)output.textContent=Number(c[key]).toFixed(2)+'s';
    schedulePersist();
  };
  function clipPresetPreviewMarkup(key){
    // Use the supplied Ethvelle animation artwork in every preset tile. The outer sprite
    // still receives each preset's own CSS motion so the previews remain individually distinct.
    return `<span class="evca-preview-scene" aria-hidden="true"><span class="evca-preview-sprite"><img src="https://i.postimg.cc/sgnfkvT3/Gemini-Generated-Image-ld9tt5ld9tt5ld9t.jpg" alt="" draggable="false" loading="eager" decoding="async"></span></span>`;
  }
  let clipAnimationSearchQuery='';
  window.filterClipAnimationPresets=function(value){
    clipAnimationSearchQuery=String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    const wrap=document.getElementById('clipAnimationSearchWrap');if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('has-query',!!clipAnimationSearchQuery);
    const root=document.getElementById('clipAnimSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    let visible=0;root.querySelectorAll('.evca-preset').forEach(btn=>{const label=String(btn.querySelector('.evca-preset-label')?.textContent||'').toLowerCase(),key=String(btn.dataset.preset||'').toLowerCase(),show=!clipAnimationSearchQuery||label.includes(clipAnimationSearchQuery)||key.includes(clipAnimationSearchQuery);btn.hidden=!show;if(show)visible++;});
    const empty=root.querySelector('#evca-animation-no-results');if(empty)empty.hidden=visible>0;
  };
  window.clearClipAnimationPresetSearch=function(){const input=document.getElementById('clipAnimationSearchInput');if(input)input.value='';window.filterClipAnimationPresets('');if(input)input.focus();};
  function renderClipAnimationPanel(){
    const root=document.getElementById('clipAnimSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    const ov=overlayForSelected();
    if(!ov||!visualTypes.has(ov.type)){
      root.innerHTML='<p class="evca-panel-note">Select an image, video, or text clip on the timeline to add clip animations.</p>';return;
    }
    const c=getAnimationConfig(ov);const options=animationOptions[animCategory];const chosen=c[animCategory]||'none';
    const durationKey=animCategory==='overall'?'overallDuration':animCategory+'Duration';
    const duration=c[durationKey] == null ? (animCategory==='overall'?1.2:0.45) : c[durationKey];
    const categoryLabel=animCategory==='in'?'In animation':animCategory==='out'?'Out animation':'Overall animation';
    const shownOptions=options.filter(([key,label])=>!clipAnimationSearchQuery||key.toLowerCase().includes(clipAnimationSearchQuery)||label.toLowerCase().includes(clipAnimationSearchQuery));
    root.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-panel">
      <div class="evca-tabs">
        <button type="button" class="evca-tab ${animCategory==='in'?'active':''}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationCategory('in')">In animation</button>
        <button type="button" class="evca-tab ${animCategory==='out'?'active':''}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationCategory('out')">Out animation</button>
        <button type="button" class="evca-tab ${animCategory==='overall'?'active':''}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationCategory('overall')">Overall animation</button>
      </div>
      <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap evca-search-wrap${clipAnimationSearchQuery?' has-query':''}" id="clipAnimationSearchWrap">
        <svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>
        <input class="editor-asset-search" id="clipAnimationSearchInput" type="search" aria-label="Search clip animations" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search animations…" value="${escapeHtml(clipAnimationSearchQuery)}" oninput="window.filterClipAnimationPresets(this.value)">
        <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" aria-label="Clear animation search" onclick="window.clearClipAnimationPresetSearch()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
      </div>
      <div><div class="evca-section-title">${categoryLabel}</div><div class="evca-preset-grid">${shownOptions.map(([key,label])=>`<button type="button" class="evca-preset ${chosen===key?'active':''}" data-preset="${key}" data-animation-category="${animCategory}" aria-pressed="${chosen===key?'true':'false'}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationPreset('${key}')">${clipPresetPreviewMarkup(key)}<span class="evca-preset-label">${label}</span></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="evca-panel-note" id="evca-animation-no-results" ${shownOptions.length?'hidden':''}>No animations found. Try another search.</p></div>
      <label class="evpa-range evca-range-control"><span>${animCategory==='overall'?'Cycle duration':'Duration'}<output id="evca-duration-value">${Number(duration).toFixed(2)}s</output></span><input type="range" min="${animCategory==='overall'?'0.25':'0.1'}" max="${animCategory==='overall'?'4':'2.5'}" step="0.05" value="${duration}" oninput="window.ethvelleSetClipAnimationDuration(this.value)" onchange="window.ethvellePreviewSelectedClipAnimation()"></label>
    </div>`;
    initClipPanelRanges(root);
  }
  window.ethvellePreviewSelectedClipAnimation=function(){
    const ov=overlayForSelected();
    if(!ov||!visualTypes.has(ov.type))return;
    try{previewClipAnimationWindow(ov,animCategory);}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle animation duration preview:',e);}
  };

  let clipFilterSearchQuery='';
  window.filterClipFilterPresets=function(value){
    clipFilterSearchQuery=String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    const wrap=document.getElementById('clipFilterSearchWrap');if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('has-query',!!clipFilterSearchQuery);
    const root=document.getElementById('clipFilterSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    let visible=0;root.querySelectorAll('.evca-filter-preset').forEach(btn=>{const text=String(btn.textContent||'').toLowerCase();const show=!clipFilterSearchQuery||text.includes(clipFilterSearchQuery);btn.hidden=!show;if(show)visible++;});
    const empty=root.querySelector('#evca-filter-no-results');if(empty)empty.hidden=visible>0;
  };
  window.clearClipFilterPresetSearch=function(){const input=document.getElementById('clipFilterSearchInput');if(input)input.value='';window.filterClipFilterPresets('');if(input)input.focus();};
  const filterPresets={
    original:{label:'Original',adjustments:{...defaults}},
    vivid:{label:'Vivid',adjustments:{brightness:103,contrast:112,saturation:142,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0}},
    warm:{label:'Warm',adjustments:{brightness:103,contrast:104,saturation:115,hue:-9,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:18}},
    cool:{label:'Cool',adjustments:{brightness:102,contrast:108,saturation:108,hue:18,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0}},
    bw:{label:'B&W',adjustments:{brightness:100,contrast:112,saturation:100,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:100,sepia:0}},
    sepia:{label:'Sepia',adjustments:{brightness:101,contrast:105,saturation:82,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:72}},
    dramatic:{label:'Dramatic',adjustments:{brightness:96,contrast:132,saturation:88,hue:0,blur:0,grayscale:0,sepia:0}}
  };
  function normalizeAdjustments(obj){return Object.assign({},defaults,obj||{});}
  function applySelectedFilterPreview(target){
    if(!target||!target.model)return;
    const model=target.model;
    const filter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter?window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(model.adjustments,model.enhanceAmount||0):'none';
    if(target.kind==='overlay'){
      const id=String(model.id);
      const base=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
      const keyed=model.type==='video'&&model.chromaKey&&model.chromaKey.enabled&&typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'?overlayChromaCanvases[id]:null;
      const el=keyed||base;
      if(el&&el.style.filter!==filter)el.style.filter=filter;
    }else{
      const img=document.getElementById('bgImgLayer');
      const video=document.getElementById('bgVideoLayer');
      if(img&&img.style.filter!==filter)img.style.filter=filter;
      if(video&&video.style.filter!==filter)video.style.filter=filter;
    }
  }
  window.ethvelleSetClipFilterPreset=function(key){
    const target=currentVisualTarget();if(!target)return;
    const preset=filterPresets[key];if(!preset)return;
    target.model.adjustments={...preset.adjustments};target.model.filterPreset=key;
    target.model.enhanceAmount=0;target.model.__evltEnhanceUserSet=true;
    applySelectedFilterPreview(target);
    try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle filter save:',e);}
    renderClipFilterPanel();
  };
  window.ethvelleSetClipFilterValue=function(key,value,rangeEl){
    const target=currentVisualTarget();if(!target||!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(defaults,key))return;
    const limits={brightness:[0,200],contrast:[0,200],saturation:[0,200],hue:[-180,180],blur:[0,12],grayscale:[0,100],sepia:[0,100]};
    if(!target.model.adjustments)target.model.adjustments={...defaults};
    target.model.adjustments[key]=clamp(value,limits[key][0],limits[key][1]);target.model.filterPreset='custom';target.model.enhanceAmount=0;target.model.__evltEnhanceUserSet=true;
    const out=document.getElementById('evf-value-'+key);if(out)out.textContent=String(target.model.adjustments[key])+(key==='hue'?'°':key==='blur'?'px':'%');
    if(rangeEl)rangeEl.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(target.model.adjustments[key]));
    applySelectedFilterPreview(target);schedulePersist();
  };
  function renderClipFilterPanel(){
    const root=document.getElementById('clipFilterSettingsBody');if(!root)return;
    const target=currentVisualTarget();
    if(!target){root.innerHTML='<p class="evca-panel-note">Select an image, video, text, or Background clip to apply a filter.</p>';return;}
    const model=target.model;const adj=normalizeAdjustments(model.adjustments);const active=model.filterPreset||'original';
    const sliderDefs=[
      ['brightness','Brightness',0,200,1,'%'],['contrast','Contrast',0,200,1,'%'],['saturation','Saturation',0,200,1,'%'],
      ['hue','Hue',-180,180,1,'°'],['blur','Blur',0,12,0.5,'px'],['grayscale','B&W',0,100,1,'%'],['sepia','Sepia',0,100,1,'%']
    ];
    const shownFilters=Object.entries(filterPresets).filter(([key,p])=>!clipFilterSearchQuery||key.toLowerCase().includes(clipFilterSearchQuery)||p.label.toLowerCase().includes(clipFilterSearchQuery));
    root.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-panel">
      <div><div class="evca-section-title">Presets</div>
        <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap evca-search-wrap${clipFilterSearchQuery?' has-query':''}" id="clipFilterSearchWrap">
          <svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>
          <input class="editor-asset-search" id="clipFilterSearchInput" type="search" aria-label="Search filters" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search filters…" value="${escapeHtml(clipFilterSearchQuery)}" oninput="window.filterClipFilterPresets(this.value)">
          <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" aria-label="Clear filter search" onclick="window.clearClipFilterPresetSearch()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M7 7l10 10M17 7L7 17"/></svg></button>
        </div>
        <div class="evca-filter-presets">${shownFilters.map(([key,p])=>`<button type="button" class="evca-filter-preset ${active===key?'active':''}" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipFilterPreset('${key}')"><span class="evca-filter-swatch ${key==='original'?'':key}"></span><span>${p.label}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>
        <p class="evca-panel-note" id="evca-filter-no-results" ${shownFilters.length?'hidden':''}>No filters found. Try another search.</p>
      </div>
      <div>${sliderDefs.map(([key,label,min,max,step,suffix])=>`<label class="evpa-range evca-range-control"><span>${label}<output id="evf-value-${key}">${Number(adj[key])}${suffix}</output></span><input id="evf-range-${key}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${Number(adj[key])}" oninput="window.ethvelleSetClipFilterValue('${key}',this.value,this)"></label>`).join('')}</div>
      <button class="evca-filter-reset" type="button" onclick="window.ethvelleSetClipFilterPreset('original')">Reset filter</button>
    </div>`;
    initClipPanelRanges(root);
  }

  // Extension panels use the editor's established slide-up panel rather than adding a
  // second modal or replacing the existing panel-routing logic.
  try{
    if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined'){
      panelContent.clipAnimations={title:'Clip animations',body:'<div id="clipAnimSettingsBody"></div>'};
      panelContent.clipFilter={title:'Filter',body:'<div id="clipFilterSettingsBody"></div>'};
    }
  }catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle panel registration:',e);}
  const oldOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpenPanel==='function'&&!oldOpenPanel.__ethvelleV30Panels){
    const wrappedOpenPanel=function(key){
      const result=oldOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='clipAnimations')renderClipAnimationPanel();
      if(key==='clipFilter')renderClipFilterPanel();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedOpenPanel.__ethvelleV30Panels=true;window.openPanel=wrappedOpenPanel;
  }

  function addToolbarTool(id,label,svg,handler){
    const row=document.getElementById('bottomToolsClip');if(!row||document.getElementById(id))return;
    const tool=document.createElement('div');tool.className='tool-item evca-toolbar-tool';tool.id=id;tool.setAttribute('role','button');tool.setAttribute('tabindex','0');tool.setAttribute('aria-label',label);tool.style.display='none';
    tool.innerHTML=svg+'<span>'+label+'</span>';
    tool.addEventListener('click',handler);tool.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();handler();}});
    const before=document.getElementById('clipTrimTool')||document.getElementById('clipForwardTool');if(before&&before.parentNode===row)row.insertBefore(tool,before);else row.appendChild(tool);
  }
  function bootToolbar(){
    const animSvg='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 16.5 8.5 12 4 7.5"/><path d="M20 7.5 15.5 12 20 16.5"/><path d="M9 5h6M9 19h6"/><path d="M12 5v14"/></svg>';
    const filterSvg='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 5h16M7 12h10M10 19h4"/><circle cx="8" cy="5" r="2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/><circle cx="15" cy="12" r="2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/><circle cx="12" cy="19" r="2" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/></svg>';
    addToolbarTool('clipOverlayAnimationTool','Clip animations',animSvg,()=>window.openPanel('clipAnimations'));
    addToolbarTool('clipOverlayFilterTool','Filter',filterSvg,()=>window.openPanel('clipFilter'));
    const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
    if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__ethvelleV30Toolbar){
      const wrappedSync=function(){
        const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);
        const a=document.getElementById('clipOverlayAnimationTool'),f=document.getElementById('clipOverlayFilterTool');
        const ov=overlayForSelected(),bg=backgroundForSelected();
        const canAnimate=!!ov&&visualTypes.has(ov.type);
        const canFilter=canAnimate||!!bg;
        if(a)a.style.display=canAnimate?'flex':'none';
        if(f)f.style.display=canFilter?'flex':'none';
        return r;
      };
      wrappedSync.__ethvelleV30Toolbar=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrappedSync;
    }
    try{if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();}catch(e){}
  }

  // Apply the chosen animation + filter after the base renderer has updated its DOM elements.
  // This keeps animation state independent of clip selection and avoids rewriting the core
  // compositor, while the export path below uses the same state function for frame parity.
  const oldRenderOverlayMedia=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldRenderOverlayMedia==='function'&&!oldRenderOverlayMedia.__ethvelleV30Animations){
    const wrappedRenderOverlayMedia=function(tSecAbsolute){
      const result=oldRenderOverlayMedia.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        const t=Number.isFinite(Number(tSecAbsolute))?Number(tSecAbsolute):(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0);
        getOverlays().forEach(ov=>{
          if(!ov||!visualTypes.has(ov.type))return;
          let el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;
          if(!el)return;
          const keyCanvas=ov.type==='video'&&ov.chromaKey&&ov.chromaKey.enabled&&typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'?overlayChromaCanvases[ov.id]:null;
          const target=keyCanvas||el;
          const tr=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov):(ov.transform||{});const a=window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,t);
          const transform=`translate(${(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+a.x}px, ${(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+a.y}px) rotate(${(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+a.rotation}deg) scale(${(Number(tr.scale)||1)*a.scale})`+(window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix?window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix(ov):'');
          if(target.style.transform!==transform)target.style.transform=transform;
          const rawAnimationOpacity=Number(a.opacity==null?1:a.opacity);
          const animationOpacity=Number.isFinite(rawAnimationOpacity)?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,rawAnimationOpacity)):1;
          const rawTextOpacity=Number(ov.textOverallOpacity==null?100:ov.textOverallOpacity);
          const textOpacityMultiplier=ov.type==='text'?(Number.isFinite(rawTextOpacity)?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,rawTextOpacity))/100:1):1;
          const combinedOpacity=animationOpacity*textOpacityMultiplier;
          if(target.style.opacity!==String(combinedOpacity))target.style.opacity=String(combinedOpacity);
          const filter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter?window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(ov.adjustments,ov.enhanceAmount||0):'none';
          if(target.style.filter!==filter)target.style.filter=filter;
          if(keyCanvas&&el.style.opacity!=='0')el.style.opacity='0';
        });
      }catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle clip animation preview:',e);}
      return result;
    };
    wrappedRenderOverlayMedia.__ethvelleV30Animations=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=wrappedRenderOverlayMedia;
  }
  const oldApplyBackground=window.applyBackground;
  if(typeof oldApplyBackground==='function'&&!oldApplyBackground.__ethvelleV30Filters){
    const wrappedApplyBackground=function(){
      const result=oldApplyBackground.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0):backgroundForSelected();
        const img=document.getElementById('bgImgLayer');
        if(bg&&img){const filter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter?window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(bg.adjustments,bg.enhanceAmount||0):'none';if(img.style.filter!==filter)img.style.filter=filter;}
      }catch(e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrappedApplyBackground.__ethvelleV30Filters=true;window.applyBackground=wrappedApplyBackground;
  }
  try{bootToolbar();}catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle clip toolbar setup:',e);}
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v32-project-open-restore-guard">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const original=window.openEditor;
  if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__evV32RestoreGuard)return;
  function latestProject(project){
    if(!project||project.id==null)return project;
    try{
      const list=(typeof projects!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(projects))?projects:[];
      const latest=list.find(item=>String(item&&item.id)===String(project.id));
      if(latest && !(latest.__evIndexOnly && !project.__evIndexOnly))return latest;
      if(latest && project.__evIndexOnly)return latest;
    }catch(_e){}
    return project;
  }
  const wrapped=function(project){
    const args=Array.from(arguments),ctx=this;
    const restore=window.__ethvelleProjectsRestorePromise;
    const openNow=()=>{args[0]=latestProject(args[0]);return original.apply(ctx,args);};
    if(restore && !window.__ethvelleProjectsRestoreReady){
      // Never open an early localStorage index entry while the full savedState/media refs are
      // still being restored from IndexedDB. Otherwise the editor starts with an empty overlays[].
      return restore.then(openNow,openNow);
    }
    return openNow();
  };
  wrapped.__evV32RestoreGuard=true;
  window.openEditor=wrapped;
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v42-focus-and-short-clip-fix">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const SHORT_CLIP_WIDTH = 42;
  const CLIP_SELECTOR = '#timelineInner .anim-clip, #timelineInner .overlay-clip, #timelineInner .track-clip';
  const observedClips = new WeakSet();
  let timelineRoot = null;
  let timelineMutationObserver = null;
  let bodyWaitObserver = null;
  let clipResizeObserver = null;

  function updateShortClip(el){
    if(!el || !el.isConnected) return;
    const width = el.getBoundingClientRect().width || Number.parseFloat(el.style.width) || 0;
    el.classList.toggle('ev-short-clip', width > 0 && width < SHORT_CLIP_WIDTH);
  }
  function observeClip(el){
    if(!el || !el.matches || !el.matches('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip')) return;
    updateShortClip(el);
    if(clipResizeObserver && !observedClips.has(el)){
      observedClips.add(el);
      try{clipResizeObserver.observe(el);}catch(_e){}
    }
  }
  function observeAdded(node){
    if(!node || node.nodeType !== 1) return;
    if(node.matches && node.matches('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip')) observeClip(node);
    if(node.querySelectorAll) node.querySelectorAll('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip').forEach(observeClip);
  }
  function attachTimelineObservers(){
    const root = document.getElementById('timelineInner');
    if(!root || root === timelineRoot) return;
    if(timelineMutationObserver) timelineMutationObserver.disconnect();
    timelineRoot = root;
    if(typeof ResizeObserver === 'function'){
      clipResizeObserver = clipResizeObserver || new ResizeObserver(entries=>entries.forEach(entry=>updateShortClip(entry.target)));
    }
    root.querySelectorAll('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip').forEach(observeClip);
    if(typeof MutationObserver === 'function'){
      timelineMutationObserver = new MutationObserver(records=>records.forEach(record=>record.addedNodes.forEach(observeAdded)));
      timelineMutationObserver.observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    }
    if(bodyWaitObserver){bodyWaitObserver.disconnect();bodyWaitObserver=null;}
  }
  if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', attachTimelineObservers, {once:true});
  else attachTimelineObservers();
  if(document.body && !timelineRoot && typeof MutationObserver === 'function'){
    bodyWaitObserver = new MutationObserver(attachTimelineObservers);
    bodyWaitObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(timelineRoot)timelineRoot.querySelectorAll('.anim-clip, .overlay-clip, .track-clip').forEach(updateShortClip);},{passive:true});

  /* Preserve the composing text input focus while applying a swatch. This keeps the software
     keyboard available when a user picks a text colour rather than forcing them to tap the text
     field again after every colour change. */
  let focusToRestore = null;
  let palettePointerDown = false;
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const inPalette = !!(e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest(
      '#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette button, #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip button, #ethvelleTextComposer .evpa-palette button'
    ));
    palettePointerDown = inPalette;
    focusToRestore = null;
    if(!inPalette) return;
    const active = document.activeElement;
    if(active && active.matches && active.matches('textarea, input:not([type="range"]), [contenteditable="true"]') && active.closest('#ethvelleTextComposer')){
      focusToRestore = active;
    }
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>{
    const input = palettePointerDown ? focusToRestore : null;
    palettePointerDown = false;
    focusToRestore = null;
    if(!input) return;
    setTimeout(()=>{
      const composer = document.getElementById('ethvelleTextComposer');
      if(!input.isConnected || !composer || !composer.classList.contains('active')) return;
      if(document.activeElement !== input){
        try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){try{input.focus();}catch(__e){}}
      }
    },90);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{palettePointerDown=false;focusToRestore=null;},true);
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v43-text-picker-timeline-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const norm = v => {
    const s=String(v||'').trim();
    if(/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(s))return s.toUpperCase();
    const m=s.match(/rgba?\((\d+)[, ]+\s*(\d+)[, ]+\s*(\d+)/i);
    return m?'#'+m.slice(1,4).map(n=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Number(n))).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('').toUpperCase():'';
  };
  let composerColor='';
  function syncComposerSwatches(color){
    const c=norm(color);if(!c)return;composerColor=c;
    const strip=document.getElementById('evtcColorStrip');if(!strip)return;
    strip.querySelectorAll('.evtc-color-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===c));
  }
  window.__ethvelleSyncTextSwatches=function(key,color){
    const c=norm(color);if(!c)return;
    if(key==='textColor')syncComposerSwatches(c);
    const palette=document.querySelector('#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette');
    if(palette){
      palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===c));
    }
    const stylePanel=document.getElementById('ethvelleTextStylePanel');
    if(stylePanel){stylePanel.querySelectorAll('.evts-swatch[data-color]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',norm(b.dataset.color)===c));}
  };

  /* Wrap, rather than replace, the existing setters so live application and project saving stay intact. */
  const oldComposerSet=window.setEthvelleComposerColor;
  if(typeof oldComposerSet==='function'&&!oldComposerSet.__ev43StableSelection){
    const wrapped=function(color){const out=oldComposerSet.apply(this,arguments);syncComposerSwatches(color);return out;};
    wrapped.__ev43StableSelection=true;window.setEthvelleComposerColor=wrapped;
  }
  const oldDockSet=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof oldDockSet==='function'&&!oldDockSet.__ev43StableSelection){
    const wrapped=function(key,color){const out=oldDockSet.apply(this,arguments);window.__ethvelleSyncTextSwatches(key,color);return out;};
    wrapped.__ev43StableSelection=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrapped;
  }
  const oldComposerOpen=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  if(typeof oldComposerOpen==='function'&&!oldComposerOpen.__ev43StableSelection){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=oldComposerOpen.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');
      const target=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;
      syncComposerSwatches(target?.textColor||input?.style.color||'#FFFFFF');
    });return out;};
    wrapped.__ev43StableSelection=true;window.openEthvelleTextComposer=wrapped;
  }

  /* Avoid moving focus to a colour button while the text field owns the Android keyboard.
     Preventing the pointer's default focus action prevents the keyboard close/reopen flash;
     the button's normal click/onClick handler still applies the colour. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const button=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest(
      '#ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch, #ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-swatch-action, #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch, #ethvelleTextStylePanel .evts-swatch'
    ):null;
    if(!button)return;
    const active=document.activeElement;
    if(active&&active.matches&&active.matches('textarea,input:not([type="range"]),[contenteditable="true"]')&&active.closest('#ethvelleTextComposer')){
      e.preventDefault();
    }
  },{capture:true,passive:false});

  /* Keep the composer ring correct if a palette rebuild recreates its swatch nodes. */
  const composer=document.getElementById('ethvelleTextComposer');
  if(composer&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const strip=composer.querySelector('#evtcColorStrip');
    if(strip&&!strip.dataset.ev43Observed){strip.dataset.ev43Observed='1';new MutationObserver(()=>{if(composerColor)syncComposerSwatches(composerColor);}).observe(strip,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','data-color']});}
  }

  /* Add Text behaves like timeline insertion: the first text clip begins at 0s. Later text clips
     insert before the nearest clip when the playhead is in its first half, or after it in the
     second half; following text clips move forward rather than overlapping. */
  window.ethvellePlanTextInsertStart=function(duration){
    const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);
    const len=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(duration)||5);
    const clips=list.filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text'&&Number.isFinite(Number(o.trimStart))&&Number.isFinite(Number(o.trimEnd))&&Number(o.trimEnd)>Number(o.trimStart))
      .sort((a,b)=>Number(a.trimStart)-Number(b.trimStart));
    if(!clips.length)return 0;
    const playhead=Math.max(0,Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec))||0);
    let anchor=clips.find(c=>playhead>=Number(c.trimStart)-.001&&playhead<Number(c.trimEnd)-.001);
    if(!anchor){
      let best=Infinity;
      clips.forEach(c=>{const st=Number(c.trimStart),en=Number(c.trimEnd),d=playhead<st?st-playhead:(playhead>en?playhead-en:0);if(d<best){best=d;anchor=c;}});
    }
    const st=Math.max(0,Number(anchor.trimStart)||0),en=Math.max(st+MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(anchor.trimEnd)||st+len);
    const insertAfter=playhead>=(st+en)/2;
    const insertAt=insertAfter?en:st;
    clips.forEach(c=>{
      if(Number(c.trimEnd)>insertAt+.0001){
        const oldStart=Number(c.trimStart)||0;
        const oldKeyframeTimes=Array.isArray(c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.map(k=>Number(k&&k.t)||0):[];
        c.trimStart=oldStart+len;c.trimEnd=Number(c.trimEnd)+len;
        shiftTimelineKeyframesOnce(c.ethvelleMediaKeyframes,oldKeyframeTimes,len);
      }
    });
    try{if(typeof timelineDuration!=='undefined')timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,insertAt+len,...clips.map(c=>Number(c.trimEnd)||0));}catch(_e){}
    return insertAt;
  };
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle v45: reliable text colour taps, stable swatch state, mobile recorder reliability,

<style id="ethvelle-v45-text-enhance-repair-css">

</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v45-text-enhance-repair-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const norm=v=>{const s=String(v||'').trim();return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(s)?s.toUpperCase():'';};
  const composerSelector='#ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch[data-color]';
  const dockSelector='#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch[data-color]';
  let currentComposerColor='#FFFFFF',currentDockColor='';
  let restoreComposerFocus=null;
  function setOnlyActive(root,selector,color){
    if(!root)return;const c=norm(color);if(!c)return;
    root.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(button=>button.classList.toggle('active',norm(button.dataset.color)===c));
  }
  function syncColorRings(){
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');
    if(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active'))setOnlyActive(composer,'.evtc-color-swatch[data-color]',currentComposerColor);
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock&&currentDockColor)setOnlyActive(dock,'.evpa-swatch[data-color]',currentDockColor);
  }
  function settleRings(color,scope){
    const c=norm(color);if(!c)return;
    if(scope==='composer')currentComposerColor=c;else if(scope==='dock')currentDockColor=c;
    syncColorRings();
    requestAnimationFrame(syncColorRings);
    setTimeout(syncColorRings,40);
  }

  // Wrap the existing setters (do not replace their model updates / redraw / save behaviour).
  const priorComposerSet=window.setEthvelleComposerColor;
  if(typeof priorComposerSet==='function'&&!priorComposerSet.__evV45Stable){
    const wrapped=function(color){const c=norm(color)||'#FFFFFF';const result=priorComposerSet.apply(this,arguments);try{const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');const t=typeof window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget==='function'?window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget():null;if(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active')&&t){t.textColor=c;if(Number(t.textOpacity)===0)t.textOpacity=100;}}catch(_e){}settleRings(c,'composer');return result;};
    wrapped.__evV45Stable=true;window.setEthvelleComposerColor=wrapped;
  }
  const priorDockSet=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof priorDockSet==='function'&&!priorDockSet.__evV45Stable){
    const wrapped=function(key,color){const c=norm(color)||'#FFFFFF';const result=priorDockSet.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='textColor')settleRings(c,'dock');return result;};
    wrapped.__evV45Stable=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrapped;
  }

  // The earlier focus guard prevented Android from focusing the palette button, but that could
  // also suppress its synthetic click. Apply the selected colour on pointerdown itself, then
  // preserve the live draft input's focus so there is no keyboard close/reopen cycle.
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const target=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest(composerSelector+', '+dockSelector):null;
    if(!target)return;
    const isComposerSwatch=!!target.matches(composerSelector),color=norm(target.dataset.color);if(!color)return;
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer'),input=$('evtcStageText');
    const keepKeyboard=!!(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active')&&input&&document.activeElement===input);
    if(keepKeyboard){restoreComposerFocus=input;try{input.__evV45SelectionStart=input.selectionStart;input.__evV45SelectionEnd=input.selectionEnd;}catch(_e){}}
    // Apply now as a user gesture. The normal click may also fire; applying the same colour twice
    // is harmless, while this guarantees the colour is not lost if a WebView cancels that click.
    try{
      if(isComposerSwatch&&typeof window.setEthvelleComposerColor==='function')window.setEthvelleComposerColor(color);
      else if(typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetColor==='function'){
        /* Stroke / Shadow / Background tabs own their colour: never write the tapped swatch to the text colour. */
        const tab=$('evpaTabs')?.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';
        const key=tab==='stroke'?'strokeColor':tab==='shadow'?'shadowColor':(tab==='highlight'||tab==='background')?'textBackgroundColor':'textColor';
        window.ethvellePicsartSetColor(key,color);
      }
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle text swatch:',err);}
    settleRings(color,isComposerSwatch?'composer':'dock');
    if(keepKeyboard){e.preventDefault();}
  },{capture:true,passive:false});

  function restoreFocus(){
    const input=restoreComposerFocus;restoreComposerFocus=null;
    if(!input)return;
    const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer');if(!input.isConnected||!composer||!composer.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const start=input.__evV45SelectionStart,end=input.__evV45SelectionEnd;
    try{if(document.activeElement!==input)input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){try{input.focus();}catch(__e){}}
    try{if(Number.isInteger(start)&&Number.isInteger(end))input.setSelectionRange(start,end);}catch(_e){}
    syncColorRings();
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>{setTimeout(restoreFocus,0);},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{setTimeout(restoreFocus,0);},true);

  // Keep rings correct after palettes are recreated without letting an old white default overwrite
  // the colour just selected in the active composer.
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const swatch=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest(composerSelector+', '+dockSelector):null;if(!swatch)return;
    const color=norm(swatch.dataset.color);if(color)settleRings(color,swatch.matches(composerSelector)?'composer':'dock');
  },true);
  const enhancePanel=$('toolPanel');
  if(enhancePanel&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    new MutationObserver(()=>{
      const enhancing=enhancePanel.classList.contains('evlt-enhance-compact')&&getComputedStyle(enhancePanel).display!=='none';
      document.body.classList.toggle('evlt-enhance-preview-active',enhancing);
    }).observe(enhancePanel,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});
  }

  // Also toggle the preview mode synchronously at the panel API boundary, so the selection frame
  // disappears before the enhanced compare overlay is shown and returns when Enhance closes.
  const priorOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof priorOpenPanel==='function'&&!priorOpenPanel.__evV45EnhanceChrome){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      document.body.classList.toggle('evlt-enhance-preview-active',key==='evltEnhance');
      const result=priorOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key!=='evltEnhance')document.body.classList.remove('evlt-enhance-preview-active');
      else requestAnimationFrame(()=>document.body.classList.add('evlt-enhance-preview-active'));
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV45EnhanceChrome=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  const priorClosePanel=window.closePanel;
  if(typeof priorClosePanel==='function'&&!priorClosePanel.__evV45EnhanceChrome){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=priorClosePanel.apply(this,arguments);document.body.classList.remove('evlt-enhance-preview-active');return result;};
    wrapped.__evV45EnhanceChrome=true;window.closePanel=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v46-targeted-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const safe=(fn)=>{try{return fn();}catch(_e){return undefined;}};

  // Opening the custom picker from Add Text deliberately dismisses the Android keyboard.
  const openPicker=window.openEthvelleColorPicker;
  if(typeof openPicker==='function'&&!openPicker.__evV46BlurKeyboard){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const composer=$('ethvelleTextComposer'),input=$('evtcStageText');
      if(composer&&composer.classList.contains('active')&&input){
        try{input.blur();}catch(_e){}
      }
      return openPicker.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evV46BlurKeyboard=true;window.openEthvelleColorPicker=wrapped;
  }

  function qualityFor(model){
    if(!model)return '';
    const raw=model.__evltPendingEnhancedQuality||model.__evltEnhanceChosenQuality||model.enhancedAssetQuality;
    const runtime=String(model.__evltRuntimeEnhancedQuality||'').toUpperCase();
    if(runtime.includes('ULTRA'))return 'ULTRA HD';
    if(runtime.includes('QHD')||runtime.includes('1440'))return 'QHD';
    if(runtime.includes('FULL HD')||runtime==='HD')return 'FULL HD';
    const q=Number(raw)||0;
    return q>=2160?'ULTRA HD':q>=1440?'QHD':q>=1080?'FULL HD':'';
  }
  function placeBadge(el,label){
    if(!el)return;
    let badge=el.querySelector(':scope > .evlt-quality-row-badge');
    if(!label){if(badge)badge.remove();return;}
    if(!badge){badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className='evlt-quality-row-badge';badge.setAttribute('aria-label','Enhanced output quality');el.appendChild(badge);}
    if(badge.textContent!==label)badge.textContent=label;
  }
  function refreshQualityBadges(){
    let overlayList=[],backgroundList=[];
    try{overlayList=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);}catch(_e){}
    try{backgroundList=Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:(Array.isArray(window.backgrounds)?window.backgrounds:[]);}catch(_e){}
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').forEach(el=>{
      const model=overlayList.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(el.dataset.overlayId));placeBadge(el,qualityFor(model));
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .track-clip[data-bg-id]').forEach(el=>{
      const model=backgroundList.find(o=>String(o.id)===String(el.dataset.bgId));placeBadge(el,qualityFor(model));
    });
  }
  window.__ethvelleV46RefreshQualityBadges=refreshQualityBadges;
  const timeline=$('timelineInner');
  if(timeline&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    let queued=false;new MutationObserver(()=>{if(queued)return;queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;refreshQualityBadges();});})
      .observe(timeline,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',refreshQualityBadges,{once:true});
  else refreshQualityBadges();

  // After Apply, force the actual timeline/canvas renderer to bind the runtime enhanced source.
  const priorApply=window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof priorApply==='function'&&!priorApply.__evV46CanvasRefresh){
    const wrappedApply=async function(){
      const result=await priorApply.apply(this,arguments);
      [50,180,420].forEach(delay=>setTimeout(()=>{
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.renderOverlayLayers==='function')window.renderOverlayLayers();});
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead==='function')window.refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead();});
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.applyBackground==='function')window.applyBackground();});
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();});
        safe(()=>{if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();});
        safe(refreshQualityBadges);
      },delay));
      return result;
    };
    wrappedApply.__evV46CanvasRefresh=true;window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance=wrappedApply;
  }
  const priorStart=window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof priorStart==='function'&&!priorStart.__evV46QualityRefresh){
    const wrappedStart=async function(){const result=await priorStart.apply(this,arguments);[80,260].forEach(delay=>setTimeout(refreshQualityBadges,delay));return result;};
    wrappedStart.__evV46QualityRefresh=true;window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance=wrappedStart;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v47-final-repair-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const normColor=value=>{
    let c=String(value||'').trim();
    if(/^#[0-9a-f]{3}$/i.test(c))c='#'+c.slice(1).split('').map(ch=>ch+ch).join('');
    return /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c)?c.toUpperCase():'';
  };
  const activeComposer=()=>!!$('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active');
  function setVisibleColorRings(color){
    const c=normColor(color);if(!c)return;
    const specs=[
      ['#ethvelleTextComposer #evtcColorStrip .evtc-color-swatch[data-color]', 'data-color'],
      ['#ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatch[data-color]', 'data-color'],
      ['#ethvelleTextStylePanel .evts-swatch[data-color]', 'data-color']
    ];
    specs.forEach(([selector,attr])=>document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',normColor(el.getAttribute(attr))===c)));
  }

  /* The composer has two legacy style objects (draft + dock target). Sync both so a colour picked
     in the Font row is what the check-mark saves, not just what the preview temporarily paints. */
  const priorComposerSet=window.setEthvelleComposerColor;
  if(typeof priorComposerSet==='function'&&!priorComposerSet.__evV47ApplyColor){
    const wrapped=function(color){
      const c=normColor(color);const result=priorComposerSet.apply(this,arguments);
      if(c){
        try{const target=window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.();if(target){target.textColor=c;target.textGradient='';target.textTexture='';if(Number(target.textOpacity)===0)target.textOpacity=100;}}catch(_e){}
        const input=$('evtcStageText');if(input){input.style.setProperty('color',c,'important');input.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color',c,'important');input.style.setProperty('background-image','none','important');input.style.setProperty('background-clip','border-box','important');input.style.setProperty('-webkit-background-clip','border-box','important');}
        setVisibleColorRings(c);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47ApplyColor=true;window.setEthvelleComposerColor=wrapped;
  }

  /* Make existing-text swatches immediately update the model, canvas renderer, and persistent save.
     In composer mode explicitly update the actual draft too; this is the path that legacy dock
     handlers could miss when a color was chosen from the Font section. */
  const priorDockSet=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof priorDockSet==='function'&&!priorDockSet.__evV47ApplyColor){
    const wrapped=function(key,color){
      const c=normColor(color);const composing=activeComposer();
      const result=priorDockSet.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='textColor'&&c){
        if(composing&&typeof window.setEthvelleComposerColor==='function'){
          try{window.setEthvelleComposerColor(c);}catch(_e){}
        }else{
          try{
            const target=window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.();
            if(target&&target.type==='text'){
              target.textColor=c;target.textGradient='';target.textTexture='';
              if(Number(target.textOpacity)===0)target.textOpacity=100;
            }
            if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();
            if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
          }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle text color apply:',err);}
        }
        setVisibleColorRings(c);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47ApplyColor=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrapped;
  }

  /* Re-sync the selected swatch on composer open/rebuild, using the real style target as source. */
  const priorComposerOpen=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  if(typeof priorComposerOpen==='function'&&!priorComposerOpen.__evV47SyncRings){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=priorComposerOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        try{
          const target=window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget?.();
          const c=normColor(target?.textColor||'#FFFFFF')||'#FFFFFF';
          if(typeof window.setEthvelleComposerColor==='function')window.setEthvelleComposerColor(c);
          else setVisibleColorRings(c);
        }catch(_e){}
      });
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47SyncRings=true;window.openEthvelleTextComposer=wrapped;
  }

  // Successful video enhancement now starts the main timeline player once. The short-lived
  // pause guard remains for unsuccessful runs and Apply's media-rebinding refreshes.
  function freezeEnhanceTargetVideo(forceClockPause){
    if(forceClockPause){
      try{
        if(typeof window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime==='function')window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();
        else if(typeof window.pausePlayback==='function')window.pausePlayback();
      }catch(_e){}
    }
    try{
      const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
      const raw=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
      const video=raw&&raw.tagName==='VIDEO'?raw:(raw&&raw.querySelector?raw.querySelector('video'):null);
      if(!video)return;
      video.autoplay=false;video.removeAttribute('autoplay');
      const pausedPlayGuard=()=>{
        if(!(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)){
          try{video.pause();}catch(_e){}
        }
      };
      try{video.pause();}catch(_e){}
      if(!video.__evV47EnhancePauseGuard){
        video.__evV47EnhancePauseGuard=pausedPlayGuard;
        video.addEventListener('play',pausedPlayGuard);
        setTimeout(()=>{
          try{video.removeEventListener('play',pausedPlayGuard);delete video.__evV47EnhancePauseGuard;}catch(_e){}
        },2200);
      }
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  let enhanceAutoPlayToken=0,enhanceSweepRaf=0;
  let enhanceAutoPreviewPending=false,enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=false,enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=0,enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer=0,enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=null;
  window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;
  function enhanceSelectedClip(){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:'');
    return typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===id):null;
  }
  function cancelEnhanceAutoPreview(reason,pauseIfRunning,renderAfter){
    const active=enhanceAutoPreviewPending||enhanceAutoPreviewRunning||window.__evEnhancePreviewMute===true;
    if(!active)return false;
    const wasRunning=enhanceAutoPreviewRunning;
    enhanceAutoPlayToken++;
    enhanceAutoPreviewPending=false;enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=false;enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=null;
    if(enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer){clearTimeout(enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer);enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer=0;}
    if(enhanceAutoPreviewRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(enhanceAutoPreviewRaf);enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=0;}
    window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;
    if(wasRunning&&pauseIfRunning&&typeof window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime==='function'&&typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying){try{window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();}catch(_e){}}
    else if(renderAfter!==false&&wasRunning){try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}}
    return true;
  }
  window.__evCancelEnhanceAutoPreview=cancelEnhanceAutoPreview;
  /* Every timeline scrub entry point writes this flag, including the compact zoom-HUD scrubber. */
  try{
    const oldScrubDescriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window,'__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing');
    if(!oldScrubDescriptor||oldScrubDescriptor.configurable){
      let scrubState=!!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing;
      Object.defineProperty(window,'__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing',{configurable:true,enumerable:true,get(){return scrubState;},set(value){const next=!!value;if(next&&!scrubState)cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('scrub',false,true);scrubState=next;}});
    }
  }catch(_e){}
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('pagehide',true,false),{once:true});
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('visibility-hidden',true,false);});
  function cancelEnhanceSweep(){if(enhanceSweepRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(enhanceSweepRaf);enhanceSweepRaf=0;}}
  window.__evSweepCancel=cancelEnhanceSweep;
  /* After Enhance finishes the BEFORE/AFTER divider glides 50% -> 0% -> 100% -> 50% so the whole
     enhanced frame is seen on both sides. Touching the divider cancels it at once. */
  function sweepEnhanceCompare(totalSec){
    cancelEnhanceSweep();
    const set=window.ethvelleSetCompareSplit;if(typeof set!=='function')return;
    const period=Math.min(6,Math.max(3,totalSec)),total=Math.max(period,totalSec),t0=performance.now();
    const step=now=>{
      if(!document.getElementById('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot')){enhanceSweepRaf=0;return;}
      const t=(now-t0)/1000;
      if(t>=total){set(50);enhanceSweepRaf=0;return;}
      // phase 0..1 inside the current period; sine keeps motion smooth at both ends.
      const phase=(t%period)/period;
      set(50-50*Math.sin(phase*2*Math.PI));
      enhanceSweepRaf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
    };
    enhanceSweepRaf=requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }
  function playEnhancedVideoOnce(lockedId){
    // The clip that was enhanced is locked in when Enhance starts, so a neighbouring clip that happens to sit
    // under the playhead can never become the selected / previewed one.
    const clip=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(lockedId)):null;
    if(!clip||clip.type!=='video'){window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;return;}
    const token=++enhanceAutoPlayToken,keepId=clip.id;
    enhanceAutoPreviewPending=true;enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=false;enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=keepId;
    // startPlayback()/pause deselect or re-select by playhead position. Put the enhanced clip back directly
    // (same pattern as the audio preview) so playback keeps running and the Enhance panel keeps its target.
    const keepSelected=()=>{
      try{
        if(String(selectedClip)===String(keepId))return;
        selectedClip=keepId;selectedKfIdx=null;
        document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip.selected,#timelineInner .track-clip.selected').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('selected'));
        const el=[...document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]')].find(n=>String(n.getAttribute('data-overlay-id'))===String(keepId));if(el)el.classList.add('selected');
      }catch(_e){}
    };
    const start=Number(clip.trimStart)||0,end=Number(clip.trimEnd)||start;
    if(!(end>start)){enhanceAutoPreviewPending=false;enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=null;window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;return;}
    const stopAt=start+0.9*(end-start);
    enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      enhanceAutoPreviewStartTimer=0;
      if(token!==enhanceAutoPlayToken)return;
      enhanceAutoPreviewPending=false;
      try{
        if(String(selectedClip)!==String(keepId))keepSelected();
        if(typeof window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime==='function'&&typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();
        if(typeof window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds==='function')window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(start);
        if(typeof window.startPlayback!=='function'){window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;enhanceAutoPreviewClipId=null;return;}
        window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=true;
        enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=true;
        window.startPlayback();
        keepSelected();
        // startPlayback() can be gated ("Preparing playback…") and start a moment later, so wait for it before treating "not playing" as stopped.
        const startWaitFrom=performance.now();let sawPlaying=!!(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying);
        // Preview once, stopping at 90% of the clip without moving the playhead afterward.
        const finish=()=>{
          if(token!==enhanceAutoPlayToken)return;
          enhanceAutoPreviewRunning=false;
          try{window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();}catch(_e){}
          window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;
          try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
          keepSelected();[60,250,700].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>{if(token===enhanceAutoPlayToken)keepSelected();},ms));
        };
        const watch=()=>{
          enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=0;
          if(token!==enhanceAutoPlayToken)return;
          if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)sawPlaying=true;
          if(typeof isPlaying==='undefined'||!isPlaying){
            if(!sawPlaying&&performance.now()-startWaitFrom<12000){enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(watch);return;}
            cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('playback-stopped',false,true);return;
          }
          keepSelected();
          const now=Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
          if(now>=stopAt-0.04){finish();return;}
          enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(watch);
        };
        enhanceAutoPreviewRaf=requestAnimationFrame(watch);
      }catch(err){
        console.warn('Ethvelle: automatic one-time Enhance preview playback failed',err);
        cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('preview-error',true,true);
        window.__evEnhancePreviewMute=false;
      }
    },150);
  }
  const priorStartEnhance=window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof priorStartEnhance==='function'&&!priorStartEnhance.__evV47PauseAfterRun){
    const wrappedStart=async function(){
      cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('new-enhance',true,true);
      let readyForPlayback=false;
      const lockedId=typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:null;
      try{
        const result=await priorStartEnhance.apply(this,arguments);
        const clip=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(lockedId)):null;
        const status=$('evltEnhanceStatus');
        readyForPlayback=!!(clip&&clip.type==='video'&&status&&/Ready to apply/i.test(status.textContent||''));
        return result;
      }finally{
        if(readyForPlayback)playEnhancedVideoOnce(lockedId);
        else freezeEnhanceTargetVideo(true);
      }
    };
    wrappedStart.__evV47PauseAfterRun=true;window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance=wrappedStart;
  }
  const priorEnhanceTogglePlayback=window.togglePlayback;
  if(typeof priorEnhanceTogglePlayback==='function'&&!priorEnhanceTogglePlayback.__evEnhancePreviewCancel){
    const wrappedToggle=function(){cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('transport-toggle',false,false);return priorEnhanceTogglePlayback.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedToggle.__evEnhancePreviewCancel=true;window.togglePlayback=wrappedToggle;
  }
  const priorEnhanceTogglePlay=window.togglePlay;
  if(typeof priorEnhanceTogglePlay==='function'&&!priorEnhanceTogglePlay.__evEnhancePreviewCancel){
    const wrappedTogglePlay=function(){cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('transport-toggle',false,false);return priorEnhanceTogglePlay.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedTogglePlay.__evEnhancePreviewCancel=true;window.togglePlay=wrappedTogglePlay;
  }
  const priorEnhanceSelectClip=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof priorEnhanceSelectClip==='function'&&!priorEnhanceSelectClip.__evEnhancePreviewCancel){
    const wrappedSelectClip=function(which){if((enhanceAutoPreviewPending||enhanceAutoPreviewRunning)&&String(which)!==String(enhanceAutoPreviewClipId))cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('clip-selection',false,false);return priorEnhanceSelectClip.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedSelectClip.__evEnhancePreviewCancel=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrappedSelectClip;
  }

  /* Successful Apply closes only after the runtime enhanced source has actually been installed.
     Errors keep the panel open so the user can read the failure and retry. */
  const priorApply=window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance;
  if(typeof priorApply==='function'&&!priorApply.__evV47CloseOnSuccess){
    const wrapped=async function(){
      const result=await priorApply.apply(this,arguments);
      const status=$('evltEnhanceStatus');
      if(status&&/\bApplied\b/.test(status.textContent||'')){
        document.body.classList.remove('evlt-enhance-preview-active');
        cancelEnhanceAutoPreview('apply-close',true,true);
        if(typeof window.closePanel==='function')window.closePanel();
        return result;
      }
      freezeEnhanceTargetVideo(true);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47CloseOnSuccess=true;window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance=wrapped;
  }

  /* Update the in-memory saved playhead as it moves, so pagehide/device-save catches the latest
     position even when no other edit happened just before refresh. */
  const priorMovePlayhead=window.movePlayhead;
  if(typeof priorMovePlayhead==='function'&&!priorMovePlayhead.__evV47SavePlayhead){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=priorMovePlayhead.apply(this,arguments);
      try{if(typeof currentProject!=='undefined'&&currentProject&&currentProject.savedState){
        const t=Number(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        currentProject.savedState.playheadTimeSec=Math.max(0,t);
      }}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV47SavePlayhead=true;window.movePlayhead=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setVisibleColorRings('#FFFFFF'),{once:true});
})();
</script>
